# OutRunners (MAME set 'orunners', World) - V70 program symbols
#
# Addresses and names only: no game data lives in this file.
# Format:  ADDRESS  KIND  NAME  [LAYOUT]   [# comment]
#   KIND: func = function entry, code = code label, data = data label
#   A code label of a computed call or jump may say where it goes: calls=TABLE, the code of a described table
#   register RN NAME: a register the whole program shares, a global variable of the C (its name)
#   exit FUNCTION ADDRESS: the function (a func line in the middle of code) goes back to the code that runs
#   it where it jumps to ADDRESS: that jump is its return, and a jump into it a call, then a jump there
# Addresses use the copy of the program the code refers to:
#   000000-03FFFF (ic37 + ic40, a 16-bit word of each in every 32-bit word), 100000-1FFFFF (ic36 + ic39)
# Mirrors are accepted and folded (e.g. FFFFF0 -> 03FFF0).
#
# LAYOUT, for data tables that hold code addresses the analysis cannot see the code load:
#   record=N        the table is an array of N-byte records
#   count=N         ... of N records, or
#   count=prefix    ... of as many records as the 16-bit word before them says, or
#   count=prefix32  ... of as many records as the 32-bit word before them says, or
#   count=bytes32   ... of as many records as fill the length in bytes the 32-bit word before them says, or
#   end=XXXX        ... closed by this 16-bit word where the next record would start
#   kinds=O:K=N,..  ... of records whose length the 16-bit field at offset O picks: N bytes for kind K
#                   (hexadecimal), 'end' for a kind that closes the array, or 'fork' for one that closes it
#                   with a table of addresses of arrays that go on from there (as many as fit before the
#                   first of them)
#   code=A,B        every record holds a 32-bit code address at these offsets (0 = no code);
#                   A:BASE is a 16-bit offset from BASE instead
#   data=A,B        every record holds at these offsets a 32-bit value that its code may take as the address
#                   of data: the data it goes with, when the value's low 24 bits (the V70's address bus) are
#                   an address of the program ROM that is not code (0 and numbers are not)
#   lists=N         the table is N 16-bit offsets, from its own address, to such arrays
#   pointers=N      the table is N 32-bit addresses of such arrays (0: no array)
# Sizes, offsets and counts are decimal; end and BASE are hexadecimal, like the data and addresses.

# ======== entry points ========
03FFF0  func  reset              # V70 reset vector (0xFFFFFFF0 on a 24-bit bus): "jmp 6"
comment reset The V70's reset vector, at the top of the program's address space: a jump to boot.
000006  func  boot               # sets SBR to 03F000, the stack to 21FC00, and copies blocks to E00000 and E80000
03F000  data  vector_table       # 256 x 32-bit entries, base loaded into SBR by boot
03F8B4  func  int_default        # target of 250 of the 256 vector table entries
03F8C0  func  int_vector_02      # NMI
# The V70 takes maskable interrupts at vector 0x40 + the number the interrupt controller (D00000) supplies
03F500  func  int_vector_40
03F794  func  int_vector_41
03F7A0  func  int_vector_42
03F7AC  func  int_vector_43
03F830  func  int_vector_44

# ======== the registers the program shares ========
# Globals of the C (decompiler/signatures.py CONTEXT); each task has its own copy, which the runtime swaps
# with it. R12 and R14 are shared too, as scratch registers whose last value the next function may read
# (a handler what the one before it left: race_cars_start the page in R12's high half, the road rows' and
# the scenery's drawing the height of the last size step in R14), and keep their register names. R26, R27
# and R28 are not: every function sets them before it reads them (tools/game_profile.py)
register R20  current_object     # the object the object loop (011CC0) runs: set before it calls the handler at field 4
register R23  player_car         # the screen's car: the object at 1000[R25] (209000 or 219000)
register R25  screen             # the block of the screen the task runs: 208000 for screen 1 (the second task), 218000 for screen 2 (the boot task)
register R32  stack_pointer      # SP, in the functions whose stack stays in memory (decompiler/stack.py); 21F400 after boot

# ======== structures ========
# The game's own (decompiler/pointers.py works out which registers point at them): the objects, 124 in the
# page of each screen's block, run by the object loop; the screen's car block, at 1000 of its block (its
# field 0 is the car's object, which player_car_update runs); the block of the screen a task runs (R25), 6000
# bytes: after screen 1's come the game's variables (20E000), after screen 2's the race records the ISDN link
# brings in (21E000) and the stacks.
# Times are in the task's frames: each task runs every other video frame, 30 a second
struct object 100
struct car 200
struct screen 6000
# Each player's race record, two copies of 0x80 bytes in the game's variables: 20FB00 and 20FB80 (the number
# of stages at 60, the stage at 62, the time of the last checkpoint pass at 68, the time left at 6A in 30ths
# of a second, the race time at 6C in thousandths), and the copies of every player's from 20FC00, 0x80 apart,
# that the screens read (eight with the link: link_receive_records brings in the other cabinets')
struct race 80
# Below each screen's block (screen - 8000), its objects' area: this frame's list of objects and the next
# frame's (each a count, then the low halves of the objects' addresses; the two, at 200 and 300, swap every
# frame), the used slots (7C bytes at 10, FF used), a spare object at 100 and the 124 objects from 400
struct object_area 8000
struct object_list 100
struct object_list_block 8   # an object list as run_objects clears it, eight bytes at a time (its first halfword the count)
field object_list_block 00 low u32
field object_list_block 00 count u16   # the list's count of objects, read before it is cleared
field object_list_block 04 high u32
field object_area 00 list u32 object_list            # this frame's list, which run_objects walks
field object_area 04 list_first u32 object_list      # its first object's place (nothing reads it)
field object_area 08 next_list u32 object_list       # the next frame's list, where run_objects puts the live ones
field object_area 0C list_end u32 object_list        # past this frame's list's last object (nothing reads it)
# The structures inside a screen's block and an objects' area (member lines): the screen's ring of eight views
# (player_car_publish), its car (player_car), the course's ring of 128 road segments, the road's segments as the
# screen shows them, the 416 depth layers' lists of sprite groups; the area's spare object, this frame's and the
# next frame's object lists, and its 124 objects
member screen 600 views view[8]
member screen 1000 car car
member screen 2000 road_segments road_segment[80]
member screen 3000 projected_segments projected_segment[80]
member screen 5000 depth_layers depth_list[1A0]
member object_area 100 spare_object object
member object_area 200 object_lists object_list[2]
member object_area 400 objects object[7C]
# ======== records of the program ROM ========
# A row of the same sprite along the road (scenery, fork signs, flashing and animated scenery: road_row_start
# 012894 reads it from object 18): its animation, palette, flags, the size table and shift, the side of the
# road, the offsets, how far apart the sprites are and how many, then the hit box (none: 0 in its first 32 bits;
# the records follow one another 18 bytes apart: 723 of the 770 the streams start, 212 of them with a hit box)
struct road_row_record 18 rom
field road_row_record 00 animation u16          # 01289B "movz.hw [R10+], R9" (012B13 in road_row_start_copy): animation_start's
field road_row_record 02 palette u16            # 01289E -> object 3A
field road_row_record 04 flags u8               # 0128A3: or-ed into object 00
field road_row_record 05 size_table u8          # -> object 45
field road_row_record 06 scale_shift u8         # -> object 44
field road_row_record 07 side s8                # -> object 3F
field road_row_record 08 x_offset s16           # -> object 20
field road_row_record 0A y_offset s16           # -> object 24
field road_row_record 0C spacing u16            # -> object 10
field road_row_record 0E count u16              # -> object 12
field road_row_record 10 hit_box_along u32          # the box's two bounds along the road (item_hit_box along_start, along_end), which the code reads as one word: 0128CA, 012B42 "test.w [R10]": none when its first 32 bits are 0; its address -> object 60, where road_row_hit_check reads it (two bounds along the road, two across: 020EBF 30 -30 50 250, decimal)
field road_row_record 14 hit_box_across u32         # the box's two bounds across the road (item_hit_box across_start, across_end), after hit_box_along: the hit checks read them through object 60
# The objects of a road's stream (road_object_streams), each a (position, command) halfword pair; command 0
# goes on with the object's handler and its parameter (12 bytes in all; 1 and 2 are 4, 3 a fork: the table of
# its branches' streams follows): road_object_stream_read 01273D "movz.hw [R10+], R0" (compared with car
# stage_segments, cmp.h: signed) and 012733 "movz.hw [R10+], R1" (the command, by which 012736 picks the code
# from road_object_stream_command_jumps), road_object_stream_start_object 012767 and 01276C
struct road_object_stream_entry 0C rom
field road_object_stream_entry 00 position s16       # 01273D: segments from the stage's start (+ stage_start -> object 1C)
field road_object_stream_entry 02 command u16        # 012733: 0 start an object, 1 the stage's end, 2 the end, 3 a fork
field road_object_stream_entry 04 handler u32        # 012767 "mov.w [R10+], 4[R21]": the new object's handler
field road_object_stream_entry 08 parameter u32      # 01276C "mov.w [R10+], 18[R21]": its field 18 (most often its record)
elements road_object_stream_forks u32 road_object_stream_entry   # a fork's table: the streams of its two branches, by the branch the car takes (road_object_stream_read, state 3)
# A flashing_scenery object's record (flashing_scenery_update 012923-012956, from field 18): road_row_record's
# fields, but with the address of the list of palettes the scenery cycles through (flashing_scenery_palettes)
# in place of the palette; then the hit box (none: all 11 records in the streams, 021027-02D04C, have 0)
struct flashing_scenery_record 1A rom
field flashing_scenery_record 00 animation u16          # 012927 "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's
field flashing_scenery_record 02 palettes u32 flashing_scenery_palettes   # 01292A -> object 18 (palette_cycle)
field flashing_scenery_record 06 flags u8               # 01292F: or-ed into object 00 (0 in all 11)
field flashing_scenery_record 07 size_table u8          # 012933 -> object 45 (0, 1)
field flashing_scenery_record 08 scale_shift u8         # 012938 -> object 44
field flashing_scenery_record 09 side s8                # 01293D -> object 3F (-1, 0, 1)
field flashing_scenery_record 0A x_offset s16           # 012942 -> object 20
field flashing_scenery_record 0C y_offset s16           # 012947 -> object 24
field flashing_scenery_record 0E spacing u16            # 01294C -> object 10
field flashing_scenery_record 10 count u16              # 012951 -> object 12
field flashing_scenery_record 12 hit_box_along u32          # the box's two bounds along the road (item_hit_box along_start, along_end), which the code reads as one word: 012956 "test.w [R10]": none when its first 32 bits are 0; its address -> object 60
field flashing_scenery_record 16 hit_box_across u32         # the box's two bounds across the road (item_hit_box across_start, across_end), after hit_box_along: the hit checks read them through object 60
# The palettes a flashing_scenery cycles through, one a frame (flashing_scenery_update 012990 "movs.hw [R10+],
# R0"): a negative entry goes back that many bytes (012997 "add.w R0, R10") to the list's start (-8 after
# three, -18 after eight, decimal: 021041, 022FF2, 023E56, 024C16, 0287AA, 02C444)
elements flashing_scenery_palettes s16
# The record of the northern lights (aurora_update 012A3B-012A4D, from field 18; two, 02C44E and 02C470)
struct aurora_record 08 rom
field aurora_record 00 animations u32 aurora_animations   # 012A3F "mov.w [R10+], 18[R20]" (animation_list)
field aurora_record 04 palette u16              # 012A44 -> object 3A (25C)
field aurora_record 06 flags u8                 # 012A49: or-ed into object 00 (0)
field aurora_record 07 animation_frames u8      # 012A4D "mov.b [R10], 9[R20]": how long each animation shows (28: 40 frames)
# The northern lights' animations, one after another (aurora_next_animation 012AAF "movs.hw [R10+], R0"): a
# negative entry goes back that many bytes (012AB6) to the list's start (-18 decimal after eight: 02C45E, 02C481)
elements aurora_animations s16
# What crosses the road when the car comes near (road_crosser_update 012BCF-012C0E, from field 18: the camels,
# 12 records 02737C-02D8A8), and the kangaroos (kangaroo_update 012CCB-012CFC reads its record, 02AB8C, the
# same way: hop_animation for crossing_animation)
struct road_crosser_record 10 rom
field road_crosser_record 00 animation u16            # 012BD3 "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's
field road_crosser_record 02 crossing_animation u16   # 012BE4 -> object 80, once the car reaches trigger_position
field road_crosser_record 04 palette u16              # 012BEA -> object 3A
field road_crosser_record 06 flags u8                 # 012BEF: or-ed into object 00 (0 or 40)
field road_crosser_record 07 side s8                  # 012BF3 -> object 3F (0 or 1)
field road_crosser_record 08 x_offset s16             # 012BF8 -> object 20
field road_crosser_record 0A trigger_distance u16     # 012BFD -> object 82
field road_crosser_record 0C x_speed s16              # 012C03 -> object 84 (-15 to -22 decimal; 0 for animation 165)
field road_crosser_record 0E draw_mode u8             # 012C09 -> object 3E
field road_crosser_record 0F scale_shift u8           # 012C0E -> object 44
# The record of scenery that travels across the view (moving_scenery_update 012DD3-012E16, from field 18; 18
# records, 021091-029248)
struct moving_scenery_record 14 rom
field moving_scenery_record 00 animation u16          # 012DD7 "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's
field moving_scenery_record 02 palette u16            # 012DDA -> object 3A
field moving_scenery_record 04 flags u8               # 012DDF: or-ed into object 00 (0 or 40)
field moving_scenery_record 05 draw_mode u8           # 012DE3 -> object 3E (9)
field moving_scenery_record 06 scale_shift u8         # 012DE8 -> object 44
field moving_scenery_record 07 side s8                # 012DED -> object 3F (0)
field moving_scenery_record 08 x_offset s16           # 012DF2 -> object 20
field moving_scenery_record 0A y_offset s16           # 012DF7 -> object 24
field moving_scenery_record 0C trigger_distance u16   # 012DFC -> object 80 (plus 16)
field moving_scenery_record 0E x_speed s16            # 012E0A -> object 82
field moving_scenery_record 10 y_speed s16            # 012E10 -> object 84
field moving_scenery_record 12 road_speed s16         # 012E16 -> object 86
# The record of the helicopter of stage 4 (swooping_helicopter_update 012EC3-012F12, from field 18; one,
# 02304C): moving_scenery_record's fields, then how long it dives and how fast
struct swooping_helicopter_record 17 rom
field swooping_helicopter_record 00 animation u16          # 012EC7 "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's
field swooping_helicopter_record 02 palette u16            # 012ECA -> object 3A
field swooping_helicopter_record 04 flags u8               # 012ECF: or-ed into object 00
field swooping_helicopter_record 05 draw_mode u8           # 012ED3 -> object 3E
field swooping_helicopter_record 06 scale_shift u8         # 012ED8 -> object 44
field swooping_helicopter_record 07 side s8                # 012EDD -> object 3F
field swooping_helicopter_record 08 x_offset s16           # 012EE2 -> object 20
field swooping_helicopter_record 0A y_offset s16           # 012EE7 -> object 24
field swooping_helicopter_record 0C trigger_distance u16   # 012EEC -> object 80 (plus 16)
field swooping_helicopter_record 0E x_speed s16            # 012EFA -> object 82
field swooping_helicopter_record 10 y_speed s16            # 012F00 -> object 84
field swooping_helicopter_record 12 road_speed s16         # 012F06 -> object 86
field swooping_helicopter_record 14 dive_frames u8         # 012F0C -> object 88 (1E: 30 frames)
field swooping_helicopter_record 15 dive_y_speed s16       # 012F12 -> object 8A (40 decimal)
# The record of the START banner and its posts (start_gate_update 012FDB-01300E, from field 18; four,
# 020D3F-020D87, 18 apart): as road_row_record, with the draw mode where the size table is; then the hit box
# (none: 0 in all four)
struct start_gate_record 18 rom
field start_gate_record 00 animation u16          # 012FDF "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's
field start_gate_record 02 palette u16            # 012FE2 -> object 3A (C9)
field start_gate_record 04 flags u8               # 012FE7: or-ed into object 00
field start_gate_record 05 draw_mode u8           # 012FEB -> object 3E (9)
field start_gate_record 06 scale_shift u8         # 012FF0 -> object 44
field start_gate_record 07 side s8                # 012FF5 -> object 3F (-1, 0, 1)
field start_gate_record 08 x_offset s16           # 012FFA -> object 20
field start_gate_record 0A y_offset s16           # 012FFF -> object 24
field start_gate_record 0C spacing u16            # 013004 -> object 10
field start_gate_record 0E count u16              # 013009 -> object 12
field start_gate_record 10 hit_box_along u32          # the box's two bounds along the road (item_hit_box along_start, along_end), which the code reads as one word: 01300E "test.w [R10]": none when its first 32 bits are 0; its address -> object 60
field start_gate_record 14 hit_box_across u32         # the box's two bounds across the road (item_hit_box across_start, across_end), after hit_box_along: the hit checks read them through object 60
# The record of a man with a chequered flag: the one at the start (start_flagman_update 01309B-0130B7, 020D14)
# and the ones at the forks and the goal (flagman_update 01311C-013138, 02DA44 and 02DC07): the standing and
# the waving animations (6E, 6F), the palette (7B), the side and the offsets
struct flagman_record 0B rom
field flagman_record 00 animation u16          # 01309F, 013120 "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's
field flagman_record 02 waving_animation u16   # 0130A2, 013123 -> object 80 (go_animation, in_view_animation)
field flagman_record 04 palette u16            # 0130A8, 013129 -> object 3A
field flagman_record 06 side s8                # 0130AD, 01312E -> object 3F (-1, 0)
field flagman_record 07 x_offset s16           # 0130B2, 013133 -> object 20
field flagman_record 09 y_offset s16           # 0130B7, 013138 -> object 24
# The record of a balloon let go at the start (start_balloon_update 0131BF-0131D5, from field 18; six,
# 0210DD-021154)
struct start_balloon_record 09 rom
field start_balloon_record 00 animation u16    # 0131C3 "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's (E2)
field start_balloon_record 02 palette u16      # 0131C6 -> object 3A (E4)
field start_balloon_record 04 side s8          # 0131CB -> object 3F (-1, 1)
field start_balloon_record 05 x_offset s16     # 0131D0 -> object 20
field start_balloon_record 07 y_offset s16     # 0131D5 -> object 24
# The record of Santa's sleigh (santa_sleigh_update 013358-01339F, from field 18; one, 02C493)
struct santa_sleigh_record 0E rom
field santa_sleigh_record 00 animation u16     # 01335C "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's (3A0)
field santa_sleigh_record 02 palette u16       # 01337B -> object 3A
field santa_sleigh_record 04 flags u8          # 013380: or-ed into object 00
field santa_sleigh_record 05 side s8           # 013384 -> object 3F
field santa_sleigh_record 06 x_offset s16      # 013389 -> object 20
field santa_sleigh_record 08 x_speed s16       # 01338E -> object 82 (-22 decimal)
field santa_sleigh_record 0A sound u16         # 013394 -> object 84 (49)
field santa_sleigh_record 0C draw_mode u8      # 01339A -> object 3E
field santa_sleigh_record 0D scale_shift u8    # 01339F "mov.b [R10], 44[R20]" -> object 44
# The record of what runs back and forth in front of a goal's crowd (goal_parade_rightward_update
# 01346C-01349F, goal_parade_leftward_update 0135E8-01361B, goal_parade_dragon_update 0136B8-0136DD, from
# field 18; eight, 02ABA4-02D58C)
struct goal_parade_record 0C rom
field goal_parade_record 00 animation u16      # 013470 "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's
field goal_parade_record 02 palette u16        # 013481 -> object 3A
field goal_parade_record 04 flags u8           # 013486: or-ed into object 00 (0, or 40 for the rightward ones)
field goal_parade_record 05 side s8            # 01348A -> object 3F (-1, 1)
field goal_parade_record 06 x_offset s16       # 01348F -> object 20
field goal_parade_record 08 x_speed s16        # 013494 -> object 80 (-22, 22 decimal)
field goal_parade_record 0A draw_mode u8       # 01349A -> object 3E (9)
field goal_parade_record 0B scale_shift u8     # 01349F -> object 44 (1)
# The record of a sounding_scenery (sounding_scenery_update 01353F-013572, from field 18; three, 02B69C,
# 02BA44, 02CAD0): road_row_record's fields, then the sound where a hit box would be
struct sounding_scenery_record 12 rom
field sounding_scenery_record 00 animation u16          # 013543 "movz.hw [R10+], R9": animation_start's
field sounding_scenery_record 02 palette u16            # 013546 -> object 3A
field sounding_scenery_record 04 flags u8               # 01354B: or-ed into object 00
field sounding_scenery_record 05 size_table u8          # 01354F -> object 45
field sounding_scenery_record 06 scale_shift u8         # 013554 -> object 44
field sounding_scenery_record 07 side s8                # 013559 -> object 3F
field sounding_scenery_record 08 x_offset s16           # 01355E -> object 20
field sounding_scenery_record 0A y_offset s16           # 013563 -> object 24
field sounding_scenery_record 0C spacing u16            # 013568 -> object 10
field sounding_scenery_record 0E count u16              # 01356D -> object 12
field sounding_scenery_record 10 sound u16              # 013572 "mov.h [R10], 80[R20]" (4A, 47, 46); its address -> object 60
# A traffic object's record (field 18: traffic_car_start 013900-013939, traffic_figure_update 013BB8-013C0C):
# the address of its sprite record, the side of the road, x_offset and speed (each list of stage_road_object_lists)
struct traffic_record 0A rom
field traffic_record 00 sprite u32 traffic_sprite_record   # 013904 "mov.w [R10+], R11"
field traffic_record 04 side s16              # 01392F "movt.hb [R10+], 3F[R20]": its low byte -> object 3F (-1, 0, 1)
field traffic_record 06 x_offset s16          # 013934 "movs.hw [R10+], 20[R20]" -> object 20
field traffic_record 08 speed s16             # 013939 -> object 2C
# What a traffic_record's first word points at (traffic_car_start 013907-01392B, traffic_figure_update
# 013BBF-013BF9): the animation, palette, the sounds of passing it and of hitting it, its weight, then its hit
# box, whose address goes to object 60 (030B60-031514, 10 apart)
struct traffic_sprite_record 10 rom
field traffic_sprite_record 00 animation u16     # 013907 "movz.hw [R11+], R9": animation_start's
field traffic_sprite_record 02 palette u16       # 01390F -> object 3A
field traffic_sprite_record 04 passing_sound u8  # 01391A -> object 81
field traffic_sprite_record 05 hit_sound u8      # 013920 -> object 82
field traffic_sprite_record 06 weight u16        # 013926 -> object 6A
field traffic_sprite_record 08 hit_box u16[4]    # its address -> object 60 (01392B), where traffic_hit_check reads it (030B60: 48 0 -20 20, decimal)
# The traffic of a stage (each list of stage_road_object_lists, from traffic_spawner field 18; 647 entries, closed by
# 8000 FFFF): traffic_spawner_update 013821-013814 starts the entries whose position, plus the stage's start, is
# within the draw distance of the leader's view, when their level is not above traffic_level
struct traffic_spawn_entry 10 rom
field traffic_spawn_entry 00 position s16          # 013821 "mov.h [R10+], R0", + stage_start (add.h; blt: signed) -> object 1C
field traffic_spawn_entry 02 level u16             # 013839 "cmp.h [R10+], 2C[R20]" (bl: unsigned): 0-E, against traffic_level
field traffic_spawn_entry 04 handler u32           # 013809 "mov.w [R10+], 4[R21]": the new object's handler
field traffic_spawn_entry 08 stop_distance u32     # 01380E "movt.wh [R10+], 84[R21]": its low half -> object 84
field traffic_spawn_entry 0C record u32            # 013814 "mov.w [R10+], 18[R21]": its traffic_record (traffic_line_record for a leader)
# The record a traffic leader starts with (traffic_car_start_convoy 01389E-0138D9, traffic_figure_start_team
# 013B56-013B8D, from field 18): the distance between the vehicles of the line, then the records of the ones
# behind it (traffic_follower, pulled_vehicle), closed by 0, then the leader's own traffic_record (030F90: 2,
# eight followers; 0311DC-0312C8: 1, one)
struct traffic_line_record 04 rom
field traffic_line_record 00 spacing u32         # 0138A2, 013B5A "mov.w [R10+], R0" (its low half: sub.h R0, R1)
# The records of the vehicles behind a traffic leader, after its traffic_line_record's spacing (0138D9, 013B91
# "mov.w [R10+], R12"), closed by 0: each one's traffic_record, into the new object's field 18
elements traffic_line_followers u32 traffic_record
field object_area 10 slots u8[7C]                    # a byte an object slot: FF used, 0 free
field object_list 00 count u16
field object_list 02 objects u16[7F]                 # the objects on the list (their places' low halves), count of them
field object 08 slot u8                # its slot in the objects' area (start_object), which run_objects frees when it dies
field object 00 flags u8               # bit 0: the object is live (run_objects runs it; else it frees its slot)
field object 04 handler u32            # the code run_objects runs for it each frame ("jsr [4[R20]]", 011CD2)
field object 0C state u8               # the step its handler is at: most handlers go by it through a table
field object 09 number u8              # what the code that starts it numbers it by: its place in its start list (start_object_list), a choice flag's player
field object 0A car u8                 # a choice flag's car (car_select_entries_run)
field object 0B car_colour u8          # the car whose colours the coin prompt's pieces take (coin_prompt_update)
# How draw_object draws it (by draw_mode, through draw_object_mode_jumps): an animation of the animations (sprite_animations),
# each a list of frames, each a list of pieces; the palette word (sprite_palettes); flags bits 6-7 flip it
field object 30 x u16                  # draw mode 0: its place on the screen, less the screen's scroll (screen 14, 16)
field object 32 y u16
field object 34 width_scale u16        # the pieces' width on the screen (sprite word 3) = their size * this >> 8
field object 36 height_scale u16       # ... and their height (sprite word 2)
field object 38 animation u16          # the animation it shows (sprite_animations)
field object 3A palette u16            # its palette (sprite_palettes: the sprites' palette words)
field object 3E draw_mode u8           # how draw_object places it: 0 on the screen at x, y; others on the road
field object 40 frame u8               # the frame of its animation
field object 43 size_step u8           # the row of piece sizes its pieces take (0x122B74, 3 a frame piece)
field object 44 scale_shift u8         # the road modes shift the road's scale left by it
field screen 20 race_copy u32 race     # 20FC00 for screen 1, 20FC80 for screen 2
field screen 24 race u32 race          # 20FB00 for screen 1, 20FB80 for screen 2

# ======== the frame loop ========
# 0008F0-000912: the program waits for the next frame polling 23[R24] (20E023), which an interrupt sets,
# and clears it; while B00[R25] says data is being unpacked it calls 010E42 for a piece of it each time
000908  code  frame_wait_loop    # "test.b 23[R24]": the poll
00090D  code  frame_wait_done    # the wait is over: a frame is done

# ======== the tasks ========
# The program runs two tasks, each on a stack of its own: the one boot starts runs the loop of screen 2
# (R25 = 218000, 000719), the other the loop of screen 1 (R25 = 208000, 0006B0). Boot (0005C5-0005EF)
# copies the second one's first interrupt frame (registers, PC 0006B0, PSW 00040000) from 000C98 to
# 21FF84, and keeps its stack pointers at 0[R24] (the frame) and 4[R24] (21F800, its L0SP). When 24[R24]
# is clear, the VBLANK handler swaps them (03F5FC): it stores the running task's pair there and returns
# into the other one
0006B0  func  second_task        # where the second task's first frame returns to
task  second_task
000719  func  second_task_frames   # both screens' frames from screen 2's, which second_task runs until the test mode
exit  second_task_frames  00077F   # the test mode: there second_task_frames goes back to second_task
03F5FC  code  task_swap          # 03F5FC-03F608: the running task's stacks go to 0[R24] and 4[R24], the other one's come back
03F60C  code  task_swap_done     # the handler goes on with the other task's interrupt frame: it returns into that task

# ======== the objects ========
# Below each screen's block (screen - 8000): this frame's list of objects at -8000 (a count, then the low
# halves of their addresses), the next frame's at -7FF8 (the two lists, at -7E00 and -7D00, swap every
# frame), the used slots at -7FF0 (7C bytes, FF used), a spare object at -7F00 and the 124 objects from
# -7C00, 100 bytes each
011CC0  func  run_objects        # the object loop: each live object of this frame's list (flags bit 0) has its handler run and goes on the next frame's list; a dead one frees its slot; then this frame's list is cleared and the two swap
011D43  func  start_object       # the first free slot: its 100 bytes cleared, live (flags bit 0), its slot number at 8, on this frame's list; R21 = the object (the spare at screen - 7F00 when no slot is free)
comment run_objects The object loop, once a frame for the screen: each live object (flags bit 0) of this frame's list (screen - 8000) has its handler run and goes on the next frame's list (screen - 7FF8); a dead one frees its slot (its byte at screen - 7FF0, by its slot number at 8). Then this frame's list is cleared and the two lists swap.
comment start_object Takes the first free object slot of the screen (FF into its byte at screen - 7FF0), clears its 100 bytes, makes it live (flags bit 0) with its slot number at 8, puts it on this frame's list and returns it; with no slot free, it returns the spare object at screen - 7F00 without any of that.

# ======== object handler lists ========
# A 32-bit count, then that many handlers: the code takes a list with "movea.b list[PC], R10" and starts an
# object per handler with "mov.w [R10+], R8" (the count) and "mov.w [R10+], 4[R21]" (011E2C-011E35); the
# object loop calls the handler at field 4 (011CD2, "jsr [4[R20]]")
003470  data  player_car_object_handlers  record=4 count=1 code=0    # the count at 00346C; list taken at 003133
003478  data  race_object_handlers  record=4 count=19 code=0   # the count at 003474; list taken at 003161
0034C8  data  demo_race_object_handlers  record=4 count=9 code=0    # the count at 0034C4; list taken at 003170
0034F0  data  route_map_object_handlers  record=4 count=1 code=0    # the count at 0034EC; list taken at 003257
001A7C  data  car_presentation_object_handlers  record=4 count=3 code=0    # the count at 001A78; list taken at 0014BA
002D5C  data  name_entry_object_handlers  record=4 count=4 code=0    # the count at 002D58

# Tables of lists taken by an index: "mov.w table[PC](R0), R10", then the records with [R10+]
014A20  data  ending_actor_lists  pointers=16 record=8 count=prefix32 code=0 data=4   # 0146E9-014709: a 32-bit count, then (handler -> field 4, data -> field 80) pairs
014A60  data  unfinished_ending_actor_count  record=8 count=prefix32 code=0 data=4    # the same, taken whole at 0146DB
0181B8  data  stage_background_object_lists  pointers=62 record=8 count=bytes32 code=0 data=4  # 017BD2-017BF6: the records' length in bytes (the count is >> 3), then (handler, data) pairs
02E1F0  data  stage_road_object_lists  pointers=32 record=16 end=FFFF8000 code=4 data=12  # 0137F1, 013809-013821: (position, type, handler -> field 4, field 84, field 18) records, closed by 8000 FFFF
00DF78  data  entry_invitation_object_handlers  record=4 count=3 code=0    # the count at 00DF74; list taken at 00DE57
000F74  data  isdn_connection_object_handlers  record=4 count=1 code=0     # the count at 000F70: game mode 8's (the ISDN connection)

# The objects of each stage's road: a stream of (position, command) halfword pairs, taken from this table by
# the stage's number (012720 and 0127BF, "mov.w 20800(R0), 80[R20]") and read at 01272C-012746 as the road
# goes by. Command 0 starts an object (handler -> field 4, then a parameter -> field 18, 012758); 1 ends the
# stage's stream, which goes on from this table when the next stage starts (012777, 0127A4-0127CA); 2 ends
# it for good (01279C); 3 is a fork: a table of the streams of its branches follows, and the one the route
# takes, C9[R23], goes on when the stage changes (012786, 0127CD-0127EB)
020800  data  road_object_streams  pointers=32 record=12 kinds=2:0=12,1=end,2=end,3=fork code=4 data=8

# A timeline: (frame, routine) pairs that 01AE0D-01AE2C calls when the object's counter, 10[R20], reaches the
# frame, from the address 01ADFC puts in 80[R20]; closed by frame 0000FFFF
01AFC0  data  timeline_1AFC0  record=8 end=0000FFFF code=4
01AE24  code  timeline_call  calls=timeline_1AFC0    # "jsr [R1]": the routine of the record the counter reached

# The radio DJ's voice lines: (sound << 24 | address) records; the address is a string of mouth shapes
# (indices into 018AE8, pairs of the LCD's custom characters, shown in its characters 0-1) closed by a
# negative byte. start_dj_message (019402) starts line R0 (the sound through request_sound, the string's
# address to F30[R25]); 0189D9 shows its next shape every third frame
019576  data  dj_messages  record=4 count=61 data=0

# 019266-019294: lists of routines an object runs one after another, a step each time; 019280 picks the list
# by a number below 14 and keeps its place at 38[R20]
01966C  data  dj_remark_lists  pointers=14 record=4 end=00000000 code=0
0196F8  data  dj_mouth_shapes      # the DJ's mouth shapes, a string a message (dj_messages' low 24 bits point at them): shapes 0-2 (dj_face_characters), lcd_update one every third frame (0189F3), closed by a negative byte
019294  code  routine_list_jump  calls=dj_remark_lists   # "jmp [R0]": the list's next routine

# ======== code and data nothing uses ========
# No scenario runs or reads them, and no table or instruction holds their addresses: functions nothing
# calls, code a branch or a jump right before it always goes past, and tables nothing reads. The "func"
# ones are decompiled too. The "code" ones are left out of the C: they jump or call into the middle of
# functions the game runs, and their C would cut those functions in two (tools/coverage.py counts them
# as code all the same)

# A backdrop sprite's data (the data, 80, of backdrop_far, backdrop_near, backdrop_landmark,
# backdrop_flashing and backdrop_translucent; stage_background_object_lists): their starts read it with [R10+] from
# "mov.w 80[R20], R10" (017D7E, 017E29, 017F00, 018017, 0180D9): "mov.w [R10+], R9" (animation_start's: 017D83),

# approach_distance, which only the landmarks' records have (the others are 12 bytes); census ROM 0182D4-01847B

034308  table fork_branch_piece_animation  sprite_animation_pointer   # fork_branch_sprite (014066 "mov.w 34138(R26), R26" with R26 = 74): entry 74 of the animations' table (sprite_animations), the record of animation 74, the branch's piece of road
0140C8  table fork_branch_stage_palettes  u16   # fork_branch_sprite (0140A7 "movz.hw 140C8[PC](R15), R15"): the palette (into sprite_palettes) of the branch's road piece by the stage being built (screen built_stage), 32 stages
0129AB  func  scenery_pair_update
0129F4  func  overhead_scenery_update
012ACF  func  fork_sign_update
012AF4  func  animated_scenery_update
012B0C  func  road_row_start_copy
012B84  func  animated_scenery_pair_update
012BAE  func  road_crosser_update
012DB7  func  moving_scenery_update
013104  func  flagman_update
comment scenery_update Static scenery along the road (trees, buildings, crowds, banners, signs): a row of identical sprites from its record. By state: 0 road_row_start; 1 road_row_crash_check, then road_row_draw; 2 (a record without a hit box) road_row_draw only. Ends when road_row_draw finds the whole row behind the view.
comment scenery_pair_update Static scenery drawn twice, at each side of the road, the second mirrored (rows of trees, houses, spectators). By state: 0 road_row_start; 1 road_row_pair_crash_check, then road_row_pair_draw; 2 (no hit box) road_row_pair_draw only.
comment fork_sign_update The signs on the road where it forks at the end of a stage: a line down the middle before the fork and a line across each branch after it. By state: 0 road_row_start; 1 fork_sign_hit_check, then road_row_draw; 2 road_row_draw only. A car that hits one is slowed, not crashed.
comment fork_sign_hit_check road_row_hit_check with kind 3 (R27): a car that hits a fork sign is slowed for 15 frames, as off the road (car slowed_frames), instead of crashing.
comment animated_scenery_update Scenery that animates in place (the Ferris wheel, windmills; also the scenery of the goal lines). By state: 0 road_row_start_copy; 1 road_row_crash_check; 1 and 2 animation_advance and road_row_draw.
comment animated_scenery_pair_update Animated scenery drawn twice, at each side of the road, the second mirrored (the people lining the start and the goal, the scenery around the forks). By state: 0 road_row_start_copy; 1 road_row_pair_crash_check; 1 and 2 animation_advance and road_row_pair_draw.
comment flashing_scenery_update Scenery whose palette cycles a step every frame, for flashing lights (the Christmas trees, the arches over the underwater tunnel, a fairground ride). By state: 0 reads its record as road_row_start does, except that the second and third halfwords are the address of its list of palettes (palette_cycle) instead of a palette; 1 road_row_crash_check; 1 and 2 the next palette from the list into palette (a negative entry goes back that many bytes first), then road_row_draw.
comment overhead_scenery_update Animated scenery high over the road, drawn from the middle of the screen instead of the road's centre line (screen_centered), so it does not swing with the bends: the schools of fish over the underwater tunnel, the cloud bands of stage 25. By state: 0 screen_centered = FF, then road_row_start (its records have no hit box); 1 and 2 animation_advance and road_row_draw.
comment flagman_update The man waving a chequered flag at each fork and at the goal. By state: 0 reads the record (animation, in_view_animation, palette, side, x_offset, y_offset), draw_mode 9, scale_shift 1, appear_position = position - draw_distance (and 58 = position - 30, never read); 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position, then in_view_animation (06F, the waving; so 06E is never drawn); 1 and 2: ends once the car (car window_segment) reaches its position, else animation_advance and draw_object.
comment road_crosser_update What walks across the road as the car comes near (the camels of the desert stages). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, crossing_animation, palette, flags, side, x_offset, trigger_distance, x_speed, draw_mode, scale_shift; y_offset -256 for animation 165), trigger_position = position - trigger_distance, appear_position = position - draw_distance; 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position; 2 drawn standing (draw_object) until the car (car segment) reaches trigger_position, then crossing_animation; 3 while the car (car window_segment) has not reached it: x_offset += x_speed and position + 1 each frame, and while within draw_distance of the car animation_advance and draw_object; it ends when the car reaches it, it gets beyond draw_distance or its screen_x leaves -F0..130.
comment moving_scenery_update Scenery that travels across the view once the car comes near (helicopters, the roller coaster train, birds, fish over the tunnel, a tanker truck on a bridge). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, flags, draw_mode, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, trigger_distance + 16, x_speed, y_speed, road_speed), trigger_position = position - trigger_distance; 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes trigger_position; 1 and 2: while the car (car window_segment) has not reached it, each frame x_offset += x_speed, y_offset += y_speed, position += road_speed, and while within draw_distance of the car animation_advance and draw_object; it ends when the car reaches it, it gets beyond draw_distance or its screen_x leaves -F0..130.
comment road_row_start Sets up a row of identical sprites along the road from its record (record): animation (animation_start), palette, flags bits to set (bit 6 mirrors the sprites), size_table, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, spacing, count and the hit box (hit_box points at it; none when its first 32 bits are 0); first_position = position, end_position = position + spacing * (count - 1) + 1. Goes on to the next state, or the one after it (no hit check) without a hit box. Used by scenery, scenery_pair, fork_sign and overhead_scenery.
comment road_row_start_copy The same code as road_row_start, at another address: the first step of animated_scenery and animated_scenery_pair.
comment road_row_hit_check Whether the screen's car hits an item of the row, unless the car's object has flags bit 3 set: the car's box (the entry screen->own_hit_box points at: its segments at +6 and +8, along the road +A..+C in 128ths of a segment from its first, across +E..+10) against each item's hit_box (along from (item - the car's first segment) * 128 + position_fraction, across from the road's width * side + x_offset), for the items from first_position to end_position within the car's segments. On the first that overlaps: the car's object gets flags bit 2 and byte 6E = R27, the kind of hit (0 a crash, 3 slowed), and the car's hit_along and hit_across half the distances from the car's box to the item's (along, across).
comment road_row_crash_check road_row_hit_check with kind 0 (R27): hitting an item crashes the car. Used by scenery, animated_scenery and flashing_scenery (and 012FBC).
comment road_row_pair_crash_check road_row_hit_check for the rows drawn in mirrored pairs, with kind 0 (a crash): each item's hit box is tested as it is and mirrored across the road's centre. Used by scenery_pair and animated_scenery_pair.
comment road_row_draw Draws the row's items in view: ends the object once end_position is not past the view's start (race_copy window_segment); nothing while first_position is beyond the draw distance (screen->draw_distance >> 7 segments); moves first_position up to the first item in view; for each item (spacing apart, to the draw distance) where the road has a scale: x = (the road's width * side + x_offset) * scale >> 12 plus the road's screen x unless screen_centered, y = y_offset * scale >> 12 plus the road's screen y; off the screen (x beyond -1E0..1E0, y beyond -160..80) it is skipped, else the scale shifted by scale_shift picks the size (size_table: a size step and a shift) and a sprite of the animation's frame with its palette (flags bits 6-7 flip it) goes into the screen's sprite list (sprite_list_last; the copy at +3000 is the halfway point from the item's sprite of the frame before, previous_sprites) and the depth list of its road segment (screen + 5000). Sets flags bit 5 and keeps where its sprites went in previous_sprites. Used by scenery, fork_sign, animated_scenery, flashing_scenery and overhead_scenery (and 01351E).
comment road_row_pair_draw road_row_draw for the rows drawn in mirrored pairs: each item twice, at the same distance either side of the road's centre, the second with sprite attribute bits 43 toggled (bit 6 flips it, bits 0-1 swap its X alignment); there is no off-screen test and no screen_centered. Used by scenery_pair and animated_scenery_pair.
comment animation_start Starts animation R9 (sprite_animations[R9] is its record) at its first frame: frame_time and frame_countdown from the record's first byte, size_step 0, and fields 34 and 36 to 100 (the width and height zoom of the screen-position drawing, draw_object_on_screen: 100 the sprite's own size).
comment animation_advance Steps the object's animation: nothing while frame_time is 0; when frame_countdown runs out (back to frame_time), the next frame. A frame's entry in the record (32 bits at +4 + frame * 4) is the address of its data when positive, else an order: 0 holds the frame before (frame_time 0), -1 goes back to frame 0, -2 starts the animation whose number is the next entry.
comment draw_object Draws the object's sprite, unless flags bit 1 is set or the screen's sprite list is full: field 3E picks the routine (draw_object_mode_jumps): some project the object's road position (1C) with its offsets (20, 24) and set fields 30 and 32 to where it lands and 3C to the road's depth layer there, some draw it at the screen position in 30 and 32, some copy a ready sprite entry from the address in field 38; then links it into the depth list of layer 3C (screen + 5000).

# The car select's records and tables of the program ROM
# Each screen's tilemap set-up for the car select (car_select_run 0050BB "movea.w 6920[PC](R0), R10" with
# R0 = screen index << 3: 20 bytes a screen; 006920 screen 1, 006940 screen 2): the addresses of the
# tilemap register copies it keeps (0050C1-0050DF "mov.w [R10+], 98/9C/A8/AC/A0/A4[R20]": 20E2A2 20E2A6
# 20E292 20E296 31C000 31C400 for screen 1), the background record for set_up_background (0050E5 "mov.w
# [R10+], R19": 006960, 006974) and the bits or-ed into video register 2 (0050F2 "or.h [R10+], 204[R24]":
# E005, E00A)
struct car_select_tilemap_setup 20 rom
field car_select_tilemap_setup 00 scroll_x_address u32
field car_select_tilemap_setup 04 scroll_y_address u32
field car_select_tilemap_setup 08 second_scroll_x_address u32
field car_select_tilemap_setup 0C second_scroll_y_address u32
field car_select_tilemap_setup 10 rowscroll_address u32
field car_select_tilemap_setup 14 rowselect_address u32
field car_select_tilemap_setup 18 background u32 rom
field car_select_tilemap_setup 1C video_register_bits u16
local car_select_run 0050BB r10 setup car_select_tilemap_setup
006920  table car_select_tilemap_setups car_select_tilemap_setup

# A ring of the turntable's scenery (turntable_scenery_show 005784 "movea.w 6A18[PC](R0), R10" by group * 2:
# 8 bytes, twelve groups): its animation (00578A "movz.hw [R10+], R9", animation_start's), palette (005793
# "mov.h [R10+], 3A[R20]"), size_step in the top four bits and size in the rest (005798 "mov.h [R10+], R0":
# 0140, 2400, 1300) and the offset from that last word to its list of items (0057AF "movz.hw [R10], R0",
# 0057B2 "movea.b [R10](R0), 18[R20]": angle, radius and height of each, closed by a negative angle)

field turntable_scenery_group 06 item_list_offset u16

comment lcd_race_info_start Starts the race information on the cabinet LCD: clears it (clear_lcd), shows the DJ's face in its first two characters (screen dj_face) and goes on to lcd_race_info_update.
comment lcd_race_info_update Shows the race on the cabinet LCD. Before the start, while the car object's timer counts the frames to it: a bar shrinking through each second (lcd_countdown_bars) with the seconds left. Then a page every 150 frames (page_frames), by page: 0 Time (lcd_race_info_time), 1 Order (the car numbers, car_numbers + 1, in race order from order_list, this screen's own blinking; skipped with one player), 2 Speed (screen shown_speed), 3 Max. (screen maximum_speed), 4 Ave. (lcd_race_info_average); page_step 0 writes the label, 1 updates the number. When the car reaches the goal (car at_goal) goes to lcd_race_info_finish; when the race is over (car race_over) shows the average and ends; while the LCD scrolls a text (screen lcd_scroll) waits in lcd_race_info_paused.
comment lcd_race_info_time_page Writes the Time page's label on the LCD, then the time (lcd_race_info_time).
comment lcd_race_info_time Writes the race time (the car's race_time, in thousandths of a second) on the LCD as minutes'seconds"hundredths.
comment lcd_race_info_average_page Writes the Ave. page's label on the LCD, then the average speed (lcd_race_info_average).
comment lcd_race_info_average Writes the average speed on the LCD: the sum of the speeds (screen speed_sum) over how many there are (speed_samples), with lcd_race_info_write_number.
comment lcd_race_info_write_number Writes a number of up to three digits on the LCD, right-aligned to end before the address it is given (F1C of the screen for the km/h pages), spaces for the leading zeros.
comment lcd_race_info_finish The car has reached the goal: shows ### FINISH ### on the LCD, waits for a text to scroll across it (screen lcd_scroll) and out, shows the average speed for 150 frames (frames), then the race time, and ends.
comment lcd_race_info_paused Waits while the LCD scrolls a text (screen lcd_scroll), then goes back to lcd_race_info_update, which writes the page's label again.
comment car_radio_start Sets up the radio as the race starts: the car each player chose (byte 49 of its race_copy record) by car number in car_models (FF where none) and how many in cars_to_introduce, this screen's car number, clears screen nearing_fork, gives the car the song of its model (car song = car_model_songs by car model), notes the stage (screen route_stage), then car_radio_wait_start.
comment car_radio_wait_start Waits for the start (the car object's timer, the frames to it, down to 0), then goes on to car_radio_read_buttons.
comment car_radio_read_buttons Each frame of the race: the buttons newly pressed (screen buttons_pressed) into buttons, then car_radio_update. Once the race is over (car at_goal at the goal, or race_over) clears them, silences the DJ (talk_step 5), turns the screen's two lamps off (button_lamps) and leaves car_radio_update alone, without the buttons.
comment car_radio_update The radio, by state: 0 plays the car's song (car song) with its title; 1 << and >> step through the ten songs (both together: song 10, Jingle Bell), START does >>, the DJ/music button goes to the station; 2 the station: title "Mega Radio Station", DJ message 29 queued, the stage's sound (route_stage_radio_sounds by screen route_stage); 3 the station plays; 4-8 while screen nearing_fork is set (fork_camera_update sets it, a new route_stage clears it): 3F to the screen's sound byte (fade_out_music_at), sound E0 after 60 frames, sound EE, 7F when it clears and after 120 frames the stage's message (stage_level_radio_messages by screen current_stage_level) and sound. On the station (3, without nearing_fork) and while 4-8 wait, the DJ talks (car_radio_talk).
comment car_radio_talk The station's DJ, each frame of car_radio_update's state 3 (without screen nearing_fork) and while its states 4-8 wait: any of the three buttons goes back to the car's song (state 0, talk_step 4, screen dj_mouth_shapes 0, sound 1F); while a message plays (dj_mouth_shapes) the queued_message is dropped; else by talk_step: 0 message 0, 1-2 each car number (message 9 + number) and its car (message 1 + model), cars_to_introduce of them, 3 message 2A, 4 the queued_message, or in state 3 the next remark of the list (remark_list), a new list of dj_remark_lists 14 times in 1024, 5 nothing.
comment car_radio_dj_leader A DJ remark: message 11 plus the car number of the car in the lead.
comment car_radio_dj_own_car A DJ remark: message 19 plus this screen's car number.
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2a A DJ remark: message 2A (also the end of the cars' introduction).
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2b A DJ remark: message 2B.
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2d A DJ remark: message 2D.
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2f A DJ remark: message 2F.
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2c A DJ remark: message 2C.
comment car_radio_song Shows the title of song R0 (radio_songs: 0 Magical Sound Shower, 1 Passing Breeze, 2 Splash Wave, 3 Picture the Rivers, 4 Blow Your Cool, 5 Looking for the Rainbow, 6 Speed King, 7 Adventure, 8 Sonic Control, 9 Last Wave, 10 Jingle Bell) and returns the song's sound.
comment car_radio_show_title Starts a song title (start_song_title) for the record it is given, sends the one shown (title) away and keeps the new one in title.
comment staff_credits_code_start Starts watching for the secret code during the presentation of the cars: takes the controls held (staff_credits_code_read_controls), puts code_place at the start of the code (001ADC) and goes on to staff_credits_code_watch.
comment staff_credits_code_watch Watches the controls for the code (staff_credits_code_sequence: shift down twice, shift up twice, the wheel left, right, left, right, the brake, the gas; a wrong one starts over); when it is complete: sound 9, the presentation 1200 frames longer (screen mode_timer), and staff_credits_code_show.
comment staff_credits_code_show Shows the staff credits (the texts at 001AE8, draw_text) for 1200 frames, then fade_out_music and staff_credits_code_done.
comment staff_credits_code_done Does nothing: the code has been used; the object stays until the presentation's objects are cleared.
comment staff_credits_code_read_controls Reads the controls as bits (0 shift up, 1 shift down, 2 the wheel right, 3 the wheel left, 4 the brake, 5 the gas, each with less needed to stay on), keeps them in controls_held and returns the ones newly on (zero set when there are none).
comment transmission_label_start_automatic Sets up the label of the automatic transmission's panel (sprite set 2C1, 24 below the panel's place; the frames show NOVICE there), then transmission_label_follow.
comment transmission_label_start_manual Sets up the label of the manual transmission's panel (sprite set 2C9, its frame from car_gears_caption_frames by the car's model; the frames show 5 SPEED there for the car coin_start takes), then transmission_label_follow.
comment transmission_label_follow Keeps the label on its panel (the panel's place plus its offsets, the panel's palette) and draws it; ends when the panel ends.
comment entry_countdown_start Sets up the invitation to join, on a seat with no player while the entry time of a game runs: the screen (set_up_background, start_palette_fades), clears the LCD (clear_lcd) with the DJ's face, the music (sound F, Mega Driver) and DJ message 3B, marks the screen (entry_countdown_shown) so the car select does not start them again, starts the WAITING YOUR ENTRY banner (entry_banner), the ring of cars (entry_car_ring, in car_ring) and the traffic behind it (entry_traffic_run), and goes on to entry_countdown_count.
comment entry_countdown_count Shows the entry time left (RACE_COPY_1's entry_frames, frames): the seconds as the big number (its frame), the frames into the second to the ring of cars, and on the LCD a shrinking bar (entry_countdown_lcd_texts) with the seconds; when it is over clears the screen's entry_countdown_shown, fade_out_music, and goes on to entry_countdown_done.
comment entry_countdown_done Does nothing (the number is no longer drawn): the entry time is over.
comment entry_banner_start Sets up the WAITING YOUR ENTRY banner (sprite set 287) at the centre of the screen, then entry_banner_blink.
comment entry_banner_blink Draws the banner 16 frames out of every 32.
comment entry_car_ring_start Sets up the ring of cars around the entry countdown with the first car's sprite (turntable_car_looks), then entry_car_ring_draw.
comment entry_car_ring_draw Draws a ring of as many copies of a car around the countdown as frames have gone in the second (copies, set by entry_countdown_count, at most 16); with a new second, the next of the eight cars (car).
comment stage_indicator_start Sets up the STAGE label at the top left of the race screen (sprite set 2E), then stage_indicator_draw.
comment stage_indicator_draw Draws the stage's name after the label (stage_level_texts by the forks taken, screen route_stage_level: PRE-STAGE, 1 to 4, FINAL STAGE) and the label itself.
comment car_select_start Starts the car select with 630 frames (21 seconds) to choose, then car_select_run.
comment car_select_run The car select, by state: 0 sets the screen up (unless screen entry_countdown_shown says the entry countdown did: clears the LCD, the title Mega Driver, sound F, DJ message 3B), the tile layer's tables from 006920, the WELCOME! letters; 1 when the screen's data is unpacked (unpack_state) starts the eight cars on the turntable and the rest, the default car (car_select_default_cars by the player's place in the race), the time display (time_digits_start); 2 30 frames; 3 the screen's turntable_depth_offset up to 0, then the instructions (select_car_heading_show) and the car's caption (car_caption_show); 4 the wheel turns the turntable (turn, turn_delay, angle; car model is the car in front) until the gas or START confirms or time_left runs out: race model = the car, car_choice = the car + 1, the car's sound (car_select_car_sounds), at least 180 frames left, the transmission choice (transmission_choice_run); 5 the wheel picks the transmission (car transmission = car_select_wheel_transmissions by the wheel: 0 automatic, 1 manual) until confirmed or out of time; 6-8 when the entry time is over (entry_over) marks the player ready (set_player_ready) and waits for the race's other players (players_not_ready), DJ message 3C; 9 60 frames, then fade_out_music and the game's next step (screen game_mode byte 2); 10 stays. Every frame from 4 on: car_select_turn_to_choice and car_select_update_rowscroll; time_left down by one and shown in seconds.
comment car_select_turn_to_choice Once the car is chosen (car_chosen), turns the turntable the short way round until the chosen car (car model) is in front, 20 a frame, skewing the floor as it goes.
comment car_select_update_rowscroll Writes the line scroll of the tile layer for the next frames: the top 104 lines all at top_scroll, which moves one more each frame, and while rowscroll_copies says, the floor's 120 lines from their offsets at 3800 of the screen.
comment car_select_skew_floor_next Skews the turntable's floor for a step toward the next car (car_select_skew_floor with 6).
comment car_select_skew_floor_previous Skews the turntable's floor for a step toward the previous car (car_select_skew_floor with -6).
comment car_select_skew_floor Adds a step that grows line by line (by R0) to the floor's 120 line offsets at 3800 of the screen, writes them to the next two pages of the line scroll and has car_select_update_rowscroll copy them to the others (rowscroll_copies).
comment car_select_wait_confirm Whether the player has not confirmed yet (zero set): confirming is pressing the gas pedal down (screen gas from C0, after letting it up under 40) or START, once confirm_delay has run out.
comment welcome_letter_start Starts the eight letters of WELCOME! (itself the first), then welcome_letter_show.
comment welcome_letter_show Shows its letter of WELCOME! (font 346): appears 2 frames after the one before it, cycles its colours (step_palette, from 006DCC) and ends 45 frames after the start.
comment clear_lcd Clears the cabinet's LCD: sixteen spaces, no DJ message (screen dj_mouth_shapes), no scrolling text (lcd_scroll_request, lcd_scroll), the DJ's face off (dj_face).
comment start_dj_message Starts DJ message R0 (typed_messages): requests its voice (the sound in the record's top byte) and has the face on the LCD move its mouth by the record's string (screen dj_mouth_shapes).
comment start_dj_message_if_quiet Starts DJ message R0 (start_dj_message) unless one is being said (screen dj_mouth_shapes).
comment start_song_title Starts an object that shows the song title of record R10 (x, then the text) on the screen and scrolls it on the LCD; returns the object.
comment random_number Returns the next pseudo-random number of the screen's sequence (kept in random_state).
comment request_sound Requests sound R0 for the screen (queue_sound), except while the screen's demo_flags bit 8 is set, when only the sounds the table at 01202E (demo_sounds) allows play, and only with advertise_sound set.
comment queue_sound Puts sound R0 in the screen's ring of 32 sound requests (801 of the screen: the place, then the requests).
comment draw_text Draws the text record R19 (x and y, an attribute, a string closed by 0) as one sprite per character and returns the address of the record after it.
comment step_palette Gives the object the next palette (palette, an index into the sprites' palette words, sprite_palettes) of its palette_list; a negative entry goes back by that many bytes, to loop.
comment binary_to_bcd Converts R0 into eight decimal digits, one per four bits, the units in the lowest.
comment set_player_ready Marks the screen's player ready in its race record (ready), after its selections.
comment players_not_ready Whether a player of the race has not marked itself ready yet (zero set): byte 5C of each one's race_copy record.

014994  table goal_actor_command_jumps  u32   # goal_actor_run (014990 "jmp [R0]") jumps through it by the command word after a step with bit 0 set: 0149AC 0149B5 0149BA 0149C5 0149CE 0149D8
014A16  table goal_actor_place_waits  u8   # goal_actor_run's command 5 "movz.bh 14A16[PC](R1), R1" (0149E5) by the screen's place (320[R25] + player), "shl.h #3" (8 counts each) added to the repeat's count; census ROM 014A16-014A1D (00 02 02 03 03 03 04 04)
# The words of a goal actor's script (the data of ending_actor_lists, unfinished_ending_actor_count: goal_actor script,
# 80, and step, 84): a word of flags, then a word for each flag set: goal_actor_run "movz.hw [R10+], R5"
# (01484B), "cmp.h [R10+], 10[R20]" (0148BF, 0148D6), "movt.hb [R10+], 40[R20]" (014900), "add.h"/"mov.h
# [R10+]" (014908-01494A), "movz.hw [R10+], R9" (014952), "mov.h [R10+], 3A[R20]" (014974), "movz.hw [R10+],
# R0" (01497C, 014988), "mov.h [R10+], R0" (0149F0); census ROM 01500C-016AFF
# (156 scripts, from the 17 lists; the first: 8000 0032, a wait of 32, then 1508 ...)
elements goal_actor_script_words u16

local goal_actor_run 0148BF t1 wait_count

017BFB  func  palette_cycle_update
017C08  table palette_cycle_state_jumps  u32   # palette_cycle_update (017C04 "jmp [R0]") jumps through it by state: 017C18 017C44 017C56 017C5B
017C68  table palette_cycle_running_jumps  u32   # palette_cycle_update (017C64 "jmp [R0]") jumps through it by running: 017C70 017C79
# A palette_cycle's data (palette_cycle data, 80; stage_background_object_lists): palette_cycle_update reads it with
# [R10+] from 017C18 "mov.w 80[R20], R10"; census ROM 018330-0184CB (8 records: 0000 0383 14159C 1, 04B0 0000
# 1415E0 1, ...)
struct palette_cycle_record C rom
field palette_cycle_record 00 delay u16          # "mov.h [R10+], 10[R20]" (017C1D)
field palette_cycle_record 02 frames_left u16    # "mov.h [R10+], 12[R20]" (017C22)
field palette_cycle_record 04 palette_list u32   # "mov.w [R10+], 38[R20]" (017C27)
field palette_cycle_record 08 step_frames u32    # "mov.w [R10+], R0" (017C2C), its low half into 14 and 16
# The steps of a palette_cycle's list (palette_cycle palette_list, 38): the address of a palette list for
# queue_palette_list, or a negative number of bytes to go back: "mov.w [R10+], R19" (017C8E, 017C98), "test.w
# R19" (bp), "add.w R19, R10" (017C95)
elements palette_cycle_steps u32
017CAA  func  sunset_fade_start
017CB8  table sunset_fade_state_jumps  u32   # sunset_fade_start (017CB3 "jmp [R0]") jumps through it by state: 017CC4 017CDB 017CE4
# A sunset_fade's data (sunset_fade data, 80; stage_background_object_lists): sunset_fade_start reads it with [R10+] from
# 017CC4 "mov.w 80[R20], R10"; census ROM 018328 (384 14152C), 018458 (0 141700), 018498 (12C 141740), 0184A0
# (258 1417A4)
struct sunset_fade_record 8 rom
field sunset_fade_record 00 delay u32       # "mov.w [R10+], 10[R20]" (017CC9: count)
field sunset_fade_record 04 fade_list u32   # "mov.w [R10+], 38[R20]" (017CCE)
# A list of palette fades (start_palette_fades, road_surface_fade_back, race_background_fade_palettes and
# sunset_fade_start read them): a length, which divided by 12 is the number of the palette_fade_entry records
# after it (the lists say 12 n + 2): sunset_fade_start "mov.w [R19+], R18; divu.w #C, R18" (017CEF, 017CF2);
# census ROM 14152C (6E: 9 records), 141700 (3E: 5), 141740 (62: 8), 1417A4 (62: 8)
struct palette_fade_list 4 rom
field palette_fade_list 00 length u32
# A palette fade of such a list (12 bytes), for start_palette_fade: sunset_fade_start reads it with [R19+]:
# "mov.d [R19+], R10" (017CFF: R10 and R11), "mov.h [R19+], R12" (017D02), "mov.b [R19+], R0" (017D05), "mov.b
# [R19+], R1" (017D08); census ROM 141530-141809 (sunset_fade's lists: 143154 143274 2DA0 01 08, ...)
struct palette_fade_entry C rom
field palette_fade_entry 00 from u32           # the palette faded from (R10), the to one (R11) next
field palette_fade_entry 04 to u32
field palette_fade_entry 08 palette_offset u16 # R12
field palette_fade_entry 0A blocks u8          # R0
field palette_fade_entry 0B period u8          # R1
local sunset_fade_start 017CEF R19 fade palette_fade_entry
local sunset_fade_start 017CEB,017CFF,017D02,017D05,017D08 R19 fade
