01818A  func  backdrop_off_screen
comment road_object_stream_read Starts the objects along the road of the stage being built (road_object_streams). By state: 0 takes the stream of the stage being built (screen built_stage); 1 reads the stream's records while their position is not beyond the segments the road generator has built of the stage (car stage_segments): command 0 road_object_stream_start_object, 1 road_object_stream_stage_end, 2 road_object_stream_end, 3 road_object_stream_fork; 2 (the stage's stream is over) waits until the generator builds the stage the route goes to (screen built_stage = stage), then stage_start = car built_segments (the segment the new stage starts at) and its stream; 3 (a fork) waits until the route's next stage is known (screen route_stage no longer stage), then goes on with the stream of the branch the car takes (car branch) from the fork's table, the same stage_start.
comment road_object_stream_start_object Command 0 of a stream: starts an object (start_object) at the record's position plus stage_start (field 1C), with the record's handler and its parameter in field 18 (most often the address of the object's record in the ROM), and moves the stream past the record.
comment road_object_stream_stage_end Command 1: the stage's stream is over; notes the stage the route goes to next (screen route_stage) in stage and waits for it (state 2).
comment road_object_stream_fork Command 3, a fork: keeps the address of the table of the branches' streams in stream, the current stage (screen built_stage) in stage, and waits for the branch (state 3).
comment road_object_stream_end Command 2: the last stage's stream is over (the goals): ends the reader.
comment crest_clip_update Cuts the sprites beyond the road's crests: each frame, for the crests the road's projection found (screen crest_count of them; their depth layers at 1F10 of the screen and their screen heights at 1F30), each whose layer is lower than all those before it gets a sprite clip command in its layer (draw_mode 6: the 16-byte entry at clip_command, command 7000: the drawing area from the top of the screen down to clip_bottom = the crest's height + screen sprite_origin_y - 1, across clip_left..clip_right), so the sprites the list draws after it (meant: what lies beyond the crest) are cut at the crest's height.
comment traffic_spawner_update Starts the traffic of the stage the race's leader is in (stage_road_object_lists). By state: 0 the list of the stage being built (screen built_stage), traffic_level = 8 - the number of players (byte 1F of the record screen host_record points at) + the game setting game_difficulty, the draw distance into screen traffic_draw_distance, no traffic yet (screen traffic_count); 1 (outside the attract demo, screen demo_flags bit 8) when the leader's stage (screen leader_stage) changes, takes its list with positions from where the leader was then (screen leader_window_segment); starts each record (position, level, handler, stop_distance, record) whose position is within the draw distance of the leader's view (window_segment of the race record screen leader_record), when its level is not above traffic_level and fewer than 32 are on the road (screen traffic_count); then writes at F84 of the screen the stretches of road the players can see (each player's view, window_segment of the race records listed after byte 1F, give or take the draw distance; a count, then each stretch's top and bottom from the highest), for traffic_out_of_view.
comment traffic_car_start Sets up a traffic car from its record (the address of its sprite record, then side, x_offset and speed): from the sprite record its animation (animation_start), palette, passing_sound, hit_sound, weight and hit_box (the sprite record's last 8 bytes); draw_mode B (the frame chosen by the angle it is seen from, traffic_car_draw), scale_shift FF, passed 0; goes on to state 1.
comment traffic_car_draw Moves and draws a traffic car: keeps the position_fraction; ends it (taking it off the count, screen traffic_count) when traffic_out_of_view; plays passing_sound whenever the screen's car (race_copy segment) passes it or it passes the car (passed); while it is within the draw distance ahead of the view (race_copy window_segment) picks its frame by the angle the player sees it from: a column (4-1B) from the difference between the road's direction (+0 of the road's segment record) at the car and at it, plus a quarter of its screen_x, and a row (0-3, 20 frames apart) from the difference in height (+2) and the car's pitch (car view_pitch), shaken for 20 frames after a hit (shake, traffic_shake_frame_offsets); traffic_hit_check, and on a hit traffic_start_hit_sound and shake = 1; draw_object.
comment traffic_start_hit_sound When the traffic object has a hit_sound, starts an object (013D15) that plays it 15 frames later.
comment traffic_out_of_view Whether road position R0 is outside every stretch of road the race's players see (zero set: the traffic there is removed): the stretches traffic_spawner writes at F84 of the screen (a count, then each stretch's top and bottom).
comment traffic_hit_check Whether the screen's car hits this traffic object (negative set), unless the car's object has flags bit 3 set: the car's box (the entry screen->own_hit_box points at) against the object's hit_box (along the road from (position - the car's first segment) * 128 + position_fraction, across from the road's width * side + x_offset). On a hit the car's object gets flags bit 2, byte 6E = 1 (the kind of hit: a single object) and 68 = this object, whose speed and weight the crash (007DBD) reads.
comment traffic_car_update A traffic car, drawn from the angle the player sees it at. By state: 0 traffic_car_start; 1 moves along the road by speed (128ths of a segment a frame, position_fraction carrying into position), then traffic_car_draw; once the screen's car has reached the goal (car at_goal) it ends when it is 8 segments behind the view.
comment traffic_car_start_convoy The first vehicle of a convoy (the only one: stage 25, animation 35C followed by eight 35D): its record starts with the distance between them and the records of those that follow, closed by 0; starts each (handler 013AB7: traffic_car_update, kept live as long as the one before it is, which ends it) that distance behind the one before, keeping the one before in its field 14, with the same stop_distance, counted in screen traffic_count; then becomes a traffic_car with the record after the list.
comment traffic_stopping_car_update A traffic car that slows to a stop: as traffic_car_update, but at each segment it counts stop_distance down and, once it has run out (stopping), its speed drops by one a segment to 0. The cars of stages 2 and 12, and what the traffic_figure teams pull (013AD8 runs it).
comment traffic_figure_start_team The leader of a team (the horses pulling a covered wagon in stage 1, the reindeer pulling a sleigh in stages 25 and 26): its record starts with the distance and the records of what it pulls, closed by 0; starts each (handler 013AD8: traffic_stopping_car_update, kept live as long as the one before it is, which ends it) that distance behind the one before (its field 14 the one before, the same stop_distance, counted in screen traffic_count), then becomes a traffic_figure with the record after the list.
comment traffic_figure_update Traffic drawn from a single view (draw_mode 5) instead of by angle: the horses of the covered wagons (stage 1), the reindeer (stages 25, 26) and the figures of stages 10 and 24 (animation 185) and 30 (258, 071). By state: 0 reads its record as traffic_car_start does (draw_mode 4, scale_shift 2 and y_offset -2C0 for animation 165; scale_shift 0 for 258 and 185, else 1); 1 once the screen's car has reached the goal (car at_goal) ends when 8 segments behind the view; moves along the road by speed, slowing to a stop once stop_distance has run out (stopping); ends when traffic_out_of_view; while within the draw distance ahead of the view: traffic_hit_check when it has a hit box (traffic_start_hit_sound on a hit), draw_object and animation_advance.
comment stage_entry_update The start of a stage's road (position 0 of streams 1-30): when the car (car segment) reaches it, the stage the car is in becomes the one whose road is being built (screen current_stage_level = built_stage_level, its level; current_stage = built_stage, its number; car reached_stage_start = stage_start, the segment it starts at), and it ends. In the attract demo (screen demo_flags bit 8) it ends at once.
comment aurora_update The northern lights over stage 26 (stream 26 at 1040: two of them, with their own lists): drawn at a screen position (draw_mode 0) that slides in from x -160 to 160, 8 a frame (y 40, or 30 for car model 6, car model), then stays; it steps through its list of animations (aurora_next_animation, each shown animation_frames frames, aurora_count_down) and is drawn only every other frame (when countdown is odd), so it looks faint. It never ends.
comment aurora_count_down Counts the current animation's frames down; when they run out, aurora_next_animation.
comment aurora_next_animation Starts the next animation of the list (animation_list; a negative entry goes back that many bytes first) for animation_frames frames.
comment kangaroo_update The kangaroos of stage 21 (Australia) that hop across the road ahead of the car. By state: 0 reads the record (animation, hop_animation, palette, flags, side, x_offset, trigger_distance, x_speed, draw_mode, scale_shift), trigger_position = position - trigger_distance, appear_position = position - draw_distance; 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position; 2 drawn standing until the car (car segment) reaches trigger_position, then hop_animation; 3 while the car (car window_segment) has not reached it: x_offset += x_speed each frame and it moves along the road at the car's own pace (position_fraction += the car's speed, car speed, >> 9), animation_advance and draw_object; it ends when the car reaches it, when it gets beyond draw_distance or when its screen_x leaves -F0..130.
comment swooping_helicopter_update The helicopter over the waterfalls of stage 4: it swoops down, then flies off. By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, flags, draw_mode, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, trigger_distance + 16, x_speed, y_speed, road_speed, dive_frames, dive_y_speed), trigger_position = position - trigger_distance, appear_position = position - draw_distance; 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes trigger_position, then dive_countdown = dive_frames; 2 for dive_frames frames y_offset += dive_y_speed and position += road_speed; 3 x_offset += x_speed, y_offset += y_speed, position += road_speed; from 2 on, animation_advance and draw_object while within draw_distance of the car (car window_segment); it ends when the car reaches it, when it gets beyond draw_distance or when its screen_x leaves -F0..130.
comment start_gate_update The START banner over the start line and its two posts with the signal lights (stream 0 at 20: four of them). By state: 0 reads the record as road_row_start does (animation, palette, flags, draw_mode, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, spacing, count, hit box; first_position, end_position), and goes on to state 2 without a hit box; 1 road_row_crash_check and draw_object (no record has a hit box); 2 the lights: palette from start_gate_light_palettes by the seconds to the start (the car object's timer, in frames, rounded up): C9 until the last 3 seconds, then CC, CB, and CA at the start, which goes on to state 3; 3 draw_object. It never ends.
comment start_flagman_update The man at the start line who waves the chequered flag as the race starts (stream 0 at 20). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, go_animation, palette, side, x_offset, y_offset), draw_mode 9, scale_shift 1; 1 drawn standing (06E) while the car object's timer counts the frames to the start, then go_animation (06F, the waving); 1 and 2: ends once the car (car window_segment) reaches its position, else animation_advance and draw_object.
comment start_balloon_update The balloons let go over the start line as the race starts (stream 0 at 24-34). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, side, x_offset, y_offset), draw_mode 9, scale_shift 1; 1 not drawn until the start (the car object's timer down to 0); 1 and 2: it rises faster and faster (rise_speed + 1 each frame, y_offset - rise_speed / 2) and drifts (x_offset + 8), animation_advance and draw_object; it ends when the car (car window_segment) reaches its position or y_offset passes -3000 (high above).
comment ambient_sound_update A sound repeated at random along a stretch of road (the jungle of stage 3: sound 51): plays it, then again after 15 to 78 frames at random (random_number), until the car (car segment) has gone length segments from where it started.
comment one_shot_sound_update Plays sound 23 once and ends: placed at the start of stage 30's stream, so it sounds as the road of stage 30 starts being built.
comment west_east_panel_update The WEST BOUND / EAST BOUND panel at the bottom centre of the screen before the first fork (stream 0 at 550): animation 39 at a screen position (draw_mode A, layer 19D), its palette cycling through base_palette to base_palette + 3 (a step every 4 frames, colour_count). By state: 0 sets it up below the screen (y 118); 1 rises 2 a frame to y DC; 1 and 2: once the car (car segment) passes 250 or the race time runs out (word 6A of the race record screen host_record points at), 3: goes down 2 a frame and ends at y 118.
comment santa_sleigh_update Santa's sleigh and reindeer flying back and forth over the goal of stage 26, with sound 49 (sound) every 15 frames. By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, flags, side, x_offset, x_speed, sound, draw_mode, scale_shift; y_offset -100 for animation 3A0, -180 for 3A2), appear_position = position - draw_distance (and 58 = position - 100, never read); 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position; 2 moves by x_speed (leftward) until its screen_x is below -B0, then 3: mirrored (flags bit 6), moves back until screen_x is above F0, then 2 again; each frame animation_advance and draw_object. It never ends (the race ends at the goal).
comment goal_parade_rightward_update What runs back and forth in front of a goal's crowd, setting off rightward: a kangaroo (stage 21), a dragon dance (22), an orca leaping over the sea (28). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, flags, side, x_offset, x_speed, draw_mode, scale_shift; y_offset -180 for animation 3A2), appear_position = position - draw_distance (and 58 = position - 100, never read); 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position; 2 moves by x_speed (mirrored by the record's flags bit 6) until its screen_x passes B0, then 3: unmirrored, moves back until screen_x is below -F0, then 2 again; each frame animation_advance and draw_object. It never ends.
comment goal_parade_leftward_update What runs back and forth in front of a goal's crowd, setting off leftward: a kangaroo (stage 21), an orca (28), the bulls (29). As goal_parade_rightward_update, with the turns the other way: 2 moves by x_speed (negative) until its screen_x is below -B0, then 3: mirrored (flags bit 6), moves back until screen_x is above F0, then 2 again. It never ends.
comment goal_parade_dragon_update The dragon dance in front of the crowd at the goal of stage 22 (with goal_parade_rightward's second dragon): as goal_parade_leftward_update, plus sound 44 on one frame in four at random (random_number). It never ends.
comment sounding_scenery_update Animated scenery at a goal that makes a sound every second: the pandas at Beijing (stage 24, sound 47), the cheering group with banners in Japan (23, sound 4A), at the Matterhorn (27, sound 46). By state: 0 reads its record as road_row_start does (animation, palette, flags, size_table, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, spacing, count), with a sound where a hit box would be; 1 and 2: every 60 frames (sound_countdown) the sound, animation_advance and road_row_draw. (State 3, the same without the sound, is never reached.)
comment fork_branch_start_right Starts the other branch of the road off to the right (placed on the left branch's stream: the car took the left way): direction -1, then fork_branch_draw.
comment fork_branch_start_left Starts the other branch of the road off to the left (on the right branch's stream): direction 1, then fork_branch_draw.
comment fork_branch_draw Draws the branch of the road the car did not take, after a fork, as a strip of sprites curving away to one side (direction) along the 40 segments from position: for each segment in view (the road's per-segment records from the view, screen + 3000) the piece of road (fork_branch_sprite) goes at the road's screen x plus a curve growing with the distance from start_position (cosine_table) times the road's scale times direction, the pieces between segments filled in, into the screen's sprite list and its segment's depth list, until the strip leaves the screen. Ends once the view (race_copy window_segment) passes end_position.
comment fork_branch_sprite Builds the sprite words of the branch's road piece for fork_branch_draw: the first frame of animation 74 (its size and place in the sprite ROM, the size steps at 0x122B74) and the palette of the stage being built (fork_branch_stage_palettes by screen built_stage, into sprite_palettes).
comment stage_end_sounds_update The sounds as the car nears the end of a stage (before each fork's checkpoint and each goal): sound 24, then sound 53 20 frames later (the list at 01466F: a delay, then sounds and delays closed by 0); when the race record's stage count (race_copy stage_count) changes (the checkpoint or the goal passed), sound 24 if this screen's player leads the race (screen player = leader_player), else 28, and it ends.
comment fork_camera_update Raises and tilts the view before each fork (256 segments before it), so the fork is seen from above. By state: 0 waits until the car (race_copy segment) reaches its position; 1 each frame the view's pitch lift (car view_pitch_lift) up by lift_step to lift_height (1E0 less the car model's view_pitch_base, screen_view_defaults view_pitch_bases by race_copy model; screen nearing_fork = FF when it gets there: the radio's checkpoint message) and its shift lift (car view_shift_lift) down by tilt_step; 2 holds until the car reaches lower_position (224 segments on); 3 lowers it back the same way and ends. Does nothing while car crash_body_shown is set.
comment goal_actor_start Sets an actor of the goal scene to a screen position (draw_mode 0) and goes on to goal_actor_run.
comment goal_actor_run Plays the actor's script (script; step is where this frame's step began), a step a frame: a halfword of flags, from bit 15: wait (counter to the next halfword, drawn), repeat from step (until the counter passes the next halfword), animation_advance, frame =, x +=, x =, y +=, y =, scale += and = (34, 36), layer += and =, a new animation, palette =, a sound (request_sound); with bit 0 clear the step ends and the actor is drawn (draw_object); with bit 0 set a command (goal_actor_command_jumps): 0 tells the scene object it is over (its state = 2), 1 ends the actor, 2 ends the step undrawn, 3 flips it (flags bit 6), 4 car segment_progress + 4000, 5 a repeat whose count grows by 8 for each place behind (goal_actor_place_waits by screen + 320 + player).
comment goal_actor_car_start The player's car in the goal scene: its animation, palette and frame from the race record (race_copy animation, palette, frame), its layer, place and scale from the scene object (scene: the car's drawing object, 00974C's, whose handler is 014694 at the goal); then goal_actor_start.
comment goal_actor_driver_start The driver in the goal scene: goal_actor_rider_place with the scene object's first rider record (+90).
comment goal_actor_passenger_start The passenger in the goal scene: goal_actor_rider_place with the scene object's second rider record (+A0).
comment goal_actor_rider_place Places a rider in the scene's car: from the car's current frame (the car's animation and frame from race_copy animation, frame) the view it is drawn from (bit 6 of the frame's attributes picks the mirrored set) and the seat's offset; from the rider record (R12: a table of offsets by view, the animation of the first view, the palette, the frame) the rider's animation, palette and frame; scaled by the scene object's scale onto its place, in its layer; then goal_actor_start.
comment palette_cycle_update Cycles palettes of the stage's scenery (stage_background_object_lists): after delay frames, every step_frames frames queues the next palette set of its list (palette_list; a negative entry goes back that many bytes) with queue_palette_list, for frames_left frames (or for good when it is 0); it waits while the stage's background is changing (screen background_fading) and ends when it changes again (running).
comment sunset_fade_start Fades the colours of the stage toward evening after a while (stage_background_object_lists: Hawaii's sunset after 900 frames, stage 26's dusk after 300 and its night after 600): waits count frames (not while the background changes, screen background_fading), then starts a palette fade (start_palette_fade) for each entry of its list (fade_list: from, to, the palette, the speed), keeps them in fades and their number in count, and goes on to sunset_fade_wait.
comment sunset_fade_wait Waits for its palette fades to finish, then ends; if the stage's background starts changing (screen background_fading) it stops them and ends.
comment backdrop_near_start Sets up a sprite on the background a little nearer than the panorama (the hill of the Acropolis with the Parthenon, stages 14 and 19): from its data the animation, palette, panorama_x, height and half_width; draw_mode A, layer 1; straight to state 1 when the background is being set up (screen background_at_start); then backdrop_near_update.
comment backdrop_near_update Keeps the sprite on the background, moving with its panorama a quarter faster (nearer): x from the car's heading (car view_yaw, a quarter of it until screen background_changing is set) less panorama_x, y from the background's height (screen background_scroll_y) and height, but not below screen road_top_line's line. By state: 0 waits until it is off the screen (so it does not pop up in view); 1 draw_object while on the screen; off it, it ends once leaving.
comment backdrop_landmark_start Sets up a landmark on the horizon that grows as the car approaches (Tiananmen in stage 10, the castle of stages 16 and 17...): as backdrop_near_start, layer 2, and approach_start = the car's segment (car window_segment) less the distance in its data; then backdrop_landmark_update.
comment backdrop_landmark_update Keeps the landmark on the background's panorama (as backdrop_near_update, a quarter faster than the panorama) and grows it with the distance travelled: its scale (width_scale, height_scale) follows the sine of (car window_segment - approach_start) / 2 (sine_table) up to full size (100, state 2). By state: 0 waits until it is off the screen; 1 and 2 drawn while on the screen; off it, it ends once leaving.
comment backdrop_translucent_start Sets up a sprite on the background that is drawn every other frame, so it looks faint (the pyramids of stages 13 and 20, the spray of stage 4's falls): as backdrop_near_start, layer 1, frame_count 0; then backdrop_translucent_update.
comment backdrop_translucent_update Keeps the sprite on the background's panorama at the panorama's own speed (as backdrop_far_update), animates it and draws it only on even frames (frame_count), so it looks faint. By state: 0 waits until it is off the screen; 1 drawn while on the screen; off it, it ends once leaving.
comment backdrop_off_screen Whether the background sprite is wholly off the screen (zero set; it clears flags bit 5 then): its x (field 30) more than half_width beyond the screen's edges (0..140).

# The player's car's records and tables of the program ROM
# The race view's constants (player_car_update 006F38 "movea.b 10BC8, R2"): the address of the draw
# distances by the race's racer count (006F3F "mov.w [R2+], R0", 010BE2: 2400 3000 3000 2800 2800 2400...;
# 006F52 "mov.h [R0](R1), 12[R25]" -> screen draw_distance), eye_distance, sprite_origin_x, sprite_origin_y
# (006F58-006F62 "mov.h [R2+], 10/14/16[R25]": C0 A0 DE) and view_pitch_base by the car's model (006F6B
# "mov.h [R2](R0), 18[R25]": E8 F0 110 110 110 100 190 140)
# The grid: by the race's racer count less 1 (none in the demo), the segments each place starts ahead
# (006FB7 "mov.w 91CC[PC](R2), R3", 006FBF "add.h [R3](R1), R0" by the place) and its place across the road
# with what is left of the segment (006FD3 "mov.w 90AC[PC](R2), R3", 006FDB "mov.w [R3](R1), R1" -> car
# road_x and segment_remaining: FFFFFF50, 000000B0 for two racers)
elements grid_segments u16
elements grid_road_places u32
0091CC  table grid_segment_lists u32 grid_segments
0090AC  table grid_road_place_lists u32 grid_road_places
# Where a car stops after the goal, by its place in the race (player_car_update 00726F "mov.w
# 9540[PC](R0), R0", then 007277 "add.h [R0+], F0[R20]" to stop_position, "mov.h [R0+], F2[R20]" stop_x,
# "mov.h [R0+], F4[R20]" stop_speed): 6 bytes a place from 009560 (17C 0 C000, 178 -B0 B800...)
struct goal_stop 6 rom

field goal_stop 02 x s16
field goal_stop 04 speed u16
009540  table goal_stops u32 goal_stop
# The crash physics of a model (player_car_load_crash_physics 007CF5 "mov.w 804A(R0), R10" by the model,
# 007CFD-007D0F "mov.h [R10+], 126/12C/128/12A[R23]": car bounce_damping, speed_change_decay, push_decay,
# gravity), its crash table right after it (007D15 "mov.w R10, 130[R23]": car crash_table): 0x208 bytes a
# model from 00806A
struct crash_physics 8 rom

field crash_physics 04 push_decay u16
field crash_physics 06 gravity u16
00804A  table car_crash_physics u32 crash_physics
# Handling of a model (player_car_load_gears 00A010 "mov.w ADCC(R0), R12" by the model -> car handling;
# 28 bytes a model from 00ADEC): the steering by the wheel (player_car_drive 00A21F "movs.bw [0[R12]](R0),
# R0" by the wheel >> 3: 32 signed bytes, 1A at one end, E6 at the other), the drag factor off the road
# (player_car_road_drag 00B58E "mulu.w 8[R12], R0": 5A), the force that starts a wheelspin and the one that
# ends it (player_car_engine 00A9D3 "cmp.w C[R12], R9", 00A883 "cmp.w 10[R12], R9": 1C0, 1A0), the drive
# force while the wheels spin (00A8F8 "mov.w 14[R12], R9": 120), the car's half width on the road
# (player_car_drive 00A17A "movz.hw 1A[R12], R3": 2C), the brake's factor (00A1CB "mulu.w 1C[R12], R9":
# -190), the grip (00A2B1 "mov.w 20[R12], R2", a signed compare: 12C000 for the first model) and the
# slide's factor past it (00A2D0 "mulu.w 24[R12], R1": 25)
elements wheel_steering s8
struct car_handling 28 rom
field car_handling 00 steering u32 wheel_steering
field car_handling 08 off_road_drag u32
field car_handling 0C spin_start_force s32
field car_handling 10 spin_end_force s32
field car_handling 14 spin_drive_force s32
field car_handling 1A half_width u16
field car_handling 1C brake_factor s32
field car_handling 20 grip s32
field car_handling 24 slide_factor s32
00ADCC  table car_handlings u32 car_handling
# A model's gears, by model * 2 + transmission (player_car_load_gears 00A02A "mov.w B04C[PC](R1), R1"):
# the top gear, then a ratio for each gear, the first's here (car top_gear, gear_ratio; car gear_ratios points at it:
# 00A032-00A037): 00B08C 00 F8 for an automatic, 00B08E 05 6A 92 BA DD FD FF for a manual
struct gear_list 2 rom
field gear_list 00 top_gear u8
field gear_list 01 first_ratio u8
00B04C  table car_gear_lists u32 gear_list
# A point of a gear's torque curve (player_car_build_torque_curves 00A05D-00A066 "movz.hw [R11+]/[R12+]":
# the speed and the force; a gear's points end with FFFF FFFF, 00A08B "test.h [R12]", "bp"), the curves of
# a model by model * 2 + transmission (player_car_load_gears 00A020 "mov.w B0C0[PC](R1), 50[R23]" -> car
# torque_curves: 00B100 0 4800, 30 4800, 60 4800...)
struct torque_point 4 rom

field torque_point 02 force u16
00B0C0  table car_torque_curves u32 torque_point
# A stretch of a roadside surface (player_car_road_drag: 00B55E "movz.hw [R13], R6" where it ends, from the
# stage's start, FFFF to its end; 00B5E1-00B5ED "mov.h 2[R13]/4[R13]" its inner and outer edges across the
# road; wheel_effect_* 00B666 "mov.b 6[R13], 141[R23]" and 00B674 "mov.b 7[R13]" the wheel effect inside and
# outside them; 00B569 "add.w #8, R13" the next): the two lists of a stage, one for each side
# (player_car_next_surfaces 00B51A "movea.w B78C[PC](R7), R7" by stage * 2, 00B522 "mov.w [R7+],
# 148[R23]", 00B528 "mov.w [R7+], 14C[R23]": 00B88C 00B894 for stage 0)

field roadside_surface 04 outer_edge u16

field roadside_surface 07 outer_effect u8

struct sprite_animation 4 rom
field sprite_animation 00 frame_time u8           # animation_start (00F372 "mov.b [R9], R9") -> object 41, 42
field sprite_animation 04 frames s32[]           # the frames' entries: 00EF66 "mov.w 4[R12](R26), R12" (by object 40); animation_advance 00F335-00F361 tests them (0 hold, -1 back to frame 0, -2 the animation the next entry names)

034138  table  sprite_animations  sprite_animation_pointer  # by object 38: animation_start (00F36A "mov.w 34138(R9), R9"), put_object_sprites (00EF5A), animation_advance, the road rows
struct sprite_frame 2 rom
field sprite_frame 00 piece_count u16             # put_object_sprites 00EF9C "movz.hw [R12+], R11" (dbr's count), draw_object_smoothed 00F21C; draw_object_one_piece and the road rows skip it (00F082 "add.w #2, R12")
struct sprite_piece 8 rom
field sprite_piece 00 attributes u16              # sprite word 0, xor-ed with flags bits 6-7 (00EF9F "mov.h [R12+], R0; xor.h R19, R0")
field sprite_piece 02 sprite u16                  # its first size step at 122B74 + 4 * it (00EFAD "add.h [R12+], R26"; all 26323 pieces of the census a multiple of 3)
field sprite_piece 04 y_offset s16                # 00EFE9 "movs.hw [R12+], R4": times the height and the flip's sign, + y
field sprite_piece 06 x_offset s16                # 00EFF6 "movs.hw [R12+], R5": times the width and the flip's sign, + x
struct sprite_size_step 0C rom
field sprite_size_step 00 source_size u16         # 00EFB8 "mov.h [R14+], R1": sprite word 1
field sprite_size_step 02 rom_offset u16          # 00EFBB "mov.h [R14+], R6": sprite word 6
field sprite_size_step 04 height u16              # 00EFC1 "movz.hw [R14+], R2": times the height scale >> 8 & 3FF, sprite word 2
field sprite_size_step 06 height_bits u16         # 00EFD0 "or.h [R14+], R2"
field sprite_size_step 08 width u16               # 00EFD3 "movz.hw [R14+], R3": times the width scale >> 8 & 3FF, sprite word 3
field sprite_size_step 0A width_bits u16          # 00EFE2 "or.h [R14+], R3"
elements sprite_palette_words u16                 # a palette's words, one a piece (put_object_sprites 00EFBE "mov.h [R13+], R7")
elements sprite_palette_pointer u32 sprite_palette_words

# What finish_sprite_list switches to, by screen and buffer (sprite_list_switches: 00F3A8 "mov.w F508[PC](R9), R19")
struct sprite_list_switch 14 rom
field sprite_list_switch 00 list u32              # the list just finished: its start entry
field sprite_list_switch 04 end_word u16          # the word that ends its chain (8003, 8004: a jump to entry 3, or the end)
field sprite_list_switch 06 entry_2_jump u16      # -> sprite RAM 400020
field sprite_list_switch 08 entry_3_jump u16      # -> sprite RAM 400030
field sprite_list_switch 0A next_buffers u16      # -> sprite_list_buffers
field sprite_list_switch 0C next_list u32         # -> screen sprite_list_last (40A000, 404000, ...)
field sprite_list_switch 10 next_limit u32        # -> screen sprite_list_limit
elements sprite_list_switch_pointer u32 sprite_list_switch
00F508  table  sprite_list_switches  sprite_list_switch_pointer  # F528 F53C F550 F564 F578 F58C F5A0 F5B4: 14 bytes apart
# The hit box of a road item (road_row: its record's 8 bytes at 10; traffic: its sprite record's last 8)
struct item_hit_box 8 rom
field item_hit_box 00 along_start u16             # road_row_hit_check 01236F "add.h [R10+], R6": along the road, from the item
field item_hit_box 02 along_end u16               # 012372 "add.h [R10+], R7"
field item_hit_box 04 across_start u16            # 012375 "add.h [R10+], R8": across, from the item's place
field item_hit_box 06 across_end u16              # 012378 "add.h [R10+], R9"
# The course: stage_courses (03CC00, by stage) holds each stage's stream of commands (car course)
elements course_pointer u32
03CC00  table  stage_courses  course_pointer      # player_car_start_course (00FCD2 "mov.w 3CC00(R0), R11") by screen first_stage; init_screen walks the 31 (013D6F "mov.w [R10+], R12")
# A fork's command (-2) is followed by the stages of its two branches and their courses
struct course_fork 0A rom
field course_fork 00 branch_stages u8[2]          # player_car_extend_course 00FFCF "movz.bw [R11](R0), R1" (R0 the branch) -> screen route_stage
field course_fork 02 branch_courses u32[2]        # 00FFD3 "mov.w 2[R11](R0), A0[R23]" -> car course
local player_car_extend_course 00FFBF R11 course course_fork
local player_car_start_course 00FCD2 R11 course course_start
elements road_projection_pattern u8               # player_car_project_road 010150 "test.b [AP+]": a byte a segment out from the window, 1 projects it, 0 copies the one before
local player_car_project_road 01013C R29 projection_pattern road_projection_pattern
local player_car_project_road 010150 t1 projected
# note_road_crest takes the last segment's height and depth layer in R27 and R28, which the function sets
# when it projects a segment (01015F, 010166); only a pattern that copied every segment out to the view's
# distance would hand it what R27 and R28 held when the function started, and segment_projection_pattern
# starts with a 1
unread player_car_project_road R27
unread player_car_project_road R28
100000  table  blank_sprite_places  u32           # road_row_draw (00F8E6 "mov.w 100000, R26", 4 times into the next sprite places), road_row_pair_draw (00FC97); 00F61E "movea.b 100000, R28" when nothing was drawn the frame before: 16-byte places 00000000 F800F800 ..., this one's first word 0

elements road_row_size_steps u8

103400  table  arctangents  u16                   # vector_angle (0120E6 "movz.hw 103400(R0), R0") by 400 * the smaller / the larger: the angle (0-200)
0121D8  table  octant_base_angles  u16            # vector_angle (0120F6 "add.h 121D8[PC](R4), R0") by the octant (bits: 1 x < 0, 2 y < 0, 4 |x| < |y|): 400 C00 400 C00 0 0 800 800
0121E8  table  octant_angle_factors  u16          # vector_angle (0120EE "mulu.h 121E8[PC](R4), R0"): FFFF 1 1 FFFF 1 FFFF FFFF 1, the arctangent added or taken off
# The screens' set-up (init_screen, init_video, init_sprite_chip, init_mixer) and their fades

# The screens' view at the start: init_screen (01065E "movea.b 10BC8[PC], R2", then [R2+]) and player_car_update (its first state: 10BC8 and 10BCC, by isdn_mode and the car model)

elements text_font_rows u8                        # a character's 8 rows of 8 one-bit pixels

012238  func  player_car_build_hit_boxes

014108  func  race_car_occupant_update

01442E  table race_car_rider_palettes  u16   # the riders' palettes, by occupant or rider (model, model + 8): race_car_occupant_update (014134) and rider_gesture_update (014229) "mov.h 1442E[PC](R0), 3A[R20]"; census ROM 01442E-01444D (00A6-00B5)
014694  func  race_car_start_ending
comment player_car_update The screen's car, each frame: player_car_check_place, player_car_read_controls, player_car_build_hit_boxes, then by state: 0 sets up the race (the view's constants in screen eye_distance-18, the course, the car on its grid place: segment 16 + grid_segment_lists by players and place, road_x from grid_road_place_lists, its world position; the palette of its model, player_car_palettes; timer 8 frames in the demo, 149 in a race with the intro view car view_shift_intro/view_pitch_intro; the gears, torque curves, crash physics, with screen is_host the race's time left (race time_left: 40 seconds plus master_time_difficulty), the engine sound); 1 the countdown to the start: brake full and the wheel centred until timer runs out (others read it as the frames to the start), then car moving and race started; 2 driving (player_car_move): goes to 3 when the time is up (car time_up), to 4 at the goal (car at_goal); counts hit_guard down; on a hit (flags bit 2, by hit_kind): 3 a fork sign slows it for 15 frames (car slowed_frames), 0 scenery, 1 a road object (hit_other), 2 the other player's car (hit_other): a speed change, a push and a bounce from the model's crash table (car crash_table, by impact_speed or the cars' speeds and places), the hit's sounds, a crash animation, the CRASH text (car crash_text), immune (flags bit 3), state 5 and its first frame; 5 the crash plays out (player_car_crash_step); 3 time up: gas off and the model's brake (player_car_time_up_brakes), 180 frames, then the race is over (car race_over), GAME OVER (004771) with one player or race_result_start with two, 60 frames, screen flags bit 4 and DJ message 36 or 37; 4 the goal: finished, the race's stage count up, waits for the screen's finish time (screen 410) into race finish_time and the race time, race finished, then drives itself (player_car_steer_to_stop) to its stop_position by its place (goal_stops), plays the model's arrival animation (player_car_arrival_animations), brakes to a stop, 90 frames, screen flags bit 4.
comment player_car_crash_step The crash playing out, each frame of player_car_update's state 5 and the frame a hit starts it: at the goal (car at_goal) back to 2 at once; else the wheel motor at 10, the hit's sound once its delay (car crash_sound_delay) runs out, no controls, the push and the speed change dying away (car push_decay, speed_change_decay), a bounce under gravity (car gravity, bounce_damping; sound 31 on landing), and back to 2 with 15 frames of hit_guard when the crash animation ends (frame_time 0).
0146A0  table race_car_ending_state_jumps  u32   # race_car_start_ending (01469D "jmp [R0]") jumps through it by state: 0146B0 01470D 01470E 01470D
comment player_car_move One frame of the car on the road: player_car_drive, player_car_place_view, player_car_move_view, player_car_set_horizon, player_car_publish, player_car_extend_course, player_car_project_road, player_car_engine_sound and player_car_animate; no wheel effects while it crashes (state 5).
comment player_car_steer_to_stop Drives the car by itself after the goal: gas 40, no brake, the speed (car speed) a 16th of the way to stop_speed each frame, and the wheel turned step by step toward stop_x across the road (centred within 8 of it; stop_wheel keeps it).
comment player_car_check_place Keeps the race order around the screen's car: screen leader_record-6C the race records (leader_player to behind_player the players) of the leader, the last, the car just ahead and the car just behind (all itself in the demo), screen leader_window_segment and leader_stage the leader's race window_segment and stage. When its place (screen 320 + player, kept in place) changes during the race: sound 37 when the car it passed or that passed it is within 160 across, else 3A. While first, blinks one of the screen's lamps (the count, period and timer bytes C9-CB of its entry at checkpoint_lamp).
comment player_car_publish Hands the car's state on: this frame's view into the screen's ring of eight (screen + 600, slot view_ring_slot, also race view_slot) and the view of the slot race_copy view_slot names back into the car and screen shown_view, so that both screens draw the view of the same frame; and the car's world position, animation, palette, frame, wheel effects, speed, sideways speed, flags, segments, place across the road and bounce into its race record (race published set), from which race_car draws it on both screens. Not in ISDN mode (isdn_mode), which reads them back instead.
comment player_car_set_horizon Works out the horizon's line on the screen (screen horizon_line) from the view's tilt: the nod (car view_pitch_nod), the rival's and the fork camera's pulls (view_pitch_rival, view_pitch_lift), the car model's base pitch (screen view_pitch_base), clamped to C0-200, and the intro's (view_pitch_intro).
comment player_car_place_on_road Puts the car on its starting segment at the start: no way into the segment, road_x from the grid's place, its world position (player_car_world_position) and the view behind it (player_car_place_view).
comment player_car_clear_hit Ends what a hit was doing to the car: no sideways_push, speed_change, bounce, no hit pending (flags bit 2), and the car no longer skidding (car skidding) or slipping (wheel_slip).
comment player_car_animate Picks the car's animation: while a hit is pending, the one it has goes on; skidding (car skidding), a skid by how far it slides (slide) toward the side the wheel is turned (player_car_skid_animations_wheel_high, player_car_skid_animations_wheel_low by model); spinning its wheels while it speeds up (car wheel_slip, acceleration above 0), the model's wheelspin (player_car_wheelspin_animations); else its body turned by the steering (player_car_steer_frame). Each one in the row of the view's height (player_car_view_height); a new one starts at frame 0.
comment player_car_steer_frame The car's body as it steers: the model's animation (player_car_steer_animations) in the row of the view's height, its frame (steer_frame) one step a frame toward the one of the steering (steer_frames_by_steering, from its middle: 0 full left to 8 full right, 4 straight on).
comment player_car_view_height The row of the car's animations for how steeply the view looks down on it: the view's pitch (car view_pitch) less B0 and less the road's pitch under the car (race_copy segment_pitch), in steps of 30, 0 to 5 (view_height).
comment player_car_move_view Moves the view with what the car does: when a rival comes close (player_car_check_rivals, rival_step) it starts the RIVAL SEKKIN warning (rival_warning) and pulls the view back and up (car view_shift_rival to -256, view_pitch_rival +8 a step) until the rival has gone, then lets it back; a speed gain of more than A0 in a frame without wheelspin sets car hard_acceleration (hard acceleration); the view nods (nod, car view_shift_nod and view_pitch_nod) one way with that hard acceleration and the other with hard braking (more than 120 a frame with the brake down), easing back; and it lags the car's rises and drops (car view_rise).
comment player_car_check_rivals Whether a rival is close (rival_near): at speed 6000 or more (5000 to stay on), the car just ahead (screen ahead_record) a little slower and within reach, or the car just behind (behind_record) a little faster and close; once on it stays for 30 frames (rival_hold) after the rival has gone.
comment player_car_load_crash_physics Loads the crash physics of the car's model (car_crash_physics): bounce_damping, speed_change_decay, push_decay, gravity and the address of its crash table.
comment player_car_load_gears Loads the car's model and transmission: its handling record (car handling), its torque curves (torque_curves), its gear ratios (gear_ratios) and top gear (top_gear), the first gear's ratio (gear_ratio), and the roadside surfaces of the first stage (player_car_next_surfaces).
comment player_car_build_torque_curves Builds the drive force of each gear by speed at screen + 1300 (256 values a gear), interpolating the model's points (car torque_curves).
comment player_car_read_controls Takes the controls: gas, brake and wheel from the screen's pedals and wheel (screen gas, brake, wheel). In the demo (screen demo_flags bit 8) the car drives itself: no brake, the gas up to F0 (eased off while skidding), the wheel back toward the middle when off the road or slowed, else against its sideways drift.
comment player_car_drive Moves the car for the frame: the drag (player_car_road_drag), the slope of its segment (race segment_pitch), the brake (no gas while braking; brake lights car brake_lights) and the gas through the gear's torque curve go to player_car_engine (speed, revs); the wheel steers through the model's table (less at low speed) into steering; the road's turn under it (previous_heading less heading) pushes it out (less with catch-up, car catch_up); beyond the model's grip it skids (car skidding, slide, tyre noise 3, smoke on both sides); sideways_speed and sideways_push move it across (car road_x, within the road's half width + 60); the speed along (less its sideways angle, vector_angle) plus a catch-up bonus by the distance to the car ahead (car catch_up_target, catch_up; not once finished) moves it along, passing segments (car window_segment, segment, segment_offset, window_offset, segment_progress, segment_fraction); then player_car_world_position. In ISDN mode (isdn_mode) the race record the link brings in gives all this.
comment player_car_world_position Places the car in the world from its segment: world_x and world_z from the segment's position and the car's place across and along it turned by the road's heading and pitch (rotate_offset), world_y the road's height there plus bounce_height (the old one to previous_world_y); the rest of the segment (car segment_remaining) and the road's heading under the car (heading, the old one to previous_heading).
comment player_car_place_view Places the view behind the car: a point on the road some way back of the car (the nod, the intro, the rival and road-object pulls: car view_shift_nod, view_shift_intro, view_shift_rival, view_shift_lift) and across (view_side) and above (view_rise) it (view_target_x/y/z), looking toward the road twelve segments on (vector_angles: car view_yaw the yaw, road_pitch the road's pitch; car view_pitch the pitch with the view's tilt), the eye screen eye_distance back from the target along those angles (angles_to_offset) in car view_x-28.
comment player_car_engine The engine and the speed: the force (brake, slope and drag, drive; acceleration) changes the speed (car speed, also impact_speed), with any speed_change from a hit, up to the top speed of the gear (its ratio, car gear_ratio); a force past the model's grip spins the wheels, and hard braking locks them (car wheel_slip: tyre noise 1 or the model's skid noise, smoke; player_car_engine_wheelspin), the rest has grip (player_car_engine_grip); the revs (car revs) follow the speed through the gear's ratio, or the gas when the car stands; car moving says it moves.
comment player_car_engine_wheelspin The speed and the revs while the wheels spin or lock (player_car_engine goes on to it from both of its sides): the speed (car speed, with any speed_change from a hit, not below 0) goes half the way toward itself plus the force (not below 0), or a thirtieth of the way down to the gear's top speed (at most FFFF), into car speed and impact_speed; standing, player_car_engine_standing; moving, car moving FF and the revs a tenth of the way toward the speed * 256 by the gear's ratio (at most FFFF).
comment player_car_engine_grip The speed and the revs with grip (player_car_engine goes on to it from both of its sides): car wheel_slip 0, the speed as player_car_engine_wheelspin works it out; standing, player_car_engine_standing; moving, car moving FF and the revs toward the speed * 256 by the gear's ratio (player_car_engine_revs).
comment player_car_engine_standing The car standing (speed 0): car moving 0 and the revs toward the gas * 256 (player_car_engine_revs).
comment player_car_engine_revs Moves the revs (car revs) a third of the way toward target_revs, at most FFFF.
comment player_car_next_surfaces Takes the roadside surfaces of the stage (screen built_stage): the two lists of stage_roadside_surface_lists for it (car surface_entry_1, surface_entry_2), from the segment where the stage starts (car surface_start) to the next one's (surface_next_start).
comment player_car_road_drag The drag on the car: speed squared, times the model's off-road factor while slowed (car slowed_frames) or off the road (car off_road, with the off-road shake); and the roadside surfaces under its wheels (car surface_entry_1, surface_entry_2) give the effects at each side (car wheel_effect_sides: bit 0 with wheel_effect_1, bit 1 with wheel_effect_2).
comment player_car_off_road_shake Off the road: the model's shake to the screen's bits of output port D (wheel_motor, the wheel motor's; car_model_off_road_shakes) and its off-road noise (car_model_off_road_noises).
comment player_car_start_course Starts the race's course (stage_courses by screen first_stage, car course): clears the ring of segments, the first road width, and builds the segments as far as the view reaches (screen draw_distance >> 7, prebuilt_segments); the first stage into the stage history.
comment player_car_extend_course Keeps the course built 104 segments past the window's start (window_record, build_end_offset). At a fork the building waits (at_fork) until the checkpoint is passed (screen forks_decided): then the branch taken (screen 349 + the fork) goes on: branch, the course of that branch (car course), its stage (screen route_stage, stage indicator A5), the stage and branch histories and forks_taken, the stage's data queued (queue_stage_load) and the branch_arrow.
comment player_car_build_segment Builds the next segment of the road from the course (car course): at the end of a run reads the next records: 0 the course ends (the car's state 3), 1 a jump to another part, 2 a fork (forks_reached, the race's stage count, at_fork), 3 and 5 the pitch and the heading back to 0, 4 a new stage (screen built_stage_level, built_stage; stage_segments, stage_start), 6 the goal (a goal object at this segment), else a run (its length, pitch and heading steps, road width, a byte for the segments). The segment: heading and pitch turned by the run's steps, the position moved along them (next_x/y/z), the width eased toward the run's; written at build_offset, then built_segments and stage_segments count up.
comment player_car_project_road Projects the road for the view of screen shown_view (player_car_publish): its rotation (view_matrix_*, from the view's pitch and yaw), then each segment from the window's start out to the view's distance turned into the view and scaled by its depth into the screen's list at screen + 3000 (scale, width, x, y, the segment's byte), segments behind the view or too far left out.
comment player_car_start_engine_sound Starts the car's engine sound on the screen's channel of the sound board: the model's engine sample (car_model_engine_samples) and the engine's loudness by the transmission (10, or 30, 6 dB louder, with the transmission's value set), then the revs on (1) and the requests for the sample and the pitch (A0); silent in the demo without sound.
comment player_car_engine_sound Sets the engine's pitch from the revs (car revs) each frame, or silences it in the demo without sound.
comment player_car_build_hit_boxes Builds the hit boxes of the race's cars at screen + 510: how many, then for each its player, race record and box (player_car_write_hit_box from its segment, model, place along and across; screen own_hit_box the screen's own), and the screen's car's box from its own fields at screen + 5B0.
comment player_car_write_hit_box Writes a car's hit box: its record, its first and last segment (4 on), and its box along and across from the model's size (car_hit_box_sizes).
comment player_car_check_car_hits Whether the car hits another car of the race (the boxes of player_car_build_hit_boxes; not while either is immune, flags bit 3): a hit of kind 2 with that car's player in hit_other.
comment vector_angle Returns the 12-bit angle of the vector (R0 - R2, R1 - R3). Used by player_car_drive.
comment vector_angles Returns the angles from a point (R3, R4, R5: x, y, z) toward another (R0, R1, R2): the pitch in R0, the yaw in R1. Used by player_car_place_view.
comment rotate_offset Turns an offset (R1 across, R2 along, R0 up) by a heading (R3) and a pitch (R4) into the world's axes. Used by player_car_world_position and player_car_place_view.
comment angles_to_offset Returns the offset of length R2 along pitch R0 and yaw R1 (R3, R4, R5: x, y, z). Used by player_car_place_view for the eye behind the view's target.
comment set_tyre_noise Sets the noise of the screen's car's tyres on the sound board (byte 718 of the screen's page; the sound program keeps it while the game keeps writing it and keys it off 255 of its main loop's passes after the last): wheelspin, skids, off-road rumble. In the demo without sound it mutes the engine instead. Used by player_car (player_car_engine, player_car_off_road_shake, the goal) and 00664E.
comment set_tyre_noise_on_road set_tyre_noise, unless the car is off the road (car off_road), where the off-road noise plays. Used by player_car_drive, player_car_engine and gear_indicator_update.
comment queue_stage_load Queues the palette of the stage the screen goes to (scene_palettes by screen route_stage) through queue_palette (the palette queue at screen + 900). Used by player_car_extend_course and the game's code (002E74).
comment rev_meter_update The rev meter, the bar left of the speed at the bottom right: state 0 sets it up (animation 6, at F2, D6), 1 draws the frame of the revs (car revs >> 11, 32 steps).
comment speedometer_update The speed in km/h at the bottom right: state 0 sets it up (digits of animation 12, units_x, digits_y) and clears the screen's maximum (maximum_speed), sum (speed_sum) and count (speed_samples); 1 each frame the car's speed / 88 into screen shown_speed, and while moving adds it to the sum, counts it and keeps the maximum (for the LCD's pages), then draws its digits from the units leftwards.
comment speed_unit_update The km/h after the speed: state 0 sets it up (animation 10 at 127, CE), 1 draws it.
comment gear_indicator_update The gear at the bottom right, and the shifting: state 0 sets it up (gear_indicator_animations by the top gear: AUTO for the automatic). 1 each frame: the shift buttons (screen buttons_pressed bits 4 up, 5 down) change the gear within 0 and the top gear; shifting up at revs F000 or more, or down into revs past FFFF, spins the wheels 30 frames (car shift_spin, tyre noise 1); the gear's ratio to car gear_ratio, its frame drawn. Below the top gear with the revs at the limit (FF00) and the car moving, after 150 frames starts the shift_up_hint once. Once the race is over (car at_goal or race_over), 2 only draws.
comment shift_up_hint_update A hint to shift up, blinking at the bottom centre (animation E): state 0 sets it up; 1 blinks it (4 frames of 8) while the car stays in the gear it started in with the revs at FC00 or more, then ends and lets the gear indicator start it again.
comment place_count_update The number of cars after the place at the bottom left (/2): ends at once with one player; state 0 sets it up (animation 5, frame the players less one) and starts the place_number; 1 draws it, and when the screen's place (screen place) changes passes the new one to the place_number (its place, state 2).
comment place_number_update The screen's place, 1st or 2nd, at the bottom left: state 0 sets it up (animation 4, the place's frame and colour); 1 draws it; 2 a new place: twice the size and blinking for 40 frames, 4 shrinking back into place in 8, then 5 draws it.
comment lap_timer_update The lap time after LAP at the top right: state 0 sets it up (the label, the text record and the checkpoints passed); 1 shows the race time since the last checkpoint (car race_time / 10 less car checkpoint_time, time_to_text); when the screen's car passes a checkpoint (screen 480 + player) the time it took (screen 3C0 records) starts the next lap (car checkpoint_time) and stays shown in another colour for 180 frames (split_frames; state 2), and C00074, which the board does not map, gets 8 + 2 * screen + (1 - the stage's parity); after the last (lap_timer_checkpoint_states) 3 keeps the last lap's time.
comment start_message_update The start's messages in the middle of the screen: state 0 shows YOU'RE IN CAR NUMBER n! (animation 28F + the screen's car number, screen car_number, with DJ message 21 + it); when the start's countdown (the car object's timer) is at 30 frames, Ready (animation 31, DJ message 31); at 0, Go! (animation 32, DJ message 32), 30 frames, then it ends.
comment time_left_display_update The time left in seconds at the top centre, and the time up: state 0 waits for the race's time (host_record's time_left), takes it into car time_left and sets the digits up (time_digits_start); 1 shows it during the start's countdown; 2 follows the race's time (until the screen's car has finished, race_copy finished) and starts EXTEND TIME at each checkpoint (time_left_display_check_extension); at 0 the time is up (car time_up) and 3 shows 0, until time comes back (the car drives on: its state 2). The digits flip as they change (time_digits_draw).
comment time_left_display_check_extension Starts the EXTEND TIME message once for each checkpoint passed (screen forks_decided, counted in car extensions_shown).
comment time_digits_start Sets up the three digits of the time at the top centre (animation 3 at A9, 11), each to open from nothing at its first value. Used by time_left_display, car_select_run and 001EC2.
comment time_digits_draw Draws the three digits of R0 (binary-coded decimal), the units at the right: a digit that changes squeezes shut and opens again as the new one; leading zeros are left out.
comment extend_time_message_start Sets up the EXTEND TIME message under the time (animation 33) for 90 frames, blinks one of the screen's cabinet lamps 16 times (bytes C1-C3 of its entry at checkpoint_lamp), then extend_time_message_blink.
comment extend_time_message_blink Blinks EXTEND TIME (4 frames on, 4 off) until its frames run out.
comment goal_update The goal line and what follows it: state 0 waits until the screen's car reaches its segment (position), then marks the car at the goal (car at_goal); 1 when the car's finish is recorded (race finished) sets up Congratulations! (animation 2F5) and the TOTAL TIME (the text at D08E and the screen's finish time, screen 410, through time_to_text), DJ message 34 when first, else 35, the screen's sound byte to 7F; 2 waits; 3 after 120 frames draws them for 60, then the title Dream Flying (start_song_title) and sound 10; 4 240 more frames of both, or with two players 5 only the TOTAL TIME beside the race_result; then it ends.
comment race_result_start With two players, the result at the end of the race: the screen's place in big letters (animation 2F8: 1st, 2nd) at the bottom for 240 frames (race_result_hold), and the verdict above it (race_result_verdict_start).
comment race_result_hold Draws the place or Loser until its frames run out.
comment race_result_verdict_start The verdict over the place: Winner (animation 2F6), blinking (race_result_verdict_blink), for the first, Loser (2F7) for the other, 240 frames.
comment race_result_verdict_blink Blinks Winner (4 frames on, 4 off) until its frames run out.
comment branch_arrow_update The arrow of the way taken at a fork, at the bottom centre, with CHECK POINT scrolling on the LCD between arrows of that way: state 0 sets it up (animation 34 or 35 by the branch, car branch; DJ message 33 when first, else 3A, unless one is playing) for 57 segments of the race (leave_segment); 1 cycles its colours (palette_list); 2 then grows and drops out of the screen. Ends when the race is over.
comment checkpoint_board_update The times at a checkpoint, in rows under the top line: state 0 takes each player's car image (checkpoint_board_car_sprites by the car chosen); 1 waits until the screen's car passes a checkpoint (screen 480 + player); 2 then shows a row for each car that has passed it, in the order they did (screen 390): the first with its time, the others with how far behind the first they were (BEHIND); each row slides in from the right, the screen's own blinks while it waits for the others, and all slide out once the race has gone C0 segments on (leave_segment) or is over; when the last has gone, back to 1.
comment checkpoint_board_draw_row Draws row of the board for player R21, slid by its offset: the car number, the car's small image, the time text (draw_text_at; another colour after the first row) and, after the first, BEHIND.
comment checkpoint_board_leader_time Writes the first car's time R0 as M'SS"HH into player R1's row text.
comment checkpoint_board_gap_time Writes how far behind the first player R1 passed (R0, hundredths) into its row text, as time_to_text does but with spaces for zero minutes and zero tens of seconds.
comment time_to_text Writes the time R0 (hundredths of a second) as M'SS"HH, closed by 0, at R10. Used by lap_timer, goal, checkpoint_board, 001EC2 and 00270C.
comment race_cars_start Starts the race's cars: for each player of the race, a race_car with its race record and the tables of its model (body, driver_places, passenger_places, sprays, tag), the two race_car_occupant objects and two rider_gesture objects that follow it; then ends.
comment race_car_occupant_update One of the two people in a race car (race_car_occupant_animations, race_car_rider_palettes by occupant), seen only while the car shows an animation of its own (its special_body: a crash): then drawn at the car's place and size until its animation ends, when the second one lets the car go back to its body.
comment race_car_draw Draws a car of the race, the screen's own or a rival, from its race record (record), as the screen's view sees it: its world position less the view's through the view's matrix and scaled by its depth (off the screen, too near or too far: not drawn); its body (animation, frame, palette from the record, plus the screen's brake lights and wheel_turn), its driver and passenger (driver_places, passenger_places), the sprays at its wheels (race_car_draw_spray), five frames of animation 44 on its hard acceleration (record hard_acceleration, car hard_acceleration); over the screen's own car the CRASH (or Ouch!) text after a hit, over a rival its number tag, blinking. A rival behind the window of the road, or off the screen, is race_car_draw_behind's. Its sprites go into the list of their depth layer (race_car_draw_link). A body animation without wheels (race_car_body_wheel_kinds) sets special_body for the occupants. Once the race is over (screen flags bit 4) it becomes race_car_start_ending.
comment race_car_draw_behind A rival behind the screen's car, or off the screen (race_car_draw goes on to it): within 256 segments behind, small at the bottom of the screen, larger as it closes in (depth layer 191), across as it is on the road (the difference of road_x, at most 58 either way) and up by its bounce; its car and its number tag (tag_animation, tag_palette), into the list of that depth layer (race_car_draw_link), with special_body and gesture_shown cleared.
comment race_car_draw_link Puts the sprites a car has just written (from the screen's sprite_list_last on, up to sprite_entry) at the head of the list of its depth layer (screen + 5000 + 4 * depth_layer; its tail too when the list was empty), and moves sprite_list_last on to sprite_entry.
comment race_car_draw_spray Draws the spray at one side's wheels (dust, smoke, water...: the record's kind, from the race record's wheel effects), cycling its frames.
comment race_car_draw_part Draws a part of the car (a wheel, a spray) at its offset from the car's place, scaled with the car.
comment race_car_put_frame Puts the first piece of frame R14 of animation R12 with palette R13 into the sprite list, scaled by R8 at R17, R18 (race_car_put_sprite).
comment race_car_put_sprite Writes one sprite of a frame's piece into the screen's sprite list (and its copy at + 3000), its size and offsets scaled by R8, at R17, R18.
comment race_car_put_flat_sprite Writes the first piece of animation R12 into the sprite list at R17, R18 at its own size (the number tags).
comment race_car_start_ending The end of the race for the screen's car: starts the objects of its ending (ending_actor_lists by its model and whether it won, or unfinished_ending_actor_count when it did not finish); when they are done (state 2) sound 4F and raises screen flags bit 1, which game step 7 waits for ( the music stopped without a finish).
comment rival_warning_update RIVAL SEKKIN !! (a rival approaching) at the centre of the screen: 12 frames on, 6 off, 12 on, with the rival_alarm, then it ends.
comment rival_alarm_update The sound of the rival warning: sound 2B twice, 15 frames apart.

# ======== the attract mode, the name entry, the ranking and the route map (naming batch E1) ========
001687  func  car_presentation_update
001980  func  car_name_start
0019C0  func  car_name_letter_update
001EC2  func  name_entry_update
00200F  func  name_entry_read_wheel   # state 2's reading of the wheel, which name_entry_update goes on to from two places
002067  func  name_entry_choose   # the rest of state 2, which name_entry_update and name_entry_read_wheel go on to
002157  func  name_entry_finish   # the end of the name, which name_entry_choose goes on to from four places
00218E  func  name_entry_store_record   # state 3, which name_entry_finish goes on to
0021D9  func  name_entry_draw_time   # the seconds left and the texts, which states 1 and 2 both go on to
002204  func  name_entry_letter_update
