comment car_presentation_update The presentation of a car in the attract mode (game mode 3, car_presentation_object_handlers): the picture of the car screen attract_car names. By state: 0 starts car_name and staff_credits_code (with this object as its presentation, kept in staff_credits_code), the screen's background (set_up_background with 001AA0 by screen), the picture (animation attract_car_picture_animations by the car, palette 275, draw mode 0) at x A0, y 60 on depth layer 0; 1 steps its animation (animation_advance) and draws it. Every frame, in all states, it queues the next of the four palette records of car_presentation_palette_cycle (palette_step, round and round) with queue_palette_list, so the picture's colours cycle. State 2 (the palette only, no picture) is not set by its own code.
comment car_name_start Starts the letters of the presented car's name (attract_car_names by screen attract_car: MAD POWER, SPEED BUSTER, EASY HANDLING, SMOOTH OPERATOR, BAD BOY, ROAD MONSTER, QUICK REACTOR, WILD CHASER): a car_name_letter for each character, spaces too, 18 pixels apart from x 168, each 2 frames later than the one before (delay 15, 17...); then ends.
017A78  table background_register_lists  u32 background_registers   # set_up_background "mov.w 17A78[PC](R0), R10" (0179B0) by screen index: where its two tilemaps' register copies are; census ROM 017A78-017A7F (017A80 017AB0)
# The addresses of a screen's two tilemaps' register copies (and the bits it clears in video_registers[2]),
# as set_up_background writes them: "mov.w [R10+], R11" then a write through R11 (0179B6-017A06), "mov.w
# [R10+], R0; and.h R0, 204[R24]" (017A0A), "mov.w [R10+], R11" (017A17: the mixer, two registers on);
# census ROM 017A80-017ADF (screen 1: 20E240 20E2B0 20E250 20E252 20E230 20E232 20E292 20E296 20E2A2 20E2A6
# FFFA 208182; screen 2: 20E244 20E2B1 20E254 20E256 20E234 20E236 20E29A 20E29E 20E2AA 20E2AE FFF5 218184)
struct background_registers 30 rom
field background_registers 00 pages_address u32 tilemap_pages   # the record's two page words at it and 8 on
field background_registers 04 tile_banks_address u32 tilemap_banks   # its two tile banks at it and 2 on
field background_registers 08 zoom_x_address u32 video_register_words   # 200: 1:1
field background_registers 0C zoom_y_address u32 video_register_words
field background_registers 10 zoom_centre_x_address u32 video_register_words   # 0
field background_registers 14 zoom_centre_y_address u32 video_register_words
field background_registers 18 scroll_x_address u32 video_register_words
field background_registers 1C scroll_y_address u32 video_register_words
field background_registers 20 second_scroll_x_address u32 video_register_words
field background_registers 24 second_scroll_y_address u32 video_register_words
field background_registers 28 video_mask u32               # its low half and-ed into video_registers[2]
field background_registers 2C mixer_address u32 mixer_value_pairs   # the two priorities into the low four bits of it and the next
# A picture on a screen's two tilemaps, for set_up_background (R19: title_background_setups and the records
# car_presentation, name_entry, route_map, car_select_run and entry_countdown_start pass): "mov.w [R19+], [R11]"
# (0179B9), "mov.w [R19+], 8[R11]" (0179BD), "mov.b [R19+], [R11]" (0179C5), "mov.b [R19+], 2[R11]" (0179C9),
# "or.b [R19+], R0" (017A22, 017A30), "mov.w [R19+], R10" (017A3C: queue_tile_pages), "mov.w [R19], R19"
# (017A45: queue_palette_list); census ROM 01AD0C-01AD33 (05040302 00000101 01 03 01 02 01AD34 01AD64, ...)
struct background_setup 14 rom
field background_setup 00 pages u32               # the first tilemap's page word
field background_setup 04 second_pages u32
field background_setup 08 tile_bank u8
field background_setup 09 second_tile_bank u8
field background_setup 0A priority u8             # 0: off
field background_setup 0B second_priority u8
field background_setup 0C tile_pages u32          # the list of tile pages for queue_tile_pages
field background_setup 10 palette_list u32        # for queue_palette_list
param set_up_background R19 background_setup background_setup
comment car_name_letter_update A letter of the presented car's name (font 346 at size A0, its colours cycling through 001D70 with step_palette, at y 14). By state: 0 sets itself up (its character from character, x + 8); 1 hidden until delay runs out; 2 slides in from the right, 24 pixels a frame for 14 frames (slide_frames); 3 stays until 120 frames after its start (frames_left); 4 slides out to the left, 24 pixels a frame, and ends once x is negative.
comment name_entry_update The name entry after a race won at the goal under the course's record time (game step 18, when course_record_beaten says so): the certificate (CONGRATULATIONS!, the course, BEST RUNNER OF THE RACE.) with the time and the name, a drum of letters to pick from. By state: 0 lights the screen's two lamps (button_lamps) and clears lcd_restart_frames, starts name_entry_lcd, the background (set_up_background with 002E4C by screen; start_palette_fades with 13F6C0), the countdown number (time_digits_start), name_entry_object_handlers (name_entry_car, name_entry_drivers, name_entry_course_name, passing_traffic) and the 32 name_entry_letter objects; open, timer 600 frames, wheel_angle A80, the name's text record at x F8, y 54 and the time's at x A8, y 54 (time_to_text of the player's time, screen 410 + 2 * player); 1 the drum spins in (wheel_angle + 20 a frame for 40 frames, spin_frames; a letter is 80 of the turn's 1000), with the seconds left (name_entry_draw_time); 2 the wheel turns the drum a letter at a time, 4 frames a letter (turn_steps; a turn under way turns back if the wheel goes the other way), name_entry_read_wheel takes the next turn when none is under way and name_entry_choose the letter in front, the entry and the time; 3 name_entry_store_record; 4 nothing. Every frame it draws the time and the name (draw_text).
comment name_entry_read_wheel State 2's reading of the wheel for the drum's next turn (name_entry_update goes on to it when no turn is under way, or when one has just ended): the wheel in sixteenths gives a turn rate (name_entry_turn_rates: none in the middle, where << and >> turn it at rate F, one way or the other, and with neither turn_delay goes to 1); turn_delay counts down by it, and when it runs out (back to F) a turn of 4 steps starts (turn_steps 4, or -4 the other way). Then name_entry_choose.
comment name_entry_choose The rest of state 2 (name_entry_update and name_entry_read_wheel go on to it): the letter in front of the pointer (letter, from wheel_angle); the gas pedal let up (under 40) then pressed (C0 or more), START or the music button enters it with a sound (name_entry_place_sounds by place): the left arrow takes the last letter back, the right arrow enters a space, ED or a third letter ends the name (name_entry_finish); the letter in front blinks at the name's next place; the timer counts down, the name ends when it runs out (name_entry_finish), and the seconds left show (name_entry_draw_time).
comment name_entry_finish The end of the name (name_entry_choose goes on to it: ED, the third letter, or the time up): open 0, the name closed at its place; an empty name, or one of name_entry_rejected_names (SEX, XXX, XX and X's with spaces), becomes ---; state 3 with 60 frames (timer), and its first frame (name_entry_store_record).
comment name_entry_store_record State 3 of name_entry_update (and the frame name_entry_finish ends the name): after 60 frames (timer) writes the record into the race record (record_serial + 1, finish_time the player's time, record_model the car's model, record_name and record_name_rest the name, record_course the course: screen built_stage - 15), turns the lamps off (button_lamps), sets the game's next step (game_step 21) and state 4; every frame it draws the time and the name (draw_text).
comment name_entry_draw_time The seconds left as the countdown number (timer frames / 30 in BCD, time_digits_draw), then the time and the name (draw_text): each frame of states 1 and 2.
comment name_entry_letter_update A letter on the name entry's drum (font 2DE; index 0-31: A-Z ! ? . and the right arrow, the left arrow and ED, name_entry_letters); ends when the entry closes (the name entry's open is 0). By state: 0 its angle index * 80 round the drum, y_offset -5; 1 for 40 frames (spin_frames) y_offset + 1, the drum dropping into place. Every frame its place from its angle less the wheel_angle (sine_table, cosine_table): x_offset, depth, a width that narrows toward the drum's edges (width_factor), mirrored (flags bit 6) with palette 27 where it faces away (the cosine under 2000, more than 60 degrees from the front), 34 in front, 30 for the letter in front (shown 4 frames of 8: hidden, flags bit 5 cleared, when bit 2 of game_frame_count is clear); drawn in perspective (100000 / (depth + 100)) about x E0, y 40.
comment name_entry_lcd_update Shows the name entry on the cabinet LCD: 0 clears it (clear_lcd), shows the DJ's face (screen dj_face = FF) and writes the time (7 characters of the entry's time_text) at F13; then every frame the name's three characters at F1C, in the LCD's codes (7F -> 7E and | -> 7F, its arrows; \ -> 65); ends when the entry closes. It means to write spaces after the end of the name, but tests the address (tb R10) instead of the character, so it never does.
comment name_entry_car_update The player's car on the certificate screen (animation certificate_car_animations by the car's model, car model; palette car_model_palettes) at x 40, y C0: state 0 sets it up and runs into name_entry_drivers_update, which with state 1 draws it; then it draws itself every frame.
comment name_entry_drivers_update The car's two drivers standing by it on the certificate screen (animation certificate_driver_animations by the car's model, car model; palette certificate_driver_palettes) at x 60, y C8: state 0 sets them up and runs into name_entry_course_name_update, which with state 1 draws them; then drawn every frame.
comment name_entry_course_name_update The course's name on the certificate (Australia Course...: animation certificate_course_animations by the course, screen built_stage - 15; palette certificate_course_palettes) at x E0, y 40: state 0 sets it up; drawn every frame.
comment passing_traffic_start Counts the steps of the passing cars' path (traffic_path: size, x, y; closed by 0) into path_steps, then runs passing_traffic_update.
comment passing_traffic_update Now and then a car passes on the road behind the certificate: by state, 0 one frame in 16 at random (random_number) starts a passing_car on the path's last step (path_steps), 1 then waits 10 frames (wait).
comment passing_car_update A car on the road behind the certificate (animation car_model_turn_animations by a random model, palette car_model_palettes; its sound, car_pass_sounds by the model, requested as it starts): state 0 picks frame C and state 1, or frame 1 and state 2 when the random number is negative; then a frame a step it goes from the path's last step (traffic_path: size 370 near the bottom edge) to its first (size 5 on the horizon), at the path's x in state 1 or at 96 - x on the other side of the road in state 2; ends past the first step.
comment ranking_update The ranking in the attract mode (game mode 5, game_mode_ranking): OutRunner of OutRunners, the record of three courses in turn. By state: 0 starts ranking_objects (01AA01, 01A6CF, which game_mode_ranking has started too), ranking_background and the two halves of the title (ranking_title_left, ranking_title_right); 1 clears course_over; once the screen's fade is over (screen flags bit 0): after three courses ends, else courses_shown + 1, 60 frames (frames_left), and starts ranking_course_banner and ranking_record for the course screen record_course; 2 when the 60 frames are over sets course_over for a frame (the background fades out) and moves screen record_course to the next of the ten courses, back to 1.
comment ranking_background_update The picture of the course behind the ranking, while the game mode is the ranking (screen game_mode 5). By state: 0 courses_shown from the ranking; for the first course I/O C0001C = 6 (C8001C = 86 for screen 2) and a fade in (screen fade_kind 1, flags bit 0); ranking_background_load; 1 waits while the pages are unpacked (screen unpack_state), then for the later courses a fade in (fade_kind 3); 2 when the ranking says course_over: after the third course a fade out (fade_kind 0) and state 4, else a fade out (fade_kind 2); 3 when the fade is over, back to 0 for the next course's picture; 4 nothing more. Every frame, in any game mode, the picture scrolls by 3 pixels (scroll_x - 3).
comment ranking_background_load Loads the picture of the course screen record_course (course_backgrounds picks it) into the screen's first tilemap: its register addresses for the screen (ranking_background_register_sets, into 80-AC), the tile bank (ranking_background_tile_banks), the palette (background_palettes, queue_palette_list), the four tilemap pages (background_tile_page_lists, with the screen's page numbers from ranking_background_page_numbers, in page_list; queue_tile_pages), start_palette_fades with background_palette_fades, zoom 1:1, zoom centre and the second tilemap's scroll 0, the pages, the scroll's start (ranking_background_scroll_xs: x 360 for screen 1, 4A0 for screen 2; y 60), its priority 1 and the second tilemap off in the mixer (ranking_mixer_addresses: screen 182 and 186; flags bit 2); send_video_registers = FF to send the registers.
comment ranking_title_left_update The left half of the ranking's title, OutRunner of OutRunners (animation 2D2, palette 2B2) at x 0, y 30: state 0 sets it up; drawn every frame.
comment ranking_title_right_update The right half of the ranking's title, OutRunner of OutRunners (animation 2D3, palette 2B3) at x 13F, y 30: state 0 sets it up; drawn every frame.
comment ranking_course_banner_update The scroll with the course's name under the ranking's title (Australia Course...: animation ranking_course_banner_animations by course, palette ranking_course_banner_palettes) at x A0, y 50, for 90 frames (frames_left).
comment ranking_record_start Sets up the course's record line from the records at 20E300 (by course: the initials, '-' shown as '.', and a 0 into text, then the time as 5'00"00, time_to_text), starts a ranking_record_character for each of its 11 characters (index, course, itself) and ends at once.
comment ranking_record_character_update A character of a course's record line (font 2DE, palette 27): 0 its character from the record's text by index, at y 80 and x -A0 plus 16 pixels for each place of index; 1 slides in, 32 pixels a frame for 7 frames (slide_steps), then starts a ranking_record_car (each of the 11 characters starts one, all at the same place); 2 index + 2 frames later (colour_delay) takes the colours of the record's car (ranking_record_character_palettes by its model); 3 58 frames after its start (frames_left) slides out to the left, 16 pixels a frame, and ends at x -20.
comment ranking_record_car_update The record holder's car under the course's record line (animation car_model_turn_animations by the model of the course's record, palette car_model_palettes; size 50, y 88): drives in from the left 16 pixels a frame to x 108 or more, turns round (frame 9 down to 4), waits 15 frames (wait_frames), drives back left 16 pixels a frame and ends at x 0. State 5 (drawn only) is not set by its own code.
comment route_map_update The map of the route after the race (game step 12, route_map_object_handlers); keeps "Thank you for your playing." scrolling on the LCD (F40 = 005014). By state: 0 copies its screen's tilemap register addresses (route_map_register_sets) into 80-BC, first_branch from screen route_branches, the background (set_up_background with 004868 by screen and first_branch: the routes on tilemap 0 over the world on the second), the palette route_map_background_palette (scene_palettes[2C]) (queue_palette); 1 zoom 1:1 about the middle of the screen, sea_colour black, 30 frames (timer); 2 the world's tilemap off, sea_colour 7EE0; 3 zooms in (zoom + 20 a frame for 9 frames, 200 being 1:1), then the close-up map (pages route_map_close_up_pages) at its start (x scroll route_map_start_scroll_xs by first_branch, y 8C), starts route_map_car and the 32 route_map_landmark objects; 4 checkpoint_legs_left from the car's forks_taken, first_legs_left from screen current_stage_level, stage 0; 5-14 the car drives the route leg by leg (leg_frames frames each): 22 frames up from the start to the first checkpoint (the car moves, the map stands), then 6 frames of 8 pixels sideways (first_branch picks the side), 11 of 5, then for each stage 10 frames of 4 up or down by the branch taken (screen route_branches + stage - 1) and 15 of 7 sideways; on these legs the map scrolls under the car (the object's x and y move the other way, for the objects on the map), screen route_map_signals telling the car its way (bits 0 down, 1 up, 2 right, 3 left, 5 the map at its edge, 7 standing); a stage_result_line starts where each stage begins (5, 7, 11), a route_map_sign (C.P) and stage + 1 where a checkpoint leg ends; when its count of legs (first_legs_left for the first leg of a stage, checkpoint_legs_left for the others) is 0 at a leg's start, the race ended on it: last_leg_progress and leg_frames are worked out from the race_copy record's segment, the car's reached_stage_start and the stage lengths at 700 of the screen, and screen route_map_signals bit 6 ends the drive after it; at the end (stage 6 or a cut leg) the car stands (bit 7), FINISH (route_map_sign 1) if the goal was reached (race_copy finished), 60 frames; 15 the routes (pages route_map_routes_pages) at zoom 2x where the close-up map was, the steps of the zoom out into x and y, screen route_map_signals bit 4 (the car, the landmarks and the signs end); 16 zooms out over 9 frames to 1:1 and the whole route (x scroll route_map_whole_scroll_xs by first_branch, y 70), then the world's tilemap again (pages route_map_world_pages); 17 sea_colour black, starts 00463C and an 004240 for each leg (stage + 1, at most 6), DJ message 38, 90 frames; 18 DJ message 39 once the DJ is quiet (screen dj_mouth_shapes); 19 when the 90 frames are over sets the game's next step (screen game_mode byte 2 = 0x10 (step 16)) and starts 0046BB; 20 nothing.
comment route_map_car_update The player's car on the route map (animation route_map_car_animations by the car's model, palette route_map_car_palettes, size 50): 0 at x 8C, y C3 with frame C; 1 until the route map's stage is not 0, drives up 4 pixels and right 1 a frame unless the map says it stands (screen route_map_signals bit 7); 2 stays where it is while the map scrolls under it, its frame by the way the map says it goes (bit 2 frame 9, bit 3 frame 4, bit 0 frame 6, bit 1 frame C), moving itself 7 pixels a frame sideways when the map is at its edge (bit 5); ends when the map zooms out (bit 4). Its depth layer follows its y (map_depth_from_y).
comment route_map_sign_update A sign on the route map, by kind: 0 C.P where the car passed a checkpoint (animation 2F9), 1 FINISH at the goal (2FA); palette 2CF, depth layer 195. State 0: x_offset and y_offset are the middle of the screen (A0, 70 less 8) less the map's place, where the car is; kind 1 goes to state 2; 1 follows the map (its x and y plus the offsets) and turns (animation_advance); 2 FINISH at the goal's place on the map (the map's x plus route_map_goal_xs by the goal, screen current_stage - 15; y 5C), drawn 4 frames of 8 (blink); both end when the map zooms out (screen route_map_signals bit 4).
comment route_map_landmark_update One of the 32 animated pictures on the close-up route map (people, animals and vehicles by the landmarks): 0 from its record (route_map_landmarks: 16 * the index in 9, the second set of 32 (+ 200) when screen route_branches is 1): animation, palette, size, x_offset and y_offset from the map's place, then field 9 from the record (set for one record: it then shows only every other frame); 1 follows the map (its x and y plus the offsets), animation_advance and draw_object, with field 9 set only when toggle comes round; ends when the map zooms out (screen route_map_signals bit 4). Its depth layer follows its y (map_depth_from_y).
comment stage_result_line_draw A line of the player's places by stage on the route map, with two players or more (ends at once with one): every frame, the stage's label (stage_result_labels by stage: PRESTG, STAGE1-4, RESULT) at x A8, y 40 + 16 * stage, and at x E8 the player's place at that stage (stage_result_places by screen 360 + player + 8 * stage: 1st-8th); the last line says Retire (stage_result_retire) instead, unless it is RESULT (the goal reached), where the player's final place (place_address) is drawn big (animation 4, draw mode 8, at x F0, y B0, size 160; palette 41 for 1st, 42 for the others).
comment map_depth_from_y Sets the object's depth layer (3C) from its y: (y + D0) >> 3, at most 19F, so that what stands lower on the route map is drawn in front. Used by route_map_car and route_map_landmark.
comment draw_text_at Draws the string at R18 as draw_text does (a sprite per character into the screen's sprite list), at the place R3 holds (x in its low half, y in its high half, as a text record's first 32 bits) with the attribute R5. Used by stage_result_line, stage_indicator_draw and song_title_show (the song title).
comment queue_tile_pages Queues the tilemap pages of the list R10 to be unpacked into video RAM: each 32-bit entry a page number in its top byte (the page at 300000 + 400 * page) and the address of its packed data below, closed by a negative entry; the requests go into the screen's ring of 64 at C00 (unpack_write_place the place) and unpack_state says there is work. Used by set_up_background, ranking_background_load and race_background_update.
comment set_up_background Sets up the screen's two tilemaps (0 and 2 for screen 1, 1 and 3 for screen 2; their register copies listed at background_register_lists by screen) from the 20-byte record background_setup (R19): their pages (two words), their tile banks (two bytes) and their priorities in the mixer (two bytes, the low four bits of mixer registers 22 and 26, 24 and 28 for screen 2; 0 is off), zoom 1:1, zoom centre and scrolls 0, the second tilemap's row scroll and row select off (video_registers[2]), the registers and the mixer to be sent (send_video_registers, screen flags bit 2); then queues the pages of the list at the record's +C (queue_tile_pages) and the palette record at +10 (queue_palette_list). Used by car_presentation, name_entry, route_map, car_select_run, entry_countdown_start and title_screen_update.
comment start_object_list Starts an object for each handler of the list R10 (a 32-bit count, then the handlers: the object_handlers_* tables), each with its place in the list at 9, and returns the last one. Used by the game modes (game_mode_car_presentation, game_mode_ranking, game_mode_play, initialise_board...), name_entry and ranking.

# ======== the program ROM's data of the boot, the attract mode, the name entry, the ranking and the route map (rom names batch B1) ========
# The tables of code addresses the switches of these functions go through
0008CC  table game_mode_jumps  u32   # second_task calls through it by game_mode ("mov.w 8CC[PC](R0), R0" 0006FC and 000761, "jsr [R0]" 000704 and 000769): 001440 00144C 001000 001458 001516 001DCC 002E74 00DDA8 000D9F
000DAC  table isdn_connection_step_jumps  u32   # game_mode_isdn_connection jumps through it by mode_step ("mov.w DAC[PC](R0), R0" 000DA4, "jmp [R0]" 000DA9): 000DC8 000DDE 000E41 000E52 000E60 000E7E 000E95
000EB0  table isdn_status_state_jumps  u32   # isdn_status_update jumps through it by state ("mov.w EB0[PC](R0), R0" 000EA8, "jmp [R0]" 000EAD): 000EB8 000F6D
001014  table title_step_jumps  u32   # game_mode_title jumps through it by mode_step ("mov.w 1014[PC](R0), R0" 00100B, "jmp [R0]" 001010): 001024 001092 0010A7 0010BE
00146C  table car_presentation_step_jumps  u32   # game_mode_car_presentation jumps through it by mode_step ("mov.w 146C[PC](R0), R0" 001463, "jmp [R0]" 001468): 00147C 0014D6 0014EB 001502
001530  table demo_race_step_jumps  u32   # game_mode_demo_race calls through it by mode_step ("mov.w 1530[PC](R0), R0" 001521, "jsr [R0]" 001526): 001544 001577 001594 0015A9 0015C0
001694  table car_presentation_state_jumps  u32   # car_presentation_update jumps through it by state ("mov.w 1694[PC](R0), R0" 00168B, "jmp [R0]" 001690): 0016A0 001709 001715
0019CC  table car_name_letter_state_jumps  u32   # car_name_letter_update jumps through it by state ("mov.w 19CC[PC](R0), R0" 0019C4, "jmp [R0]" 0019C9): 0019E0 001A2D 001A38 001A47 001A5B
001DE0  table ranking_step_jumps  u32   # game_mode_ranking jumps through it by mode_step ("mov.w 1DE0[PC](R0), R0" 001DD7, "jmp [R0]" 001DDC): 001DF0 001E62 001E6F 001E7A
001ED0  table name_entry_state_jumps  u32   # name_entry_update jumps through it by state ("mov.w 1ED0[PC](R0), R0" 001EC6, "jmp [R0]" 001ECB): 001EE4 001FB0 001FC0 00218E 0021EE
002088  table name_entry_gas_step_jumps  u32   # name_entry_update jumps through it by gas_step ("mov.w 2088[PC](R0), R0" 002081, "jmp [R0]" 002086): 002090 00209D
0020C8  table name_entry_place_jumps  u32   # name_entry_update jumps through it by place ("mov.w 20C8[PC](R2), R2" 0020C1, "jmp [R2]" 0020C6): 0020D4 0020E2 0020E2
00221C  table name_entry_letter_state_jumps  u32   # name_entry_letter_update jumps through it by state ("mov.w 221C[PC](R0), R0" 002212, "jmp [R0]" 002217): 002228 00225E 00226B
002304  table name_entry_lcd_state_jumps  u32   # name_entry_lcd_update jumps through it by state ("mov.w 2304[PC](R0), R0" 0022FB, "jmp [R0]" 002300): 00230C 002327
002384  table name_entry_car_state_jumps  u32   # name_entry_car_update jumps through it by state ("mov.w 2384[PC](R0), R0" 00237D, "jmp [R0]" 002382): 00238C 00EAE4

# The car's place on a leg of the map after the race by the leg's progress: x, y and its frame (which way it faces; read as
# a halfword cut to a byte, "movt.hb [R10+], 40[R20]"); route_map_leg_car_show 004494 "movea.b 4964[PC](R0), R10" leg 0,
# 0044D4 "movea.b 49A4[PC](R0), R10" leg 1 (direction * 40 + progress * 8), 00452F "movea.b 4A24[PC](R0), R10" legs 2-5
# (direction * C0 + branch * 60 + progress * 8), then "mov.h [R10+], 30[R20]" / "add.h [R10+], 30[R20]"; census ROM
# 004964-004BA3: frames 4 6 9 C, the last halfword 0
struct route_map_leg_car_place 8 rom
field route_map_leg_car_place 00 x u16
field route_map_leg_car_place 02 y u16
field route_map_leg_car_place 04 frame u16
004964  table route_map_start_leg_car_places  route_map_leg_car_place         # 8, frame C
0049A4  table route_map_first_fork_leg_car_places  route_map_leg_car_place    # 2 directions x 8
004A24  table route_map_fork_leg_car_places  route_map_leg_car_place          # 2 directions x 2 branches x 12
local route_map_leg_car_show 004494,0044D4,00452F R10 place route_map_leg_car_place
local route_map_leg_car_show 00449A,0044DA,00454B R10 place
# A text drawn with draw_text_at: its attribute word (r5), then the characters, closed by 0 (stage_result_line_draw: 00417B
# "movea.b 4BA4[PC](R1), R18" by stage * 9, 0041E1 "movea.h 4C0A[PC], R18", then "mov.h [R18+], R5")
struct stage_result_text 9 rom
field stage_result_text 00 attribute u16
field stage_result_text 02 characters u8[7]
004BA4  table stage_result_labels  stage_result_text  # census ROM 004BA4-004BD9: 00A0 PRESTG, STAGE1-4, 00F0 RESULT
004C0A  table stage_result_retire  stage_result_text  # census ROM 004C0A-004C13: 00C0 Retire
# The player's place at a stage (stage_result_line_draw 0041CB "movea.h 4BDA[PC](R0), R18" by the place * 3, "mov.h [R18+], R5")
struct stage_result_place_text 6 rom
field stage_result_place_text 00 attribute u16
field stage_result_place_text 02 characters u8[4]
004BDA  table stage_result_places  stage_result_place_text   # census ROM 004BDA-004C09: 00C0 1st, 00B0 2nd-8th
local stage_result_line_draw 0041CB R18 place_text stage_result_place_text
local stage_result_line_draw 0041D1 R18 place_text
# A landmark's picture on the close-up route map (route_map_landmark_update 004083 "movea.b 4C14[PC](R0), R10" by index * 10
# + screen route_branches * 200, then 004089 "movz.hw [R10+], R9", "mov.h [R10+]" into 3A 34 36 84 88, 0040C9 "movt.hb [R10+],
# 9[R20]"); census ROM 004C14-005013: 2 x 32, animations 0039-033C, half_rate 0 or 1, the last halfword 0
struct route_map_landmark_record 10 rom
field route_map_landmark_record 00 animation u16
field route_map_landmark_record 02 palette u16
field route_map_landmark_record 04 width_scale u16
field route_map_landmark_record 06 height_scale u16
field route_map_landmark_record 08 x_offset s16
field route_map_landmark_record 0A y_offset s16
field route_map_landmark_record 0C half_rate u16
004C14  table route_map_landmarks  route_map_landmark_record
local route_map_landmark_update 004083 R10 landmark route_map_landmark_record
local route_map_landmark_update 004089,004092,004097,00409C,0040A1 R10 landmark


# ======== the car select, the map after the race and the entry screens (naming batch E2) ========

comment turntable_draw Places the object at a point of the car select's turntable scene and draws it: R1 across, R2 down and R3 in depth (the turntable's centre at 140), seen from a view that screen turntable_depth_offset takes back (car_select starts it at -300 and raises it to 0, so the scene shrinks into its place): with d = R3 + 100 + turntable_depth_offset, x = R1 * (100000 / d) >> 12 + A0, y = R2 * (100000 / d) >> 12 + 60, the depth layer (3C) 100 - d >> 4, the width and height scale (100000 / d) * the object's size (1C) >> 12. Not drawn (flags bit 5 cleared) with d under 10 or x outside -28..168. Used by car_attendant, turntable_car and turntable_scenery.
comment queue_palette Queues a block of colours (record R19: its place in the palette, its length in bytes, the colours) for the screen's palette RAM, copied in the vertical blank (upload_queued_palettes).
comment queue_palette_list Queues a list of colour blocks (record R19: its length in bytes, then 8-byte entries: the colours' address, their place in the palette, how many blocks of 16) for the screen's palette RAM, copied in the vertical blank (upload_queued_palettes).
comment queue_palette_upload Adds R19 (bit 31 from R8: a list, not a block) to the screen's queue of palette uploads (the count at palette_upload_count, the entries after it, up to 128).
comment upload_queued_palettes Copies the queued palette uploads (queue_palette, queue_palette_list) into the screen's palette RAM in the vertical blank and empties the queue.
comment upload_palette Copies a block of colours (place, length in bytes, colours) into the screen's palette RAM (600000 for screen 1, 680000 for screen 2).
comment upload_palette_list Copies a list of colour blocks (the list's length in bytes, then the address, place and number of 32-byte blocks of each) into the screen's palette RAM.
comment route_map_leg_show A leg of the route the player drove, drawn in red over the map shown after the race (003520), by state: 0 sets it up by its number (leg): 0 from the start to the first fork, 1 on from there by the direction taken (screen route_branches: 0 east, 1 west, the map's 09), 2-5 by the branch taken at each fork (screen route_branches + leg - 1) from the place of its stage (route_map_stage_xs, route_map_stage_ys by screen route_stages + leg - 2), the map shifted by the direction (route_map_direction_x_shifts) and 6B down; a leg driven already shows whole (frame 7, or B for 2-5), the leg the car is on (the map's stage) up to the frame before its progress, with the car on it (route_map_leg_car); legs 1-5 start the CP sign at their start (route_map_checkpoint), and leg 5, when the race reached the goal (race_copy finished), also the FINISH sign and the car at its end; 1 draws it.
comment route_map_leg_car_show The player's car on the map after the race, where the red line of its leg ends, by state: 0 sets it up: the sprite of its model (route_map_car_animations, route_map_car_palettes by car model) at a quarter size, its place and frame (which way it faces) from a record by the leg's progress (route_map_start_leg_car_places for leg 0, route_map_first_fork_leg_car_places for leg 1 by the direction, route_map_fork_leg_car_places for legs 2-5 by the direction and the branch, from the stage's place), shifted like the legs; 1 draws it.
comment route_map_checkpoint_show A sign on the map after the race, by state: 0 sets it up by its kind (route_map_sign_animations, route_map_sign_palettes): 0 the CP sign at the start of its leg (the fork it leaves from), 1 the FINISH sign beside the goal end of the last leg (route_map_finish_x_offsets, route_map_finish_y_offsets by the direction and the branch); 1 the CP sign turns (animation_advance) and is drawn; 2 FINISH blinks, drawn 4 frames out of 8 (blink).
comment route_map_direction_sign_show The WEST BOUND / EAST BOUND sign at the foot of the map after the race: a globe between the two, placed by the direction (screen route_branches) so that only the half of the way taken shows, by state: 0 sets it up below the screen; 1 rises 2 a frame to DC; 1 and 2 cycle its colours through four palettes from palette_base, one every 4 frames (colour_timer), and draw it.
comment route_map_wait_banner_show JUST A MOMENT PLEASE over the map after the race, while the game waits to go on, by state: 0 waits 32 frames (timer), then sets it up at the centre of the screen, tiny; 1 grows to full size in 15 frames; 2 drawn 15 frames; 3 hidden 5 frames, back to 2.
comment game_over_banner_show GAME OVER at the centre of the screen when a one-player race ends short of the goal, by state: 0 sets it up, tiny; 0 and 1 grow it to full size in 15 frames (timer); 2 draws it.
comment car_attendant_run The man who stands by the car in front on the car select's turntable, by state (car_attendant_state_jumps): 0 sets him up half a car round from the front (angle, radius, size); 1 stands beside the car in front (car model) until it changes, then 2 walks round the turntable, 20 a frame, to the front, facing his way (flags bit 6), and stands again; once the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen), 3 walks to one side of the car or the other by the transmission the wheel picks (side_offset to car transmission * 80) and stands there; once it is chosen (transmission_chosen), 4 strikes the pose for it (animation 267 automatic, 268 manual). Each frame he is drawn at his angle (plus side_offset) less the turntable's (car_select angle) with turntable_draw, and animated.
comment turntable_car_show One of the eight cars on the car select's turntable (car: its model and its place, car << 9), by state: 0 its sprite set and palette (turntable_car_looks by car); 1 placed by its angle less the turntable's (car_select angle), with the frame that shows it from there (turntable_car_frames), round the centre (radius screen turntable_radius_offset + 140) with turntable_draw; once the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen) the car in front takes the palette 4 on if it is the chosen one (car model) and goes to 2, where draw_object draws it where it was.
comment turntable_scenery_show A ring of scenery round the car select's turntable (one of twelve groups, from 006A18: the crowd, the palm trees), by state: 0 its animation, palette, size_step, size and list of items (item_list: angle, radius and height of each, closed by a negative angle); 1 each frame takes the turntable's angle (car_select angle) and draws the items on the far side (their angle less it 480..B7F) with turntable_draw, keeping for each its previous_place and flags bit 5 in item_places for draw mode 0A's halfway copy; then animation_advance.
comment select_car_heading_show "To Select A Car," at the top of the car select, by state: 0 starts the four pieces of the instructions under it (select_instruction, records at 006DF6) and sets it up; 1 drawn 20 frames (timer); 2 hidden 10 frames; it ends as soon as the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen).
comment select_instruction_show A piece of the car select's instructions ("Turn your", the steering wheel, "Then foot the", the accelerator; piece_animation, palette and place from select_car_heading), by state: 0 starts its animation; 1 drawn and animated until the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen); 2 rises 2 a frame for 16 frames (timer), over the transmission choice; 3 drawn and animated until the transmission is chosen (transmission_chosen), when it ends.
comment transmission_choice_run The transmission choice of the car select: the AUTOMATIC and MANUAL panels (on the second tile layer, with the sprites automatic_panel and manual_panel following x and y), by state: 0 starts the two panels; 1 slides them down into view, 10 a frame for 14 frames (y, car_select second_scroll_y_address); 2 when the wheel picks the other transmission (car transmission against shown_transmission) queues the panels' colours for it (queue_palette_list 13F998 automatic, 13F95C manual); when it is chosen (car_select transmission_chosen) starts the driver couple (driver_couple) and 3 slides the panels aside, 10 a frame for 10 frames (slide_step, slide_frames; x, second_scroll_x_address), the chosen one to the half the couple leaves free; 4 stays. From 2 on, every frame, the colours of the transmission picked or chosen cycle: the next palette list of automatic_panel_palette_lists (automatic, automatic_cycle) or manual_panel_palette_lists (manual, manual_cycle) every two frames.
comment automatic_panel_show The AUTOMATIC TRANSMISSION title of the left panel of the transmission choice, by state (automatic_panel_state_jumps): 0 starts its label (transmission_label_start_automatic) and sets it up; 1 cycles its colours (step_palette from palette_list) while the wheel picks automatic (car transmission 0), else palette 2AE; drawn at x_offset, y_offset less the choice's x, y (transmission_choice), which slide it. Nothing sets state 2 (drawing only).
comment manual_panel_show The MANUAL TRANSMISSION title of the right panel of the transmission choice, by state (manual_panel_state_jumps): 0 starts its label (transmission_label_start_manual) and sets it up; 1 cycles its colours (step_palette from palette_list) while the wheel picks manual (car transmission 1), else palette 2AE; drawn at x_offset, y_offset less the choice's x, y (transmission_choice), which slide it. Nothing sets state 2 (drawing only).
comment driver_couple_start Sets up the couple who drive the chosen car (a man and a woman, by the car's model: 006E58) on the left (x 50) after automatic (left_side) or on the right (F0) after manual, the half the panels slid away from; draw_object then draws it as its handler.
comment car_caption_show The caption of the car in front on the car select (EASY HANDLING, SMOOTH OPERATOR...: animation 26E, frame car model, palette car_caption_palettes), by state: 0 starts the gearboxes under it (car_gears_caption) and sets it up; 1 drawn 10 frames (timer); 2 hidden 10 frames; a new car in front (shown_car) shows it at once, for 10 frames more; it ends when the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen).
comment car_gears_caption_show The gearboxes of the car in front under its caption (Auto/5 Speed...: animation 2D1, frame car_gears_caption_frames by car model, the manual box's), blinking like car_caption: 1 drawn 10 frames (timer), 2 hidden 10, at once for a new car (shown_car); it ends when the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen).
comment waiting_banner_show The car select's message once the choices are made, by state: 0 sets up WAITING FOR CHALLENGERS! JUST A MOMENT PLEASE (animation 28B); 1 drawn 20 frames (timer); 2 hidden 10; once the entry time is over (car_select entry_over) JUST A MOMENT PLEASE (animation 28D) instead: 3 drawn 20 frames, 4 hidden 10.
comment car_select_entries_run The other players on the car select, by state: 0 its list of the race's players (race_order: the count, then the players); 1 when the LCD is not scrolling a text (screen lcd_scroll) writes Entry: on it with _ at each player's car number (car_numbers); then every frame: this screen's number blinks with _ every 8 frames (own_number, digit_toggle, blink_timer); each other player not yet marked (a bit each from 40, the frame field) gets its number on the LCD and, once it has chosen a car (byte 48 of its race_copy record, which car_select writes), a flag over that car on the turntable (choice_flag, kept in car_flags by car; one more player on the same car gets a stacked_choice_flag on the last one) and its bit; flags_visible follows the blink pattern car_select_entries_flag_blink (frames >> 1).
comment choice_flag_start Sets up a flag over the car another player has chosen (animation 1B, a palette by the car, choice_flag_palettes; angle: the car's place on the turntable), starts its number (choice_flag_number) and goes on to choice_flag_follow.
comment choice_flag_follow Keeps the flag over its car as the turntable turns (its angle less car_select angle, projected as turntable_draw does, above the cars) and draws it while car_select_entries' flags_visible says so (visible); not drawn behind the view.
comment stacked_choice_flag_start Sets up the flag of one more player on a car that has one (on the last flag there, below), starts its number (choice_flag_number) and goes on to stacked_choice_flag_follow.
comment stacked_choice_flag_follow Draws the flag 14 higher (and 3 right) than the one below it while that one is drawn (its flags bit 5 and visible); else it is not drawn.
comment choice_flag_number_start Sets up the car number of a flag's player (choice_flag_number_animations by its car number, car_numbers), then choice_flag_number_follow.
comment choice_flag_number_follow Draws the number on its flag (13 right, 10 up from its place) while the flag is drawn (its flags bit 5 and visible); else it is not drawn.
comment entry_traffic_run Sends cars up the road behind the entry countdown (entry_traffic_car), by state: 0 one chance in 16 each frame to start one; 1 then waits 10 frames (delay).
comment entry_traffic_car_drive A car coming up the road toward the view behind the entry countdown (car: a random one of the eight, turntable_car_looks), by state: 0 sets it up and picks its way: 1 along the path at 0067B2 (scale, x and y a frame, path_step) seen from the front and one side (frame 6), 2 the same path mirrored (x = 96 - x) from the other side (frame 7); one car in 128 goes to 3 or 4 instead, the same paths, but after 45 frames (spin_delay) its frames, its views all round, run five a frame (it spins) with set_tyre_noise(4) each frame; at the end of the path it requests its car's sound (car_pass_sounds) and ends.

# ======== the title screen, the coin prompts, the road surface and the backgrounds (naming batch F) ========
013AB7  func  traffic_follower_update
013AD8  func  pulled_vehicle_update
013D15  func  traffic_hit_sound_wait
0141F4  func  rider_gesture_update

# A timed sound of the lists of race_opening (014660) and stage_end_sounds (01466F): when it plays (the
# opening: the count of its steps it waits for; stage_end_sounds: the frames after the one before), then the
# sound; a time of 0 closes the list. The object keeps the place of the next sound (sound_list, 18)
struct sound_cue 2 rom
field sound_cue 00 time u8      # race_opening "mov.b [R10+], 12[R20]" (0144BF: next_count), stage_end_sounds "mov.b [R10+], 10[R20]" (01453D: countdown); "test.b [R10]" (0144E1, 014562: 0 ends)
field sound_cue 01 sound u8     # "movz.bw [R10+], R0" then request_sound (0144D8, 014559)

# The road tilemap's set-up for a screen (road_surface_layer_setups, by screen index): road_surface_start reads
# it with [R10+] from 016C2A "movea.w 1710C[PC](R0), R10" (R0 = index << 3, a word each: 32 bytes)
struct road_surface_layer_setup 20 rom
field road_surface_layer_setup 00 video_mask u16          # "and.h [R10+], R0" (016C35) with video_registers[2]
field road_surface_layer_setup 02 video_bits u16          # "or.h [R10+], R0" (016C38)
field road_surface_layer_setup 04 scroll_x_address u32 video_register_words    # "mov.w [R10+], R0; mov.h #160, [R0]" (016C40)
field road_surface_layer_setup 08 scroll_y_address u32 video_register_words    # "mov.w [R10+], R0; mov.h #0, [R0]" (016C49)
field road_surface_layer_setup 0C tile_bank_address u32 tile_bank_values   # "mov.w [R10+], R11; mov.b #0, [R11]" (016C55)
field road_surface_layer_setup 10 page_register u32 page_select       # "mov.w [R10+], 54[R20]" (016C5C)
field road_surface_layer_setup 14 mixer_address u32 mixer_value_pairs       # "mov.w [R10+], R11" (016C61), then its low bits 8
field road_surface_layer_setup 18 line_scroll_table u32   # "mov.w [R10+], 58[R20]" (016C78)
field road_surface_layer_setup 1C line_select_table u32   # "mov.w [R10], R10" (016C7D) -> 5C[R20]
01710C  table road_surface_layer_setups  road_surface_layer_setup   # census ROM 01710C-01714B (screen 1: 03FA E005 20E2A2 20E2A6 20E2B2 20E248 208186 31C1C0 31C5C0; screen 2: 03F5 E00A 20E2AA 20E2AE 20E2B3 20E24C 218188 31C3C0 31C7C0)

01714C  table road_surface_page_words  u32   # the road's page words, five (by the near scroll, 512 lines a step) for each of page sets 3-5: road_surface_start copies them into its page_table (18) with "movcu.h 1714C[PC], R0, 18[R20], R0" (016CDE, 1E halfwords); census ROM 01714C-017187 (36332C29 36332C29 34332A29 34352A2B 34352A2B, ...)

# The bytes of a road_tiles_change's data list (road_tiles_change data, 38: 017444 for the first flag, 0174B4 for
# the second; 0x70 bytes): the four pages by page set (from data - C, four bytes a set from set 3), the

# put back (+ 34, + 52, 30 each); road_tiles_change_update walks them a byte at a time: "movz.bw [R12+], R0"

01AE6B  func  demo_captions_music_select
01AE75  func  demo_captions_start
01AE8D  func  demo_caption_update
comment title_screen_update The title of the attract mode. By state: 0 sets it up: clears screen 5800 and 5801 (title_logo_shine, the logo's shine signal), keeps the addresses of the tilemaps' register copies (title_screen_layer_setups by screen: scroll_x_address, scroll_y_address for the first tilemap, second_scroll_x_address, second_scroll_y_address for the second), the background (set_up_background with title_background_setups by screen) and its scrolls, picks the car of the next car presentation (screen attract_car from next_attract_car, which steps through the eight), starts the credits line (credit_display), the prompt (coin_prompt) and the eight cars driving past (title_car); 1 for 45 frames (frames): palm trees (title_screen_start_palms) and the two tilemaps scrolled by 8 and 20 a frame, then the logo (title_logo, title_logo_copy), three logo cars (title_logo_car) and the three lines across the logo (title_logo_stripe); 2 the same palms and scrolls, with three more logo cars at each count of wave_times (120, 240, 300, 390, 450).
comment title_screen_start_palms Starts the title's palm trees: a near one (title_palm_tree with near_or_far 0) every 8th frame (palm_frames), else a far one (1) every other frame.
comment title_car_drive One of the eight cars driving past at the bottom of the title (title_screen starts them, index 0-7). Sets itself up from its record (title_car_records by index: the model, whose animation title_car_animations gives, palette, width_scale, height_scale, position, y, speed, and its depth_layer less 18F; draw mode A, frame 9) and starts its driver (title_car_driver) and passenger (title_car_passenger); each frame drives on by speed along a strip 4096 wide that wraps (clearing flags bit 5 as it wraps), is drawn at the position less the screen's offset (title_car_screen_offsets by screen car_number) and bobs a line up on odd frames (frames; rest_y keeps its y meanwhile).
comment title_car_driver_update The driver of a title car (the blond man at the wheel): sets up its picture by the car's model (animation title_driver_animations, palette title_driver_palettes, x_offset title_driver_x_offsets, y_offset title_driver_y_offsets, a layer in front of the car), then is drawn with the car each frame (title_rider_draw).
comment title_car_passenger_update The passenger of a title car (the red-haired woman beside the driver): sets up its picture by the car's model (animation title_passenger_animations, palette title_passenger_palettes, x_offset title_passenger_x_offsets, y_offset title_passenger_y_offsets, a layer in front of the car), then is drawn with the car each frame (title_rider_draw).
comment title_palm_tree_update A palm tree passing the title from right to left (title_screen_start_palms starts them): near (near_or_far 0: full size, low on the screen, 48 a frame) or far (1: 3/8 of the size, along the beach, 18 a frame); ends once off the left edge.
comment title_logo_update The OutRunners logo of the title: slides in from the right (16 a frame for 10 frames); then starts the palm trees of its board (title_board_palms), the two seagulls (title_seagull) and the title's still pictures (title_picture: the board, SEGA, (C) SEGA 1992), flashes through the palettes at 01AC54 (palette_list) for 6 frames and settles in palette 269 at its next frame; then shines (its animation again from frame 1) each time the first logo car reaches the right edge (screen title_logo_shine).
comment title_logo_copy_update A copy of the logo in other colours (cyan) that slides in from the left as the logo (title_logo) slides in from the right, meets it, flashes through the palettes at 01AC62 (palette_list) and ends.
comment title_logo_car_update A small car racing across the logo along one of its lines (title_screen starts three at a time, index 0-2): after 1, 5 or 9 frames by index, one of the eight cars at random (title_car_animations, title_logo_car_palettes) at a quarter of its size at the left edge; 16 a frame to the right edge; the first of the three then makes the logo, its board's palm trees and the lines shine (screen title_logo_shine for a frame), and it ends.
comment title_logo_stripe_update One of the three thin coloured lines across the logo that the logo cars race along (index 0-2): after 1, 5 or 9 frames by index it slides in from the left (16 a frame for 19 frames), then shines with the logo (its animation from frame 0 when screen title_logo_shine is set).
comment title_board_palms_update The palm trees at the right end of the logo's board: grow in from 3/4 of their size (8 frames), then sway each time the logo shines (screen title_logo_shine).
comment title_seagull_fly One of the two seagulls over the title (index 0, 1): flies up out of the logo for 8 frames growing to full size, then circles about that place in a figure of eight (the sine of x_angle, a turn in 128 frames, and of y_angle, twice as fast), flapping (its animation).
comment title_picture_draw One of the title's still pictures (title_logo starts them): by index, from its record at 01AC70 (animation, palette, width_scale, height_scale, x, y, depth_layer): 0 (C) SEGA 1992, 1 the oval board behind the logo, 2 the SEGA logo.
comment coin_prompt_update The prompt at the bottom of the title, the car presentations and the demo races, and its text on the cabinet's LCD; ends at once in game mode 6 (screen game_mode). Every 30 frames (round_frames): a little car crosses the message (coin_prompt_car with this round's car; next_car then steps through the eight, from screen car_number) and the message by the seat (seat1_credits and seat1_coin_units for screen 1, seat2_credits and seat2_coin_units for screen 2): with free play (free_play) or a credit message 2, TURN THE IGNITION KEY (coin_prompt_start_key_prompt); else message 1 with coins in toward a credit or 0, a coin_prompt_message in the car's colour (INSERT COIN(S)). Each frame the LCD's text to scroll (screen lcd_scroll_request) is the message's (coin_prompt_lcd_texts: Insert Coin(s), Insert More Coin(s), Turn the Ignition Key), except in game modes 6 and 7 or while a text scrolls (lcd_scroll).
comment coin_prompt_start_key_prompt Starts R1 pieces of the ignition key prompt (ignition_key_prompt), pieces R2 on, and returns the last one.
comment coin_prompt_car_drive The little car that drives across the prompt from right to left each round (coin_prompt starts it): one of the eight cars (car) at a quarter of its size, 14 a frame, ending at the left edge.
comment coin_prompt_message_update The prompt's message for a round (coin_prompt starts one every 30 frames): after 6 frames, INSERT COIN(S) (message 0; 1 when coins are in, animation coin_prompt_message_animations) in the colour of the round's car (coin_prompt_message_palettes by car_colour), animating in for 22 frames over the one before, then a layer back for 30 frames, and ends; ends at once when the prompt's message changes.
comment ignition_key_prompt_update A piece of the prompt when a game can start (coin_prompt starts both every round): by piece, from its record at 01A95B (animation, palette, width_scale, height_scale, x, y, depth_layer, frames_left): 0 the words TURN THE IGNITION KEY, 1 the START / IGNITION key with its arrow; animates until frames_left runs out (24 or 30 frames) and ends.
comment credit_display_update The credits line under the prompt (title, car presentations, demo races); ends in game mode 6. With free play (free_play) FREE PLAY; otherwise, for the screen's seat (seat1_credits and seat1_coin_units for screen 1, seat2_credits and seat2_coin_units for screen 2): Credit (0 or 1) or Credits and the number of credits in the font's digits (tens at x AC when there are, units at B4), and while coins are in toward the next credit the coins and the coins a credit costs (coins_per_credit) as a fraction (C0 and C8), each drawn with credit_display_digit.
comment credit_display_digit Draws one digit of the credits line at x and y: digit_kind picks the animation and palette (credit_display_animations, credit_display_palettes: 0 the font, whose frames are its characters, 1 the coins, 2 the coins a credit costs) and digit the frame.
comment demo_captions_run The captions over the demo race of the attract mode: counts its frames and runs the routines of timeline_1AFC0 as it reaches their frames (30 demo_captions_head_to_head, 225 demo_captions_checkpoint, 420 demo_captions_music_select); ends once the count passes a record's frame (the closing FFFF).
comment demo_captions_head_to_head Frame 30 of the demo captions: HEAD TO HEAD! 2 PLAYER RACING (demo_caption records 0, 1 and 3 + machine_count, or 4 when stand_alone is set; 4 in attract.txt).
comment demo_captions_checkpoint Frame 225 of the demo captions: AT THE CHECKPOINT ONLY THE RACE LEADER CAN SELECT THE NEXT STAGE ! (demo_caption records 2 and 3).
comment demo_captions_music_select Frame 420 of the demo captions: the radio's panel, the hand pressing its button and MUSIC SELECT (demo_caption records 8-11).
comment demo_captions_start Starts R1 demo_caption objects with the records from R0 on.
comment demo_caption_update One picture of the demo captions (demo_captions_start starts them): from its record at 01AF00 (animation, palette, width_scale, height_scale, x, y, depth_layer, frames_left: 180 frames for records 0-7, 240 for 8-11), animated and drawn until frames_left runs out.
comment traffic_follower_update A vehicle in a line of road traffic behind a 0138EB leader (traffic_car_start_convoy starts the line from the leader's list: each one the list's spacing behind the one before it, with its own record): moves and draws as the leader's family does (traffic_car_update), but lives exactly as long as the one before it (leader): when that one has ended it ends too (screen traffic_count, the count of the traffic, less 1). The only line in the road's lists is in list 25 of stage_road_object_lists (a leader of animation 35C and eight of 35D); the scenarios start it only for a frame or two.
comment pulled_vehicle_update The vehicle a road animal pulls (traffic_figure_start_team starts it behind a 013BA3 leader from the leader's list): the covered wagons behind the horses (list 1 of stage_road_object_lists), the sleighs behind the reindeer (lists 25 and 26). Moves and draws as the 013AF9 traffic does (traffic_stopping_car_update), and lives exactly as long as the one before it (leader): when that one has ended it ends too (screen traffic_count less 1).
comment traffic_hit_sound_wait Plays a road object's own sound 15 frames after the car has hit it (traffic_start_hit_sound starts it with the sound of the object's record): counts delay down, then requests sound (request_sound) and ends.
comment rider_gesture_update A rider's gesture on another racer's car (00974C; race_cars_start starts one for its driver, rider = model, and one for its passenger, model + 8; only some have a gesture, rider_gesture_animations, the others end at once): when the car moves up a place in the race (rider_gesture_check_place) it plays its gesture once (the driver raises an arm, a finger up) in place of the rider, and while the car can be seen from the angle and the screen's car is racing (rider_gesture_check_view) it is drawn on the car (rider_gesture_place) at the car's size and a layer in front; then waits for the next time.
comment rider_gesture_check_view Whether the gesture shows (zero clear): only while the screen's car object is in state 2 or 4 (rider_gesture_car_states) and the car is seen from one of the angles that show it (rider_gesture_views by the car's view); otherwise clears the car's gesture_shown; then rider_gesture_check_place.
comment rider_gesture_check_place Notes the racer's place in the race (screen + 320 + player) in its car's last_place and sets its gesture_shown when it is better than the last one; returns gesture_shown masked with R9 (zero set when there is no gesture to show).
comment rider_gesture_place Places the gesture on its car: the car's x and y (plus the screen's scroll, screen sprite_origin_x and sprite_origin_y) and the rider's offset, rider_gesture_x_offsets and rider_gesture_y_offsets by rider plus the seat's offset for the car's view (race_car_rider_places by model, by the car's view, the driver's or the passenger's), scaled by R0 (the car's width_scale).
comment race_opening_update The opening of a race (not in the demo): the view comes down from high above the start line to behind the car, taking the car's view_shift_intro up by 18 and view_pitch_intro down by 3 a step until it is 0 (80 steps from FA60 and F0; camera_step), while the opening's sounds play (race_opening_sounds_play; sound_step); ends when both are done.
comment race_opening_sounds_start Starts the opening's sounds: the first at count 1, the rest of race_opening_sound_cues.
comment race_opening_sounds_play Counts the opening's steps (count) and requests each sound of the list at its count (sound_list: sounds 2D, 24, 0A, 21, 2E, 2D, 24 at the counts 1, 20, 65, 120, 150, 160, 190); after the last goes on to race_opening_sounds_done.
comment race_opening_sounds_done Does nothing: the opening's sounds are over.
comment road_surface_update The road of the race, drawn on its tilemap with line scroll and line select. By state: 0 road_surface_start; then each frame this frame's lines (road_surface_lines) and, from state 2, those of the frame in between (road_surface_halfway_lines); where the first segment's page set (segment_page_set) is 0 it starts the stage's palette fades (start_palette_fades with screen road_palette_fades; state 3), and at the next one that is not takes it as page_set and fades back (road_surface_fade_back; state 2). Each frame the tilemap's page word (at page_register) is the page table's word for page_set and near_scroll (five a set). At each new segment (segment past last_segment) acts on the segment's event when it changes (after the first 30 segments, event_delay): 4 and 5 change the background within the stage (screen stage_background_change = FF, stage_background_branch = 0 or 1, for race_background), 1 and 2 turn the two road_tiles_change flags (0A, 0B) on and off, 3 both; a flag turned on starts a road_tiles_change (the Golden Gate Bridge in race_to_goal.txt, frames 7659-8325).
comment road_surface_start Sets up the road's tilemap from the screen's road_surface_layer_setups record: its bits in video_registers[2], its scroll (160, 0), tile bank, mixer bits, page_register, the line tables in the video RAM (line_scroll_table, line_select_table) with the line select's start from 100800, the page set of the stage (stage_road_page_sets by screen first_stage), the page table (road_surface_page_words) and the stage's palettes (stage_road_palettes by stage: the fades' list into screen road_palette_fades, the rest queued with queue_palette_list).
comment road_surface_lines Works out this frame's road: from the segments in view (screen + 3000, 16 bytes each, the first at the view's position race_copy window_segment) the first one's page set and event (+C, +D) and number (segment), each segment's point (+2 depth, +4 x, +6 y) into the points buffer (screen + 3800 by points_buffer), and between points the lines of the line scroll (the road's x) and line select (its depth, every other pair of segments in the picture's second half: the stripes) tables, from the bottom line up (road_surface_fill_lines); a stretch it passes over is filled after (road_surface_fill_skipped_lines); the farthest point's line into screen road_top_line and the bottom line's scroll into near_scroll.
comment road_surface_halfway_lines Works out the road of the frame in between: the same lines from the average of this frame's points and the last frame's (the two halves of the points buffer, matched by segment), into the next tables; flips points_buffer.
comment road_surface_fill_lines Fills the road's lines between two of its points, from the lower one up: the line scroll steps evenly from one point's x to the other's and the line select from one's depth to the other's (at most R18), plus R14 (100 on alternate pairs of segments); returns the two steps.
comment road_surface_fill_skipped_lines Fills the stretch of lines road_surface_lines passed over (from line skipped_line, at the table places it kept) by going on from the kept point with the kept steps down to line DF; nothing when there is none.
comment road_surface_start_tiles_change Starts the road_tiles_change of event 1 (its data road_tiles_change_first_data, the even bytes of the page table) with the road's first flag (0A).
comment road_surface_fade_back Starts the palette fades of list R19 backwards (start_palette_fade from each record's second palette to its first): the road's colours back after a change of page set.
comment road_tiles_change_update Changes the road's tiles for a stretch of road (road_surface starts it when an event turns one of its flags on; the Golden Gate Bridge in race_to_goal.txt): copies the four tile pages the road shows (source_pages to target_pages, a page a frame), turns its half of the road's page table (table_half: the even or odd bytes) to the copies, copies the new look into the pages by the road's page set (16 halfwords of each a frame for 16 frames) and turns the table back; when the road turns its flag off (flag) it changes them back the same way and ends; its data list (data: road_tiles_change_first_data for the first flag, road_tiles_change_second_data for the second) holds the pages and the bytes for the table.
comment race_background_update The scenery behind the road (the race's first tilemap). By state: 0 sets it up: its register copies (race_background_layer_setups by screen: scroll_x_address, scroll_y_address, tile_bank_address, page_word_address), the stage's background (background: stage_backgrounds by screen first_stage, none for stage 0), its tile pages (background_tile_page_lists, loaded at once with unpack_tile_pages), tile bank and palettes (race_background_fade_palettes, queue_palette_list), its objects (race_background_start_objects) and, for stages 6, 8 and 1C, its animation (race_background_start_lower_animation, race_background_start_upper_animation); 1 waits for a change: a fork's new stage (the car's new_branch; background 2 * screen built_stage + the branch, car branch) or the road's events 4 and 5 (screen stage_background_change; background 2A + built_stage): screen background_changing set, the new background's pages queued into the other half of the pages (page_half; queue_tile_pages); 2 changes over as the view turns away and back (the car's view_yaw; change_step): when it starts its palettes, tile bank and the first page word (next_page_words), at 2A0 (off the screen) the fades of background_leaving_fades and of the road (background_road_palettes, into screen road_palette_fades) and the next, the new stage's objects (screen route_stage), at 160 its palette fades (race_background_fade_palettes) and the next, at 60 the last, at 0 back to state 1 with the animation of backgrounds 5, 7, 8, 25 and 26. Each frame scrolls it: x by the car's view_yaw (a quarter of it, all of it during a change) less A0, y by the slope (the car's road_pitch) and screen horizon_line, kept between 0 and 180 less the road's top line (screen road_top_line; also into screen background_scroll_y).
comment race_background_start_lower_animation Loads the tiles of the background's lower animation (queue_tile_pages with background_lower_animation_tile_pages by screen) and starts it (race_background_lower_animation).
comment race_background_start_upper_animation Loads the tiles of upper animation R0 (queue_tile_pages with background_upper_animation_tile_pages by R0 and screen) and starts it (race_background_upper_animation).
comment race_background_start_objects Starts the background objects of stage R0 (stage_background_object_lists; R0 + 1F during a change within the stage, screen stage_background_change), each with its data (field 80) and this object (14).
comment race_background_fade_palettes Starts the palette fades of list R19 (start_palette_fade for each record) and a race_background_fade_watch that keeps them; with an empty list clears screen background_fading (the background's palettes are changing) at once.
comment race_background_lower_animation_wait Waits until the animation's tiles are loaded (screen unpack_state), then goes on to race_background_lower_animation_cycle.
comment race_background_lower_animation_cycle Every 2 frames (delay) puts the next of four page pairs (background_lower_animation_page_pairs; step goes two on, keeping to the screen's own) into the second half of the background's page word (the lower pages); ends when a change of background starts (screen background_changing).
comment race_background_upper_animation_wait Waits until the animation's tiles are loaded (screen unpack_state), then goes on to race_background_upper_animation_cycle.
comment race_background_upper_animation_cycle Every 3 frames (delay) puts the next of three page pairs (background_upper_animation_page_pairs) into the first half of the background's page word (the upper pages: the schools of fish over the underwater tunnel swim); ends when a change of background starts (screen background_changing).
comment race_background_fade_watch_update Waits for the background's palette fades (fades, count of them) to end, forgetting each as it ends (its flags bit 0 clear); then clears screen background_fading (the background's palettes are changing) and ends.
comment palette_fade_prepare Prepares a palette fade, one colour a frame for its 16 (colours_left): the colour of the first palette (from) into its colours (34 on) and its red, green and blue (five bits each) less those of the second (to) into its differences (54 on); then palette_fade_step.
comment palette_fade_step Every period frames (delay; start_palette_fade staggers the first) moves each of the 16 colours one step of each of red, green and blue toward the second palette (by its differences, counted down to 0) and queues them (queue_palette_list with its record at 28: 8 bytes, the colours, palette_offset, blocks); ends when no colour moved, or when the screen fades out (screen flags bit 0, with fade_kind at most 5 and even).
comment start_palette_fade Starts a palette_fade of 16 colours from the palette at R10 to the one at R11, for palette offset R12 (R0 blocks of 16 in its upload record), a step every R1 frames, the first after (screen palette_fades_started mod R1) + 1 (palette_fades_started counts the fades started); returns the object.
comment start_palette_fades Starts a palette_fade for each 12-byte record of list R19 (a 32-bit length in bytes, then records: from, to, palette offset, blocks, period).
comment song_title_show A song title on the screen (start_song_title starts it, for car_radio, the car select and 00C8B6): its x and text from the record (x, and the text, which also scrolls on the LCD through screen lcd_scroll_request), then slides in from the right (slide from D2, slowing down to a stop by speed), stays for time_left (60 frames) and slides out to the left speeding up; sent away early (time_left 0, car_radio_show_title) it leaves at 40 a frame. Drawn each frame with draw_text_at at y C8 in colours that cycle every 2 frames (song_title_colours by colour_step).

scoped object race_background_lower_animation 20 page_word_address u32 tilemap_pages
scoped object race_background_upper_animation 10 delay u8
scoped object race_background_upper_animation 12 step u8
scoped object race_background_upper_animation 20 page_word_address u32 tilemap_pages
scoped object race_background_fade_watch 10 count u32
scoped object race_background_fade_watch 14 background u32 object
scoped object race_background_fade_watch 80 fades u32 object
scoped object palette_fade 10 delay u8
scoped object palette_fade 12 period u8
scoped object palette_fade 13 colours_left u8
scoped object palette_fade 18 from u32 palette_colours
scoped object palette_fade 1C to u32 palette_colours
scoped object palette_fade 20 colour_place u32 palette_fade_colours
scoped object palette_fade 24 difference_place u32 palette_fade_differences
scoped object palette_fade 30 palette_offset u16
scoped object palette_fade 32 blocks u16
scoped object song_title 0D colour_step u8
scoped object song_title 10 time_left s16
scoped object song_title 12 speed u16
scoped object song_title 18 text u32 rom
scoped object song_title 20 slide s16
scoped object song_title 22 y u16
field object 3C depth_layer u16
scoped object car_select_entries 40 shown_players u32  # bit n: player n's choice is shown already (test1/set1 R0, 40[R20])

# ======== the frame loops, the inputs, the game modes and the EEPROM (naming batch G) ========
