comment boot The reset's code (000006): SBR to the vector table, the stack 21FC00, 16 bytes from 0000A4 to E00000 and 4 from 0000B4 (boot_e80000_bytes) to E80000 (which the board does not map), the interrupt controller's registers from 0000B8, privilege level 0 with the stack 21F400 and a delay; then clears 21E000-21E3FF, turns the link board off (801000), clears 21F000-21FFFF, sets the I/O chips up (init_io_chips), clears the game's variables (20E000-20FFFF), writes the second task's first interrupt frame (000C98 to 21FF84: its PC 0006B0) and its stack pointers (waiting_task_frame, waiting_task_stack), initialise_game, lcd_start (the LCD), the interrupts on, the link's check (network_check), and goes on as the boot task's frame loop (second_task(1), screen 2's).
comment second_task The two tasks' frame loops, one function in the C: second_task(0) is the second task (from 0006B0), which runs screen 1; second_task(1) the boot task after boot (from 000637), which first sets up the link's race (link_take_master_settings), the interrupt controller's timer (D00008, D0000A), task_swapping, each screen's first attract car (screen next_attract_car from first_attract_car_lists by machine_count and the screen's car number) and the screens on (C0001C, C8001C), then runs screen 2 (second_task_frames, which comes back when the test mode starts). A frame of either: wait_for_frame (the VBLANK swaps the tasks meanwhile; the second task calls link_send_records before it with second_task_waiting set, and the boot task calls link_send_records and link_receive_records only while that is clear), the test mode when test_mode_on is set, link_receive_records, race_entries_update, race_progress_update and course_records_update (the link and the race records), game_frame_count + 1 (screen 1's loop), take_screen_inputs, check_game_start, the game mode's code (game_mode_jumps by game_mode: game_mode_power_on, game_mode_restart, game_mode_title, game_mode_car_presentation, game_mode_demo_race, game_mode_ranking, game_mode_play, entry_invitation_step (the entry countdown on a seat without a player), game_mode_isdn_connection), run_objects and lcd_update; with single_task set it goes on with the other screen's frame instead of waiting. The test mode: task_swapping off, test_mode_start, then test_mode_frame each frame while test_mode_on lasts; then the screens off, the variables cleared, the second task's frame written again, initialise_game, lcd_reset, both screens in game mode 1 with a fade in (fade 1), network_check_again, and the boot task's part again.
comment second_task_frames Both screens' frames from screen 2's on, until the test button (test_mode_on) ends them: then back to second_task, for the test mode. Screen 2's frame (as second_task's, without game_frame_count) over and over; with single_task set, screen 1's frame after it (second_task_waiting set around its link_send_records and wait_for_frame, game_frame_count + 1), which goes on over and over while single_task is clear, and back to screen 2's when it is set.
comment wait_for_frame Waits for the next frame: polls frame_ready (set by the VBLANK, which also swaps the tasks) and clears it; while the screen has tile pages to unpack (screen unpack_state) it unpacks them a piece at a time (unpack_queued_step) between the polls.
comment initialise_game Sets the game up, at start-up and after the test mode: 13, 0, 0, 1F to C00074 (which the board does not map); for screen 2, then 1, its objects' page cleared (clear_screen_ram) and its constants (init_screen: index, its video registers, the view's, the courses' stage lengths at 700); initialise_board; then initialise_screen for screens 1 and 2.
comment initialise_screen Sets a screen up: its sound off (reset_screen_sound), no objects (clear_objects), its tilemaps' registers and their copies (init_mixer), three palettes uploaded (upload_boot_palettes) and the courses' records (load_course_records).
comment initialise_board Sets the board up: the I/O chips (init_io_chips), the settings (load_settings), the sprite chip and its RAM (init_sprite_chip, load_sprite_ram_tables), the video RAM and its control (init_video), the backup RAM checked (bookkeeping_check), the credits (init_coins), the sound requests (start_sound_program), three lists of tile pages unpacked at once (unpack_tile_pages: 109420, 109428, 109578), then the inputs read and taken twice (read_switches_and_analog, take_screen_inputs) so that their previous values hold.
comment read_switches_and_analog Reads the inputs in the VBLANK (int_vector_40; twice at start-up): the switches of both I/O chips (ports E and F: bits 0-1 the seats' service switches, 2-5 the coin switches, 7 a test switch) into switches, with previous_switches, switches_pressed and switches_released; then, on the turn of the screen whose task runs, half the analog inputs through the A/D converter (C00050 + 4 * index): screen 1's turn seat 1's wheel, gas and brake and seat 2's wheel, screen 2's seat 2's gas and brake (the converter's second bank), each raw into analog_raw on and scaled to 0-FF between its calibration (a low and a high byte from 20E638, in the settings) into analog_inputs on; then the converter's bank for the other turn (C00060).
comment pack_seat_inputs Packs both seats' inputs for the coming frame (the VBLANK, on screen 1's turn) into their records: the screen's player + 1, the buttons (port A's high bits into 0-3, its low two into 4-5, the shift; port E bit 4 into bit 6, START), the wheel (analog_inputs inverted), the gas and the brake: seat_1_player to seat_1_brake and seat_2_player to seat_2_brake. On ISDN (isdn_mode) they go to the link board instead (link_send_seat_inputs).
comment link_send_seat_inputs Never runs in the scenarios (ISDN only): writes a seat's five bytes as halfwords on the link board at R11 (800020 on): 0, the buttons (R0), the wheel (inverted), gas and brake from analog_inputs at R10; or, when its screen has a link_event (R2: screen + 40), the event and the four bytes after it, which it then clears (without moving R10 on, so the next seat would read this one's analog inputs).
comment queue_seat_inputs queue_screen_inputs for screen 1 (seat_1_buttons, the first I/O chip) and screen 2 (seat_2_buttons, the second): the VBLANK on screen 2's turn.
comment queue_screen_inputs Puts a screen's inputs into its ring of eight (screen + 130, at input_write_place): the seat's buttons (R11) with ~port B of its I/O chip (R12 + 2) in bits 8-15; and the record's wheel, gas and brake into the screen's wheel, gas and brake. On ISDN the record is the screen's race_copy's +2C (the inputs the link brought).
comment take_screen_inputs Takes the screen's inputs for the frame from its ring (input_read_place; both frame loops, before check_game_start): buttons, with previous_buttons, buttons_pressed and buttons_released (bit 6 START, 4-5 the shift up and down, 0-3 the others, 8-15 port B). On ISDN also the link's status (race_copy link_status) into link_status, with link_status_set, link_status_cleared and link_status_changed, and, unless in it already, game mode 8 (game_mode_isdn_connection) while it says the line connects (bits 2-3).
comment check_game_start Each frame before the game mode's code: in the attract modes (not 0, not 6), with a credit on the seat (seat1_credits, seat2_credits for screen 2) or free play (free_play), blinks the START lamp (bit 2 of the seat's port_d_outputs, 8 frames on and 8 off by start_lamp_blink); START pressed (buttons_pressed bit 6) takes the credit (not with free play; and clears the bytes at 20E0A2 and 20E0AA, after each seat's coin units), turns the lamp off and starts a game: game mode 6 at step 0, link_event 1, the race record's car part cleared (race_record_clear) and race entry_request = FF. Returns the game mode.
comment game_mode_power_on Game mode 0 (the first frame after start-up): game mode 2 and its first step (game_mode_title).
comment game_mode_restart Game mode 1 (after the ranking, game_mode_ranking, and after the test mode): game mode 2 and its first step (game_mode_title).
comment game_mode_title Game mode 2, the title (title_screen_update), by mode_step: 0 the screen cleared (as clear_screen), the title's palette (title_palette), title_screen_update started, a fade in (fade 1), the input ring's places to 0 (input_write_place, input_read_place), the screen on (C0001C = 6, C8001C = 86 for screen 2); 1 when the fade is over, mode_timer 450 frames (15 seconds); 2 counts them down, then a fade out (fade 0); 3 when it is over, game mode 3 (the car presentation). attract_lamps_update every frame first.
comment game_mode_car_presentation Game mode 3, the presentation of a car (car_presentation_update), by mode_step: 0 the screen cleared (as clear_screen), the palettes of the car's scene (scene_palettes by attract_car_scene_palettes by attract_car) and 031740, car_presentation_object_handlers (credit_display, coin_prompt, car_presentation) started, a fade in; 1 when it is over, mode_timer 180 frames (6 seconds; the staff credits code adds 1200); 2 counts them down, then a fade out; 3 when it is over, link_event 3 and game mode 4 (the demo race). attract_lamps_update every frame first.
comment game_mode_demo_race Game mode 4, the demo race of the attract mode (the car just presented, attract_car, on its stage): attract_lamps_update, then by mode_step demo_race_set_up, demo_race_fade_in, demo_race_start_timer, demo_race_count_down, demo_race_end, and but on the set-up's and the end's frames the race's own steps (game_mode_play by game_step, from step 10).
comment demo_race_set_up Step 0 of the demo race: demo_flags bit 8 (the race's code tells the demo by it: the car drives itself), the screen cleared (as clear_screen), step 1 with mode_timer 0, and game_step 10 (the demo race's set-up in game_mode_play, which does not run this frame).
comment demo_race_fade_in Step 1 of the demo race: after two frames (mode_timer), a fade in and step 2.
comment demo_race_start_timer Step 2 of the demo race: when the fade is over, mode_timer 600 frames (20 seconds) and step 3.
comment demo_race_count_down Step 3 of the demo race: counts mode_timer down, then a fade out and step 4.
comment demo_race_end Step 4 of the demo race: when the fade is over, game mode 5 (the ranking) and the race record's car part cleared (race_record_clear), without the race's step that frame.
comment attract_lamps_update The lamp show of the attract modes (game_mode_title, game_mode_car_presentation, game_mode_demo_race, game_mode_ranking, game_mode_isdn_connection): every 8 video frames (vblank_counter >> 3) the next of 32 steps of the pattern attract_lamp_steps, two bytes a step and screen, into the screen's two lamp entries (byte 0 of checkpoint_lamp and leader_lamp by 4 * index: bits 1 and 3 of the seat's output port D, which the VBLANK writes).
comment game_mode_ranking Game mode 5, the ranking (ranking_update), after the demo race and after a game, by mode_step: 0 demo_flags bit 8 clear, the wheel motor off (wheel_motor by index), the sound off (reset_screen_sound), the screen cleared (as clear_screen), clear_published_car, the ranking's palette (ranking_palette), ranking_objects (credit_display, coin_prompt) and ranking_update started, mode_timer 270 frames (9 seconds), race game_over cleared; 1 waits until no fade runs (ranking_background fades the screen in); 2 counts the 270 frames down; 3 when no fade runs, game mode 1 (back to the title). attract_lamps_update every frame first.
comment game_mode_isdn_connection Game mode 8, on ISDN only (never in the scenarios; take_screen_inputs goes to it while link_status says the line connects, bits 2-3), by mode_step: 0 a fade out (attract_lamps_update in 0 and 1); 1 when it is over, no objects, the sound off, the screen's tilemaps, sprites and text off, palette 031740, the lamps off (checkpoint_lamp, leader_lamp by index), clear_lcd, isdn_status_update started (the list at 000F70); 2 a fade in; 3 when it is over, 4; 4 when link_status says connected (bit 0: isdn_count_players) or no longer connecting (bits 2-3 clear), mode_timer 150 frames; 5 counts them down, then a fade out; 6 when it is over, game mode 2 (the title).
comment isdn_status_update Never runs in the scenarios: the ISDN connection screen's texts (game_mode_isdn_connection starts it from the list at 000F70), on the text layer (print_text_layer), by state: 0 by link_status: bit 0 ISDN SUCCESS, THIS SIDE, OTHER SIDE and TOTAL with the players (race_copy players_this_side, players_other_side and their sum, print_hex_byte), then 1; bits 2-3 clear ISDN FAILED, then 1; else ISDN CONECTING (sic) blinking every 8 frames (its blink_counter counting); 1 nothing more.
comment isdn_count_players Never runs in the scenarios (ISDN only): the players on this side and on the other (race_copy players_this_side, players_other_side) into isdn_players_this_side and isdn_players_other_side, their sum into player_count (the race's players) and half of it into machine_count (its cabinets).
comment game_mode_play Game mode 6, a game (check_game_start starts it at step 0) and the demo's race (game_mode_demo_race, from step 10), by game_step: 0 sound 21 (queue_sound), the screen's two lamps on (button_lamps), lcd_restart_frames cleared (the LCD's timer), a fade out, race game_over cleared; 1 when the fade is over, waits until the screen's player is in the list of the race's players (from byte 1F of the race_copy record of host_player), then the host itself (not on ISDN) writes 1 to the link board's 80000A; 2 the car select: demo_flags bit 8 clear, the wheel motor off (wheel_motor), game_start_time from the backup RAM's seconds (701F10), a game counted on the seat (701F14 + 2 * index, bookkeeping_increment16), C00074 = game_start_c00074_bytes by index, the sound off (reset_screen_sound) unless the entry countdown's music plays (entry_countdown_shown), clear_screen, clear_race_state, palette car_select_palette, in_game, host_record (the host's race_copy record), the stages (screen first_stage-A6) 0, the seat's lamps on (checkpoint_lamp 2, leader_lamp 8), car_select_start started, a fade in, the screen on; 3 the car select (car_select_run goes on to 4); 4 a fade out, entry_countdown_shown cleared, the lamps on; 5 when the fade is over, race ready cleared, silence_and_clear_screen, the screen off (C0001C = 4), the stage's palette (queue_stage_load), the screen's car (player_car_object_handlers: player_car_update, its place from screen 320 + player), step 23; 23 once the car has published its state (race_copy published): race_running, the race's objects (race_object_handlers; the demo's demo_race_object_handlers), 24; 24 and 25 a frame each; 26 step 11, the input ring's places to 0, the screen on; 11 a fade in, step 6; 6 when it is over, 7; 7 the race, until its ending is over (flags bit 1, race_car_start_ending): race_running cleared, a fade out, 8; 8 when it is over, the lamps on, 9; 9 the text and the tilemaps off (clear_text_layer, reset_tile_layers), no objects, step 12; 12 the route map: short of the goal (race_copy finished clear) the sound off, the song title Meaning of the Light (start_song_title with 003504) and sound 0B; palette route_map_palette, route_map_update (route_map_object_handlers), a fade in; 13 when it is over, 14; 14 the map until START (buttons_pressed bit 6) or route_map_update sets 16; 15 (nothing sets it) 16; 16 with a new course record (course_record_beaten) a fade out and 18, else 21; 18 when the fade is over, no objects, name_entry_update started, 19; 19 a fade in, 20; 20 the name entry (name_entry_update sets 21); 21 set_player_ready and 22; 21 and 22 once every player of the race is ready (players_not_ready): a fade out, fade_out_music, 17; 17 when the fade is over, the game's end: the time played (the backup's seconds less game_start_time) added to the seat's total (701F18 + 4 * index) and kept if the longest (701F20) or the shortest (701F22), a game counted at the stage level reached (screen current_stage_level, 6 at the goal: 701F24 + 2 * it), C00074 = game_end_c00074_bytes then game_end_c00074_next_bytes by index, sound 1E, the sound and the tilemaps off, in_game, screen race_started and is_host cleared, the race record's car part cleared (race_record_clear), no objects, race game_over (the screens in the attract modes go to the ranking, race_entries_update), the lamps off, clear_lcd, 28; 28 game mode 5 (the ranking). The demo: 10 silence_and_clear_screen, clear_race_state, the stage and the car model from attract_demo_races by attract_car (the stage into screen first_stage, A1, A3, A5), host_record its own record at screen + 490 (demo_player_count 1, demo_players this screen's player, demo_stage), the model into car model and race model, palette title_palette, then as 27; 27 waits until race_copy published is clear, then as 5.
comment course_record_beaten Whether the screen's player has beaten the course's record (zero clear): when the race was finished (race_copy finished), the course (the goal's stage, screen built_stage, less 15) into record_course, and a record when the player came first (screen 320 + player is 0) in a time (screen 410 + 2 * player) under the course's (course_record_times by course).
comment load_course_records Loads the ten courses' records into course_records (6 bytes each: the car's model, the name's three letters, the time in hundredths) from the backup RAM (701F32); a course without one there (its first byte negative) gets its default from default_course_records (SGA, 5'00"00); the model is kept to its low three bits.
comment silence_and_clear_screen The screen's sound off (reset_screen_sound), then clear_screen.
comment clear_screen Clears what the screen shows: no objects (clear_objects), its tilemaps off in the mixer (reset_tile_layers), no sprites (clear_sprite_group_count, screen sprite_groups) and the text layer cleared (clear_text_layer).
comment clear_race_state Clears the race's state: the race record's car part (race_record_clear), the screen's car block and the 100 bytes after it (300 bytes from player_car), the race's order, places and times (screen + 330, 160 bytes) and the ring of views (screen + 600, 100 bytes).
comment clear_published_car Clears what the screen's car hands on to the race record (player_car_publish's), for the ranking (game_mode_ranking): race published, the next of the ring of eight views (screen view_ring_slot, race view_slot, its address in screen shown_view) with the view's place and angles cleared in it and the car (view_x-z, view_pitch, view_yaw), the wheel effects and hard acceleration (race wheel_effect_sides-53), segment_fraction, segment, road_x, window_segment (race segment_fraction-46), window_offset, segment_remaining, the horizon (screen horizon_line), and the halfword at C8 of current_object (player_car_publish's bounce_height) with race bounce_height; from the ranking current_object is what run_objects left in it, not the car.
comment staff_credits_code_control_bit Sets bit R3 of the controls being read (R1) when the value R2 is E0 or more, or C0 or more when the bit was on in the controls held before (R0): staff_credits_code_read_controls' test for the wheel turned right, the gas and the brake.
comment crc16 Returns the CRC-16 (polynomial 1021) of the R11 bytes at R10, worked out in the top half of a 32-bit register that starts at DEBDEB00, each byte entering its low byte, with eight more shifts after the last. Used for the EEPROM's settings (load_settings, eeprom_write_settings) and the program's signature (signature_checksum).
comment signature_checksum Returns the crc16 of the program's 48-byte signature text at 00010C (OUTRUNNERS EUROPA and its build date, 93.4.21), which the EEPROM keeps in settings_signature so that a new program is noticed (load_settings).
comment load_settings Loads the settings from the EEPROM at start-up (eeprom_read_settings into 20E600-20E67F) and repairs them: a block marked SEGA gets the defaults (0004C0, marked S32A) with eeprom_write_count 100; one without the S32A mark the defaults; with it, a new program (signature_checksum unlike settings_signature) gets the defaults keeping the eeprom_write_count of a sound copy (the settings or their backup, each checked with its crc16); with the same program a bad copy (or a backup unlike the settings) is rewritten from the other, both bad the defaults. Every repair but rewriting a copy also resets the backup RAM's data (bookkeeping_reset); each one saves the result (eeprom_write_settings).
comment count_eeprom_write Counts a save of the settings in eeprom_write_count, up to 10000 (the test mode warns when the EEPROM's limit of 10,000 writes is reached).
comment eeprom_lock Locks the EEPROM against writes (command 00, the 93C46's write disable) and turns the interrupts back on.
comment eeprom_read_settings Reads the EEPROM's 64 words into 20E600-20E67F (settings_magic, settings_signature, the settings from settings_checksum on, their backup from backup_settings_checksum on), with the interrupts off.
comment save_settings Saves the settings into the EEPROM after they changed (the link's, link_take_master_settings; the test mode's, test_mode_exit): eeprom_write_settings_counted, then eeprom_lock.
comment eeprom_write_settings_counted Counts the save (count_eeprom_write) and writes the settings (eeprom_write_settings).
comment eeprom_write_settings Writes the settings into the EEPROM: their checksum (crc16 of the 3A bytes from eeprom_write_count) into settings_checksum, checksum and settings copied over the backup (backup_settings_checksum on), the EEPROM unlocked (command 30, write enable) and its 64 words written from 20E600 (eeprom_write_word). Leaves the interrupts off and the EEPROM unlocked (save_settings locks it).
comment eeprom_read_word Reads the EEPROM word of read command R4 (eeprom_send_command): 18 clock pulses collecting the data out (bit 7 of R12, port F of the second I/O chip), the 16-bit word into R0; then chip select off.
comment eeprom_write_word Writes R0 into the EEPROM word of write command R4 (eeprom_send_command): its 16 bits high first, chip select dropped to start the write, then waits, clocking, until the data out (bit 7 of R12) says it is done.
comment eeprom_send_command Sends command R4 to the EEPROM (a 93C46 on port H of the second I/O chip, R13: bit 7 data in, 6 clock, 5 chip select): chip select, a start bit, then the 8 bits of R4 high first (two of opcode: 2 read, 1 write, 0 with the address's top bits 3 write enable or 0 write disable; six of address), each with a clock pulse.
comment eeprom_begin Starts an EEPROM access: the interrupts off, R13 the EEPROM's lines (C8000E, port H of the second I/O chip), R12 its data out (C8000A, port F, bit 7), R2 the lines' present value.
comment eeprom_set_lines Writes R2 to the EEPROM's lines (R13), with bit 4 set too.
comment eeprom_read_one_word Unused: reads the EEPROM's word R4 (its read command, R4 | 80) into R0 and turns the interrupts back on.
comment reload_settings Unused: reads the settings from the EEPROM again (eeprom_read_settings) and turns the interrupts back on.
comment eeprom_write_one_word Unused: writes R0 into the EEPROM's word R4 (its write command, R4 | 40) without unlocking it first, and turns the interrupts back on.

field screen 98 game_start_time u32

field screen 9D record_course u8
field screen E1 start_lamp_blink u8
field screen E2 entry_countdown_shown u8
field screen 100 wheel u8
field screen 101 gas u8
field screen 102 brake u8
field screen 108 input_write_place u8
field screen 109 input_read_place u8
field screen 110 buttons u32
field screen 114 previous_buttons u32
field screen 118 buttons_pressed u32
field screen 11C buttons_released u32
field screen 31A in_game u8
field screen 31B race_running u8
field screen 4AF demo_player_count u8
field screen 4B0 demo_players u8
field screen 4F2 demo_stage u8
field race 28 game_over u8
field race 5C ready u8
field race 5F published u8
20E004  var  waiting_task_stack u32
20E023  var  frame_ready u8
20E024  var  single_task u8
20E026  var  game_frame_count u8
20E02B  var  second_task_waiting u8

# ======== the race's logic, the link between cabinets, the draw modes and the LCD (naming batch H) ========

0186FC  func  lcd_start
# A line of the LCD's text, 16 characters, as the screens keep it (screen lcd_text, four words at a time):
# lcd_start and lcd_reset copy lcd_boot_text into both screens' with
# "movea.b 18B90[PC], R10" (01875F, 01883D) and "mov.d [R10+]"
struct lcd_text_line 10 rom
field lcd_text_line 00 text_0_to_3 u32
field lcd_text_line 04 text_4_to_7 u32
field lcd_text_line 08 text_8_to_11 u32
field lcd_text_line 0C text_12_to_15 u32
018B90  table lcd_boot_text  lcd_text_line   # census ROM 018B90-018B9F ("SEGA Enterprises")
# The rows of the LCD's custom characters, eight a character, as lcd_start sends them after command 40: "movea.b
# 18B4E[PC], R10" (018791), "mov.b [R10+], R0" while not negative; census ROM 018B4E-018B8E (8 characters, the
# DJ's face among them; FF closes them)
elements lcd_character_rows u8
018B4E  table lcd_custom_characters  lcd_character_rows
0187A3  func  lcd_write_character
0187C1  func  lcd_write_data_nibble
0187F0  func  lcd_command_nibble_after_pause
0187F8  func  lcd_write_command_nibble
018827  func  lcd_reset
018863  func  lcd_refresh
0188BE  func  lcd_send_next_nibble
018B21  table lcd_restart_nibbles  u8   # lcd_send_next_nibble "not.b 18B21[PC](R0), R0" (0189A4) by lcd_init_step, from 2C down to 1: the LCDs' start sequence, a nibble each timer interrupt; census ROM 018B22-018B4D (C3 CB 03 0B 23 2B 33 3B 33 3B 33 ... 3B)
0189D9  func  lcd_update
018A70  table lcd_scroll_step_jumps  u32   # lcd_update (018A6E "jmp [R1]") jumps through it by screen lcd_scroll_step: 018A80 018AA5 018AC2 018AD8
018AE8  table dj_face_characters  u16   # lcd_update "mov.h 18AE8[PC](R0), [R10+]" (0189FA; the closed mouth, the first, 018A17) by mouth shape: the two custom characters of the DJ's face into the LCD's text; census ROM 018AE8-018AEF (0201 0403 0605 0403)
# The mouth shapes of a DJ message (screen dj_mouth_shapes, F30: the low 24 bits of its typed_messages
# record): lcd_update "movz.bw [R11+], R0" (0189F3) every third frame, a shape of dj_face_characters each, a
# negative byte closes them
elements dj_mouth_shape_numbers u8
local lcd_update 0189F3 R0 mouth_shape
comment distance_offset_from_x Unused (only distance_offset_functions lists it, for octants 1, 2, 5 and 6 of an angle): the distance along angle R0 that has the x distance from R11's x (+4) to the object's field 20 (that distance / the sine of R0), turned to angle R1 by distance_offset_at_angle. Its C has no body: nothing reads the results (R2, R3).
comment distance_offset_from_z Unused (only distance_offset_functions lists it, for octants 0, 3, 4 and 7 of an angle): the distance along angle R0 that has the z distance from R11's z (+8) to the object's field 28 (that distance / the cosine of R0), turned to angle R1 by distance_offset_at_angle. Its C has no body: nothing reads the results (R2, R3).
comment distance_offset_at_angle Unused (the end of distance_offset_from_x and distance_offset_from_z): R2 / R6 as a length along angle R1, its sine part into R2 and its cosine part into R3. Its C has no body: nothing reads the results.
comment wheel_effect_1_none The wheel at road_x plus the model's half track (handling +1A) is within the bounds of both surface entries: no effect (player_car_road_drag's pick, wheel_effect_1_jumps).
comment wheel_effect_1_surface_1_inner wheel_effect_sides bit 0 and wheel_effect_1 = the effect (+6) of surface_entry_1 past its inner bound (+2): the wheel at road_x plus the half track is past it and no other bound (player_car_road_drag's pick, wheel_effect_1_jumps).
comment wheel_effect_1_surface_1_outer wheel_effect_sides bit 0 and wheel_effect_1 = the effect (+7) of surface_entry_1 past its outer bound (+4), when the wheel at road_x plus the half track is past it and not past the bounds of surface_entry_2.
comment wheel_effect_1_surface_2_inner wheel_effect_sides bit 0 and wheel_effect_1 = the effect (+6) of surface_entry_2 past its inner bound (+2), when the wheel at road_x plus the half track is past it but not past its outer bound.
comment wheel_effect_1_surface_2_outer wheel_effect_sides bit 0 and wheel_effect_1 = the effect (+7) of surface_entry_2 past its outer bound (+4): for the wheel at road_x plus the half track it comes before every other.
comment wheel_effect_2_none The wheel at road_x less the model's half track is within the bounds of both surface entries: no effect (player_car_road_drag's pick, wheel_effect_2_jumps).
comment wheel_effect_2_surface_1_inner wheel_effect_sides bit 1 and wheel_effect_2 = the effect (+6) of surface_entry_1 past its inner bound (+2), when the wheel at road_x less the half track is past it but not past its outer bound.
comment wheel_effect_2_surface_1_outer wheel_effect_sides bit 1 and wheel_effect_2 = the effect (+7) of surface_entry_1 past its outer bound (+4): for the wheel at road_x less the half track it comes before every other.
comment wheel_effect_2_surface_2_inner wheel_effect_sides bit 1 and wheel_effect_2 = the effect (+6) of surface_entry_2 past its inner bound (+2), when the wheel at road_x less the half track passes no bound of surface_entry_1 nor the outer one of surface_entry_2.
comment wheel_effect_2_surface_2_outer wheel_effect_sides bit 1 and wheel_effect_2 = the effect (+7) of surface_entry_2 past its outer bound (+4), when the wheel at road_x less the half track passes no bound of surface_entry_1.
comment network_check_again The network check after the test mode: network_check, or in ISDN mode (isdn_mode) the link board off for a moment, the link's variables (from machine_count), the board's memory at 800004 and 800600, this machine's two race records and all the players' records cleared, then network_check_isdn.
comment network_check The network check at boot: the link board off (801000), the link's variables, the board's first bytes and all the race records cleared; with the STAND-ALONE setting (stand_alone into link_off) only network_check_stand_alone. Else it shows NETWORK CHECK (MASTER, SLAVE or ISDN) and BOARD CHECK, tests the board's memory (network_check_board_memory), writes its greeting V70 and turns the board on, waits for it (network_check_wait_board), checks the board's answer Z80 (network_check_greeting), waits again, shows the board's self-test (network_check_four_ics); in ISDN mode goes on to network_check_isdn; else gives the board this machine's role (link_master at 800004), waits, shows the self-test again (network_check_five_ics), waits for the board's flag (801002 bit 7) with CHECK NETWORK STATUS blinking, gives it this machine's number (800002 to 800E20) and FF at 800006, shows CHECK TRANS END until the board's 800008 is set (link_lost if 80000C is), and goes on to network_check_join. The test button (test_mode_on) gives it up at any wait (link_failed FF).
comment network_check_join Takes the link from the board: machine_count from its byte at 800000, player_count twice it, this machine's number from 800002, the board's flag 801002 set; with no machine on the link (and not in ISDN mode) network_check_stand_alone; else the two screens' car numbers from the cabinet ID (cabinet_id * 2 and + 1, into screen car_number and race car_number), then network_check_result with this machine's number.
comment network_check_isdn The network check's ISDN mode: the link board off for a moment, 1 into its byte at 800014 (0 in network mode), its flag clear, the board on, network_check_wait_isdn, then network_check_join.
comment link_lost The link has failed (the board's error byte 80000C, seen by network_check, link_receive_records or link_send_records): link_failed 1, then network_check_stand_alone (which shows COMMUNICATION FAILED. for 128 frames).
comment network_check_stand_alone Sets the machine up alone: link_off, one machine and two players (machine_count, player_count), car numbers 0 and 1 for the two screens (screen and race car_number), then network_check_result as machine 1.
comment network_check_result Sets up the machine's two players from its number R0 (1-4): both race records' machine, their players ((R0 - 1) * 2 and + 1, also screen player), the screens' race_copy (race_copy_offsets by player: 20FC00 + 80 each) and race (20FB00, 20FB80); machine 1 also puts its settings (advertise_sound, steering_wheel_reaction, coins_per_credit, first_chute_multiplier, bonus_adder, coin_setting, free_play, game_difficulty, time_difficulty[0-5]) at 03-10 of its first record (the master_ fields; second_chute_multiplier is not sent) and its course records (the backup's, 701F32) at 30. Then NETWORK CHECK and the result for 128 frames: COMMUNICATION FAILED. (link_failed), THIS MACHINE IS ISDN MODE., THIS MACHINE IS STAND-ALONE., or SLAVE / MASTER CONTROLLER (the board's 800004) with COMMUNICATION SUCCESS. and THIS MACHINE ID IS n (the board's 800002); the text cleared.
comment network_check_board_memory Tests the link board's memory (network_check_memory_test over 7FF of its bytes); on a fault the screens off, CONNECTION ERROR and network_check_error.
comment network_check_memory_test A memory test: eight passes (seed 01, 02, ... 80), each writing a pattern from the seed into R1 bytes (one a halfword) from R10, reading them back and gathering the bits that differ into R0's low byte (0: no fault); CHECK DUALPORT RAM flips on the status line after each pass (network_check_toggle_memory_text).
comment network_check_wait_board Waits for the link board's flag (801002 bit 7), WAIT blinking, for up to E10 frames (two minutes, link_wait_frames): then the screens off, COMMUNICATION TIME OUT and network_check_error; the test button gives the check up (link_failed FF); the status line cleared after.
comment network_check_wait_isdn ISDN mode: waits, WAIT blinking, until the link board's byte 800000 (its machine count) is set, then 60 frames more (link_wait_frames); the test button gives the check up (link_failed FF); the status line cleared after.
comment network_check_greeting Checks the greetings on the link board: V70 (this program's, at 800000) and Z80 (the board's answer, at 800010); otherwise the screens off, CONNECTION ERROR and network_check_error.
comment network_check_five_ics The link board's self-test (its byte 80000C), the second time: clear, IC17, IC23, IC11, IC10 and IC9 GOOD (network_check_show_ics); else the screens off, COMMUNICATION BOARD ERROR and network_check_four_ics, which, the byte still set, shows four of the ICs and halts there (the five after it only show if the byte clears meanwhile), then network_check_halt.
comment network_check_four_ics The link board's self-test (its byte 80000C), the first time: clear, IC17, IC23, IC11 and IC10 GOOD (network_check_show_ics); else the screens off, COMMUNICATION BOARD ERROR with each of the four GOOD or BAD, the screens on, and network_check_halt.
comment network_check_show_ics Shows R0 of the link board's ICs (IC17, IC23, IC11, IC10, IC9), each GOOD or BAD by its bit in R2 (link_board_ic_texts).
comment network_check_blink_text Blinks a text record (at current_object: its cell, attribute and characters) by flipping it into the text layer every 16th call (network_check_blink_counter): WAIT, CHECK NETWORK STATUS, NETWORK ERROR.
comment network_check_clear_status_line Clears row 24 of the text layer, where the network check's waits blink their texts.
comment network_check_toggle_memory_text Flips CHECK DUALPORT RAM on or off on the status line (row 24), once each pass of network_check_memory_test.
comment network_check_error Shows the error R19 (a text list: CONNECTION ERROR, COMMUNICATION TIME OUT...) on a cleared text layer with the screens on, then halts like network_check_halt.
comment network_check_halt Blinks NETWORK ERROR until the test button (test_mode_on) is pressed; then link_failed FF and the network check is given up (it returns past its caller).
comment link_take_master_settings After the network check (unless the test button is down): sends and receives the race records (link_send_records, 5 frames, link_receive_records; in ISDN mode once the board's 8006E1 is set, link_isdn_receive_records), keeps each player's car number (race car_number into car_numbers), takes the first player's (the master's) course records into the backup (701F32 with its checksum, 011418, and the copy at 701FAE) and into course_records (001D84), and adopts its settings (record +03-10 into advertise_sound, steering_wheel_reaction, coins_per_credit, first_chute_multiplier, bonus_adder, coin_setting, free_play, game_difficulty, time_difficulty[0-5]; second_chute_multiplier is not taken; 011018 and 01139E when any of the coin settings changes, all saved by 00036B when any changes).
comment race_entries_update The entries to a race, each frame. Player 0's screen keeps them in its record: once every entry is taken (it means to wait for each record's entry_serial_seen, but compares player 0's own each time), a player's entry_request not yet accepted (accepted_players) is accepted: entrant = that player, entry_serial + 1, and the first one starts entry_frames at 600 (entry_frames_by_machines), cut to 40 once every player is in; entry_frames counting down to 0 closes the entries (entrant FF, entry_serial + 1, accepted_players cleared, 1 to the link board's 80000A). Every screen: when some player's race is over (race game_over) an idle screen (game steps 1-5) goes to step 5 (cleared_on_race_over cleared), and while player 0's entry_frames runs to step 7 (entry_invitation_step); each new entry_serial of player 0's record (entry_serial_seen) adds its entrant to the screen's entrants (entrant_count), or the close clears them (and entry_accepted); the screen's own player accepted: its entry_request cleared, entry_accepted, the first entrant into host_player and is_host when that is itself; the host keeps racer_count and race_order in its record from the entrants.
comment race_progress_update The race's progress, each frame of a race (screen in_game). The host (is_host) runs it in its record: once every racer has taken the last pass (pass_seen = pass_count), a racer whose stage_count changed (against counted_stages) has passed a checkpoint (stage_count - 1): pass_player, pass_checkpoint, pass_order (counted_passes), pass_branch (the side of the road it was on: race road_x bit 15), pass_time (race_time in hundredths plus the order), pass_count + 1; the first to pass a checkpoint before the goal adds the time of the stage its branch leads to (stage_start_times by fork_next_stages[stage * 2 + branch]) plus the setting for that checkpoint (master_time_difficulty) to time_left. Once the race has started (race_started) race_time + 34 and time_left - 1 a frame, and one pass of sorting race_order: the finished (race finished) first by finish_time, then by distance (race segment_fraction-43). Every screen from the host's record (host_record): the leader's segment and stage (leader_window_segment, leader_stage), each player's place (places, its own in place); each new pass: pass_seen (screen and race), checkpoints_passed, checkpoint_passes, the order and the time by player and by order (checkpoint_orders, checkpoint_order, checkpoint_times_by_player, checkpoint_times); the first car past a fork decides the route: forks_decided + 1, its branch in route_branches and the stage it leads to in route_stages.
comment entry_invitation_step Game step 7, an idle seat while another one's game takes entries (race_entries_update sends it here), by screen game_mode's byte 2: 0 screen fade_kind = 0 and flags bit 0; 1 once that bit has cleared: the seat's sound channel (012004), the screen's objects (011C60), layers (01070C), sprite count (010889) and text layer (01076A) set afresh, its race record's race part cleared (race_record_clear), not the demo (demo_flags bit 8), the wheel motor off (wheel_motor), both of the seat's lamps blinking (bytes C0-C3 and C8-CB of its entry at checkpoint_lamp); 2 starts entry_invitation_object_handlers (entry_countdown_start, credit_display_update, coin_prompt_update): the invitation to join with the entry time left; 3 the seat's screen on (its I/O chip's CNT, 6 or 86), fade_kind = 1, flags bit 0; 4 once player 0's entry_frames is over: the sound channel again (012004), step 2, the lamps off, the LCD cleared (clear_lcd).
comment race_record_clear Clears bytes 30-7F of the screen's race record (the race's part: the car's published state, the checkpoint passes, the times), from the zeros at 100800.
comment course_records_update Takes the players' new course records, each frame: when a player's record_serial changes (against name_entries_seen), its time (finish_time) on its course (record_course) at or under the course's record (course_records, 6 bytes a course: model, the three letters of the name, the time) replaces it with its record_model and record_name (the first letter) and record_name_rest (the other two), also in the two copies in the backup (701F32 with its checksum, 011418, and 701FAE).
comment link_receive_records Takes the race records of all the players into 20FC00 (80 bytes each, by player), each frame: alone (link_off, link_failed) this machine's own two (20FB00, 20FB80); in ISDN mode the eight from 21E000; on the link the eight from the link board (link_copy_records_in from 800020, or 800720 when its byte 80000E is set), or link_lost when its error byte 80000C is set.
comment link_isdn_receive_records ISDN mode: copies the eight race records from the link board (800600) to 21E000, where link_receive_records takes them; the link interrupt (int_vector_44) does it on the board's command 3.
comment link_copy_records_in Copies R1 race records from the link board's memory at R10 (a byte in each halfword) to R11: their first 70 bytes, 80 apart.
comment link_send_records Sends this machine's two race records to the link board, each frame (not in ISDN mode, nor alone): their first 70 bytes to 800E20 and 800F00 (a byte in each halfword), then FF to its byte 800006; link_lost when its error byte 80000C is set. link_wait_frames from link_wait_frames_start (which nothing sets).
comment draw_object_between_segments Draw mode 2: the object between the road's segment at its position (field 1C, counted from the view drawn: race_copy window_segment) and the next, by the fraction in field 28 (80ths), offset across and up by fields 20 and 24 scaled by each segment's scale (draw_object_at_scale). Its size, meant as the two segments' scales by the fraction, multiplies them by the second point's offsets instead (R4 and R5 used twice): a slip in the game's code; nothing in the scenarios draws with it.
comment draw_object_from_road_edge Draw mode 5: the object on the road's surface between its segment (field 1C) and the next by the fraction in field 28, across at field 20 from the road's edge on side field 3F (the segment's half width times the side), its size the two segments' scales by the fraction shifted by scale_shift (draw_object_at_shifted_scale).
comment draw_object_from_road_edge_unshifted Draw mode 7: as draw_object_from_road_edge, at the size of scale_shift 0 whatever the object's.
comment draw_object_from_road_edge_doubled Draw mode B (the traffic cars'): as draw_object_from_road_edge, at twice the size of draw_object_from_road_edge_unshifted (scale_shift 1) whatever the object's.
comment draw_object_at_car_window_segment Draw mode 4: as draw_object_at_segment, but the segment counted from the screen car's own window_segment instead of the view drawn (race_copy window_segment).
comment draw_object_at_segment Draw mode 9: the object at the road's segment of its position (field 1C, counted from the view drawn: race_copy window_segment), offset across and up by fields 20 and 24 scaled by the segment's scale, its size that scale shifted by scale_shift; not drawn when the segment is behind or not projected.
comment draw_object_at_car_window_segment_on_road Draw mode 3: as draw_object_at_car_window_segment, on the road's surface (no up offset).
comment draw_object_at_segment_scale Draws the object at R0, R1 at the projected segment R11's own scale (draw_object_at_shifted_scale).
comment draw_object_at_shifted_scale Draws the object at R0, R1 at the scale R2 shifted by its scale_shift (and >> 4) (draw_object_at_scale).
comment draw_object_at_scale Places a road object projected at R0, R1 with size R2 (both scales): x and y; off the screen (x beyond 1A0 or -1A0, y beyond 80 or -160) back to draw_object's caller undrawn; the depth_layer of the projected segment R11 (+8), then put_object_sprites.
comment draw_object_on_screen Draw mode 0: the object at x, y on the screen, less the screen's scroll (screen sprite_origin_x, sprite_origin_y), at its width_scale and height_scale (put_object_sprites).
comment put_object_sprites Writes the object's frame (animation, frame) into the screen's sprite list at R10 and its copy at + 3000, a sprite of 8 halfwords for each piece: its word flipped by flags bits 6-7, its size from 0x122B74 by size_step scaled by R2 (height) and R3 (width), placed at R0, R1 plus its offsets scaled the same way (signs flipped by the flags: sprite_flip_x_signs, sprite_flip_y_signs), its palette word from sprite_palettes by palette; R10 past them.
comment lcd_start Starts the cabinets' LCDs at boot (both, through port G of the two I/O chips): the 4-bit start (command nibbles 3, 3, 3, 2), display on (0C), SEGA Enterprises as both screens' text (lcd_text), command 40 then the eight custom characters (lcd_custom_characters: the DJ's face among them); the screens' lamps off (screen button_lamps), lcd_restart_frames E10.
comment lcd_write_character Writes the data byte R0 to both LCDs, its high nibble then its low one (lcd_write_data_nibble), with pauses.
comment lcd_write_data_nibble Writes the high nibble of R0 to both LCDs as data (port G of the two I/O chips, inverted: the data lines, register select set, then the enable strobe bit 3 dropped).
comment lcd_command_nibble_after_pause A pause, then lcd_write_command_nibble.
comment lcd_write_command_nibble Writes the high nibble of R0 to both LCDs as a command (register select clear), then drops the enable strobe (bit 3).
comment lcd_reset After the test mode: both screens' LCD text back to SEGA Enterprises (lcd_text), their lamps off (screen button_lamps), and lcd_restart_frames 0 so that the next VBLANK starts the LCDs again.
comment lcd_refresh Each VBLANK: counts lcd_restart_frames down and, when it runs out, has the LCDs started again (lcd_init_step 2C, E10 VBLANKs to the next, a minute); else, unless a nibble is being written (lcd_busy), after a byte cut in half (an odd lcd_send_step) writes one more command nibble (0 or 4) to keep the LCDs in step, takes both screens' lcd_text into lcd_send_buffer_1 and lcd_send_buffer_2 and starts sending them (lcd_send_step 0).
comment lcd_send_next_nibble One nibble to the LCDs each timer interrupt (int_vector_43), with the other bits of port G (port_g_outputs, the lamps): while lcd_init_step counts, the start sequence (lcd_restart_nibbles, from the end), then idle (lcd_send_step 22); else by lcd_send_step: the cursor home command (80), then the 16 characters of lcd_send_buffer_1 to screen 1's LCD and of lcd_send_buffer_2 to screen 2's, a nibble at a time; lcd_busy while it writes the port.
comment lcd_update The screen's LCD, each frame. With the DJ's face on (dj_face) its first two characters are the face, its mouth the next shape of dj_mouth_shapes every third frame (dj_mouth_frames; dj_face_characters, pairs of custom characters), closed when the string ends (a negative byte); the rest of the line (all 16 without the face) scrolls a text: a new lcd_scroll_request clears the line and starts lcd_scroll, which shifts the text in from the right a character every 5 frames (lcd_scroll_frames; ` shows custom character 7), then shifts spaces in until the text has gone (lcd_scroll_step 0-3).

field screen 3C0 checkpoint_times_by_player u16[30]   # by checkpoint (8 players each), then player: lap_timer_update, checkpoint_board_update; checkpoint 5's are the finish times (410 + 2 * player: goal_update, player_car_update)
field screen 420 checkpoint_times u16[30]   # by checkpoint (8 each), then the order the cars passed it in

# ======== the sprite lists, the boot of the video, unpacking, fades, coins, bookkeeping and sound (naming batch I) ========
00F04E  func  draw_object_one_piece
00F104  func  draw_object_entry
00F126  func  draw_object_command
00F15C  func  draw_object_smoothed
00F396  func  finish_sprite_list
010243  func  note_road_crest

0113C0  func  bookkeeping_check

01140E  func  bookkeeping_copy

01143E  func  bookkeeping_checksum

011868  func  print_text_layer_list

01192B  func  print_hex_digits
011A72  func  draw_text_sprites

011E88  func  start_sound_program
011FE6  func  fade_out_music
011FEA  func  fade_out_music_at
012004  func  reset_screen_sound
comment draw_object_one_piece Draw mode 8: the first piece of the animation's frame at its own size (no zoom, no flip), at x, y on the screen (less sprite_origin_x, sprite_origin_y), with the first word of its palette (sprite_palettes), into the sprite list and its halfway copy alike.
comment draw_object_entry Draw mode 1: copies a ready 16-byte sprite entry, from the address in fields 38-3B (animation and palette read as one 32-bit address), into the sprite list and its halfway copy, with the screen's bit in its fourth word (sprite_screen_bit). Not run in the scenarios.
comment draw_object_command Draw mode 6: copies a ready 16-byte entry from the address in fields 38-3B into the sprite list and its halfway copy as it is, without the screen's bit: for list commands such as crest_clip_update's clip.
comment draw_object_smoothed Draw mode A: every piece of the animation's frame at x, y on the screen (less sprite_origin_x, sprite_origin_y), zoomed by width_scale and height_scale, flipped by flags bits 6-7 (with bit 7 the pieces' offsets are negated, smoothed_flip_signs), each with its palette word; the halfway copy (+3000) is drawn from the averages of this frame's x, y, width_scale and height_scale and the last frame's, kept in the ring of sprite places (next_sprite_place, 16 bytes a record; the object's field 50 points at its record) when flags bit 5 says it was drawn the frame before. Keeps this frame's values in a new record and sets flags bit 5.
comment finish_sprite_list Finishes the screen's sprite list in the vertical blank (int_vector_40): picks a record of sprite_list_switches by the screen (index) and sprite_list_buffers; when groups were drawn (sprite_groups), chains the depth lists from layer 0 to 415 (depth_lists: the list's start entry jumps to the first layer's newest group, each layer's tail to the next layer's head; the heads cleared), in the list and its halfway copy, and ends the chain with the record's word (a jump on to sprite entry 3, screen 2's list, after screen 1's; entry 4, the end, after screen 2's); points sprite RAM entries 2 and 3 (the jumps to each screen's list) at the halfway copy of the list just finished and at the other screen's finished list; then the next buffer (sprite_list_buffers, sprite_list_last, sprite_list_limit) and sprite_groups 0.
comment init_sprite_chip Sets up the sprite chip at boot: its control registers 500000-50000C from 010B0A-010B0F (rendering every frame), the buffer's flip bits inverted when the screen is flipped (flip_screen), then init_sprite_lists.
comment init_sprite_lists Sets up the sprite lists: sprite RAM entries 0-4 (0 clips to the whole screen, 1 sets the offset sprites can add, x A0 y DE (sprite_origin_x, sprite_origin_y), and jumps to 2; 2 and 3, the jumps to screen 1's and screen 2's lists, and 4 an end), an end at the start of the eight list buffers (404000, 40A000, 414000, 41A000 and their halfway copies 3000 on), each screen's list at its first buffer (sprite_list_last 404000 and 414000, sprite_list_buffers 0), its ring of sprite places at 20C000 and 21C000 (next_sprite_place), sprite_groups 0, and sprite_lists_set_up = 1 (nothing reads it).
comment clear_sprite_group_count Sets the count of sprite groups drawn this frame (sprite_groups) to 0: unless something is drawn after it, the vertical blank (finish_sprite_list) ends the screen's list at its start, without sprites. Used as the game modes start.
comment draw_text_sprites Writes a sprite per character of the string at R18 (closed by 0) into the sprite list and its halfway copy alike: words 0-3 from R1 and R2 (an 8x8 piece, with the screen's bit), the place R3 (y, x; x 8 more each character), the graphics R4 + 8 * the character, the attribute R5; then links them into depth layer 19E (screen 5678, drawn after nearly all the others) and counts them (sprite_groups). Returns the address after the string. The end of draw_text and draw_text_at.
comment load_sprite_ram_tables Copies data into sprite RAM outside the lists at boot: 1E0 bytes from 12297C to 410000, 2400 bytes from 107020 to 401000, and twelve tables of sixteen halfwords from 010C44 to 403400 (laid out as the indirect colour tables sprites can take from sprite RAM).
comment note_road_crest Notes a crest of the road while player_car_project_road projects it, for crest_clip_update: when it is the first, or higher on the screen (R27) than the last one noted, crest_count goes up, its depth layer R28 goes into crest_layers (at the old count, with 3 in the next place) and its height into crest_heights (at the new count; entry 0 is the bottom of the screen).
comment init_screen Sets up the constants of the screen's block at boot: index (0 for the block at 208000, 1 at 218000) and by it (screen_hardware_records) sprite_screen_bit, palette_ram, mixer and io_chip; eye_distance, draw_distance (the second of the draw distances at 010BE2), sprite_origin_x, sprite_origin_y and view_pitch_base from 010BCC (010BC8 holds the draw distances' address); and stage_lengths: for each of the 31 roads of stage_courses (the courses player_car_start_course starts by screen first_stage) the sum of its sections' lengths (the first halfword of each 6-byte record; the negative ones are commands: -1 goes on at the address it holds, -4 is 4 bytes long, -2 and -6 end it), and branch_road_length (14A) after them.
comment clear_screen_ram Clears the screen's work RAM at boot: E000 bytes from 8000 below its block (the objects and their lists) to 6000 into it.
comment init_mixer Sets the screen's mixer (registers 00-4E at mixer) and its two copies in the screen's block (mixer_values, mixer_defaults) to the defaults at 010AB6, with the other screen's two tile layers turned off (010B06: NBG1 and NBG3 on screen 1, NBG0 and NBG2 on screen 2; the two screens share video RAM, each showing two of the four layers).
comment init_io_chips Sets up the two I/O chips at boot: ports D, G and H outputs and cleared (the coin meters, lamps, LCD, the tile bank and the EEPROM's lines), each chip's screen on and the sound CPU out of reset (CNT 6 and 86), and the first bank of the wheels' and pedals' A/D inputs (C00060).
comment upload_boot_palettes Uploads three blocks of colours straight into the screen's palette RAM at boot (upload_palette): 256 colours at 9E00 (03153C), 16 at 9C00 (031740) and 2560 at 8000 (153904).
comment init_video Sets up video RAM: cleared (clear_video_ram), the text font (load_text_font), the video control register 31FF00 (0800; 0A00 flipped, flip_screen) and the registers 31FF02-31FF8F from 010B12, with their copy at video_registers, the four tile layers' scroll targets (scroll_targets-20E2AF) cleared, then reset_tile_layers.
comment reset_tile_layers Puts the screen's two tile layers (NBG0 and NBG2 for screen 1, NBG1 and NBG3 for screen 2) back at page 0 and scroll 0: their page selects (video_registers[32], video_registers[36]) and scroll targets (scroll_targets, scroll_targets[4]) cleared, and bits 12-15 of the video control register (31FF00, video_registers) cleared. Used as the game modes start.
comment clear_video_ram Clears video RAM below its registers (300000-31FEFF): the tilemap pages, the text layer and its font, the bitmap and the line tables.
comment clear_text_layer Clears the text layer's first 30 rows (64 cells each, 31F000-31FEFF).
comment load_text_font Copies the text layer's characters (3000 bytes, 384 characters of 8x8 pixels) from 104020 into video RAM at 318000.
comment print_text_layer Writes a string on the text layer: from the record R19, the cell's address (310000 + its first halfword), an attribute byte (the cells' high byte: palette) and the characters, closed by 0; returns the address after it.
comment print_hex_byte Never runs in the scenarios (ISDN only): writes a number's low byte as two hex digits on the text layer, ending before the cell it is given, with the attribute byte it is given (print_hex_digits).
comment print_hex_digits Writes a number in hex on the text layer, in as many cells as it is told, ending before the cell it is given, the lowest digit last: each digit a character ('0'-'9', 'A'-'F') with the attribute byte it is given; when the number runs out, the cells left before it are cleared (0). Only print_hex_byte runs it; the entries for four, six and eight digits before it (print_hex_halfword, print_hex_24_bits, print_hex_word) are unused.
comment print_text_layer_list Writes the strings of the list R19 on the text layer (print_text_layer each) until an FFFF halfword. Not run in the scenarios.
comment unpack Unpacks the packed data at R10 into R11: flag bits read the highest first, 8 to a byte as needed; 1 copies a byte; 01 takes a halfword (an offset back of its value >> 3, -1 to -8192, and a length of its low three bits + 2, or when they are 0 the next byte + 1; a 0 byte ends the data); 00 takes two bits more (a length of 2-5) and a byte (an offset back of -1 to -256); a length copies that many bytes from that far back in what has been unpacked.
comment unpack_queued_step Unpacks a little of the screen's queued tile pages (queue_tile_pages), from the frame loop's wait while unpack_state says there is work: with none under way, takes the next request of the ring unpack_queue (unpack_read_place; unpack_state 0 when it is empty); else goes on where it stopped, by unpack_state's second byte: FF one byte of a match (unpack_copy_source, unpack_copy_count), 1 one step of unpack's format (unpack_flags, unpack_flags_left, unpack_source, unpack_destination): a byte, or a match's first byte.
comment unpack_tile_pages Unpacks a list of tilemap pages into video RAM at once (unpack): each 32-bit entry a page number in its top byte (the page at 300000 + 400 * page) and the address of its packed data below, closed by a negative entry; the list format of queue_tile_pages.
comment step_screen_fade Steps the screen's fade while the screen's flags bit 0 is set (vblank_update_mixer, every other vertical blank): a fade asked for by fade_kind (0 to black, 1 from black, 2 to white, 3 from white, 4 to a red tint, 5 back from it; records at 01173A) sets fade_level, fade_step, fade_end and fade_channels and fade_kind 6 (under way); then each time the level moves a step (6 bits: 20 is -32, black; 1F is +31, white) and goes into the mixer's colour offset 0 in mixer_values (registers 40-44: red, green, blue, those fade_channels has; register 3E's high byte C0), which vblank_update_mixer copies to the mixer; flags bit 0 is cleared when the level reaches fade_end.
comment clear_objects Ends all of the screen's objects at once: every slot free (screen - 7FF0), both object lists empty (this frame's at - 7E00, the next one's at - 7D00, with run_objects' pointers to them at - 8000, - 7FFC, - 7FF4 and - 7FF8), and the queue of palette uploads (palette_upload_count) emptied. At boot and as each game mode starts.
comment start_sound_program Starts the sound at boot: the screen's ring of sound requests with one request of sound 0 waiting (800 the place read, 801 the place written, the requests from 802), I/O chip 0's CNT 6 (its screen on, the sound CPU out of reset), screen byte 822 cleared, and 701FFF cleared, which lets the sound program's main loop run.
comment fade_out_music Fades out the screen's music with 20 (fade_out_music_at): a step every 4 x (20 + 1) of the sound program's ticks.
comment fade_out_music_at Fades out the screen's music: R0 into byte 18 of the screen's first track in the shared sound RAM (700918 for screen 1, 701118 for screen 2; music_fade_addresses), which has the sound program lower the music's volume, from its last CC 10, by one every 4 x (R0 + 1) of its ticks; at 0 it stops the music's player and its MultiPCM voices (CC 50 7E). 20 from fade_out_music, 3F and 7F from car_radio_update, 7F from goal_update.
comment reset_screen_sound Sends sound command 1F straight to the screen's request byte in the shared sound RAM (700700 + 100 * screen, without going through the ring of requests) and mutes the car's engine (710 = 0, 712 bit 5, as set_tyre_noise does in the silent demo). As the game modes start. Command 1F plays song 03, then FF: song 03 sends CC 50 7F on channel E, which stops every MultiPCM voice of the screen, and FF clears the screen's eight sequence players (music and effects); FM notes go on.
comment update_coins The coins and service credits, in the vertical blank unless on free play (free_play): queues each seat's pending coin sound (4E, seat1_coin_sounds, seat2_coin_sounds) and service sound (50, seat1_service_sounds, seat2_service_sounds), one each vertical blank; SERVICE newly pressed (switches_pressed bits 0-1) gives that seat a credit (add_service_credit), counted in the bookkeeping's service credits (701F04); a coin chute let go (switches_released bits 2-3 seat 1's two chutes, 4-5 seat 2's) counts its coin (insert_coin, entries at 0114D4) and the credits it gives in the bookkeeping (701F02); then drives the coin meters (pulse_coin_meter: each seat's first, and its second unless the chutes are shared, chute_entry_offset).
comment pulse_coin_meter Drives coin meter R1 (its bit in the port D outputs at R12, port_d_outputs: bit 0 a seat's first chute's meter, bit 1 its second's) from its record R7 in coin_meters (frames on, frames off, pulses owed): when idle with a pulse owed, the bit goes on for 6 vertical blanks and off for 6. Then writes the credits into the bookkeeping and its backup (write_bookkeeping_credits) with the checksum.
comment write_bookkeeping_credits Writes the seats' credits (the halfwords at seat1_credits, seat1_bookkeeping_byte, seat2_credits, seat2_bookkeeping_byte) at R12: 701F74 in the bookkeeping or 701FF0 in its backup.
comment add_service_credit A service credit for the seat whose credits are at R10 (seat1_credits or seat2_credits): one credit more, up to 9 (at 9 its coin units are cleared; beyond, nothing); a credit given brings the screen R12 to the title from the attract mode (show_title_on_credit), asks for the seat's service sound (R11) and counts one in R9, which it returns. Not run in the scenarios.
comment insert_coin A coin from the chute of entry R19 (when its bit is in R6): takes the entry's variant (chute_entry_offset: with the chutes shared, the second counts as the first), writes its byte to I/O C00074, owes the chute's coin meter a pulse (coin_meters) and counts the coin in the bookkeeping (701F06-701F0C, bookkeeping_increment16); with fewer than 9 credits: the screen's coin sound, and the chute's coin units (first_chute_multiplier or second_chute_multiplier) go toward a bonus unit (the credit record's third byte; one more unit each bonus_adder, when set) and toward a credit (its second byte, seat1_coin_units: a credit each coins_per_credit, up to 9, show_title_on_credit). Returns R9 plus the credits given.
comment show_title_on_credit A credit on the screen R12 during the attract mode (game modes 1-5, the low byte of its game_mode) brings it to the title: game_mode = 2 (game mode 2, game_mode_title, from its first step).
comment init_coins Sets up the coins: chute_entry_offset 14 (the two chutes shared: the second's coins counted as the first's, on its meter) when not on free play and both chutes give the same units (first_chute_multiplier = second_chute_multiplier), else 0; the seats' credits back from the bookkeeping (701F74-701F7A, bytes); and the low four bits of I/O chip 1's port F, inverted, into second_io_chip_port_f (nothing reads it).
comment bookkeeping_checksum Returns the checksum of the 3E halfwords at R2 (the bookkeeping or its backup): from 1, each halfword XORed in, then a shift right by one, with 8810 XORed in when the bit shifted out was 1.
comment bookkeeping_update_checksum Writes the checksum of the bookkeeping (701F02-701F7D in the shared sound RAM) into 701F00, after a change to it; returns it.
comment bookkeeping_copy Copies 3E halfwords from R2 over R3 (the bookkeeping and its backup at 701F7E, either way, or the defaults), then bookkeeping_update_checksum.
comment bookkeeping_check Checks the bookkeeping at boot: when the block (701F02) matches the checksum (701F00), copies it over the backup (701F7E); else when the backup matches, the backup over the block; else resets both (bookkeeping_reset).
comment bookkeeping_reset Resets the bookkeeping: the defaults at 011458 into the block (701F02), then the block into its backup (701F7E), with the checksum.
comment bookkeeping_increment16 Adds one to the 16-bit bookkeeping counter at R7 (and its backup's, 7C on) unless it is at FFFF, with the checksum: the coins by chute (insert_coin), the games by seat and the races' results (game_mode_play).
comment bookkeeping_add16 Adds R9 to the 16-bit bookkeeping counter at R7 (the credits from coins, 701F02, and the service credits, 701F04: update_coins) and to its backup's (7C on), with the checksum; past FFFF it stays at FFFF (and the backup's is left as it was: the code writes R7 twice).
comment bookkeeping_add32 Adds R9 to the 32-bit bookkeeping counter at R7 and to its backup's (7C on), with the checksum; past FFFFFFFF it stays there (the backup's left as it was): the play time by seat (game_mode_play).
comment bookkeeping_set16 Sets the 16-bit bookkeeping value at R7 and its backup's (7C on) to R9, with the checksum: the longest and shortest play (701F20, 701F22; game_mode_play).
comment bookkeeping_clear_credits Clears the credits kept in the bookkeeping (701F74-701F7B) and in its backup (701FF0-701FF7), with the checksum. Not run in the scenarios.
field screen 00 index u8
field screen 04 palette_ram u32

field screen 14 sprite_origin_x u16
field screen 16 sprite_origin_y u16

field screen B0 sprite_groups u16
field screen B4 next_sprite_place u32 sprite_place
field screen C0 sprite_list_last u32
field screen C4 sprite_list_limit u32
field screen 150 fade_level u8
field screen 151 fade_step s8
field screen 152 fade_end u8
field screen 153 fade_kind u8
field screen 154 fade_channels u8
field screen 160 mixer_values u16[1F]          # the mixer's registers 00-3D as the screen keeps them (init_mixer from mixer_register_defaults, the other screen's layers cleared by number: "mov.h #0, 160[R25](R0)" 0108CD); vblank_update_mixer sends them with 4C-4F (1AC); 3E-4B are the colour offsets
field screen 1B0 mixer_defaults u16[1F]        # the set vblank_update_mixer makes the current one (flags bit 3), registers 00-3D as mixer_values (4C-4F at 1FC)
field screen 700 stage_lengths u16[1F]         # the 31 roads of stage_courses, the sum of their sections' lengths (init_screen "mov.h R8, [R11+]" 013DB6); branch_road_length after them
field screen B00 unpack_state u16
field screen B02 unpack_read_place u8
field screen B03 unpack_write_place u8
field screen B04 unpack_source u32
field screen B08 unpack_destination u32 unpacked_bytes
field screen B0C unpack_copy_source u32 unpacked_bytes
field screen B10 unpack_copy_count u8
field screen B11 unpack_flags u8
field screen B12 unpack_flags_left u8
field screen C00 unpack_queue u32
field screen 1F00 crest_count u8
field screen 1F10 crest_layers u16[10]   # by crest (note_road_crest: at the old count, 3 in the next place)
field screen 1F30 crest_heights u16[10]   # by crest (entry 0 the bottom of the screen; note_road_crest at the new count)
field screen 5000 depth_lists u16
20E020  var  sprite_list_buffers u16
20E080  var  coin_meters u8
20E08C  var  seat1_coin_sounds u8
20E08D  var  seat2_coin_sounds u8

20E0A0  var  seat1_credits u8

20E0A8  var  seat2_credits u8
20E0A9  var  seat2_coin_units u8

# ======== the test mode, first part (naming batch J1) ========
01C241  func  test_mode_start
01C388  func  test_mode_frame
01C392  func  test_mode_menu
01C3E6  func  test_mode_menu_run
01C435  func  test_mode_menu_show_write_count
01C5BA  func  test_mode_menu_toggle_continue
01C5DB  func  test_mode_memory_test
01C928  func  test_mode_memory_test_run
01C93B  func  test_mode_memory_test_draw
01C98E  func  test_mode_memory_test_checksum
01C9BE  func  test_mode_memory_test_words
01CA5D  func  test_mode_memory_test_halfwords
01CAF8  func  test_mode_memory_test_bytes
01CB8F  func  test_mode_memory_test_nibbles
01CC80  func  test_mode_memory_test_blink
01CDE0  func  test_mode_volume_adjustment
01CE15  func  test_mode_volume_adjustment_select

01CEDB  func  test_mode_volume_adjustment_draw
