# ======== the 79 fields named after their offset ========
struct road_segment 20     # a segment of the course's ring (screen + 2000, 128 of them, build_offset & FFF): player_car_build_segment writes it
field road_segment 00 heading u16        # the heading angle's high half (car heading_angle)
field road_segment 02 pitch u16          # the pitch angle's high half (car pitch_angle)
field road_segment 04 x u32              # where it is in the world (car next_x, next_z, next_y)
field road_segment 08 z u32
field road_segment 0C y u32
field road_segment 10 width u16          # the road's width
field road_segment 12 half_width u16
field road_segment 14 width_code u8      # the course run's width code (0 at a fork)
field road_segment 15 road_event u8      # car road_event at the segment
field object 02 flags_upper u16          # bits 16-31 of the object's flags word, which the draw routines read and write with flags and flags_high as one word
field object 14 parent u32 object        # the object that started it (race_background_start_objects; families name it presentation, panel, leader)
field object 18 parameter u32            # what the list or the road's stream that starts it gives it (road_object_stream_start_object: the entry's parameter; families read their record there)
field object 80 list_data u32            # the data the object list that starts it gives it (object_lists: data -> field 80)
field race 0C master_time_difficulty_1 u8    # the time difficulty the master cabinet sends (link_send_records, link_take_master_settings)
field race 5A link_pad_5A u8             # sent with the record (link_send_records); nothing else reads or writes it
field race 5B link_pad_5B u8
local clear_published_car entry R20 car_object object.player_car   # the screen's car's object (player_car_update calls it)

# ======== records reached through pointers (mem_access) ========
struct view 20             # a view of the screen's ring of eight (screen + 600 + 20 * slot): player_car_publish writes this frame's, and the screen draws the one shown_view names
field view 00 x u32                      # where the view is in the world (car view_x, view_y, view_z)
field view 04 y u32
field view 08 z u32
field view 0C pitch u16                  # its angles (car view_pitch, view_yaw)
field view 0E yaw u16
field view 10 window_offset u16          # the window's first segment in the ring (its offset from screen + 2000)
field view 12 horizon_line u16           # the horizon's line on screen (screen horizon_line)
struct projected_segment 10   # a segment of the road as the screen shows it (screen + 3000, one for each segment in view, scale 0 after the last): player_car_project_road writes them
field projected_segment 00 scale u16     # the segment's scale by its depth (0: not shown)
field projected_segment 02 width u16     # the road's width on screen
field projected_segment 04 x u16         # the road's centre on screen
field projected_segment 06 y u16
field projected_segment 08 depth_layer u16   # the depth layer its sprites go in (depth_layers_by_depth)
field projected_segment 0C width_code u8     # the segment's width code and road event (road_segment 14, 15)
field projected_segment 0D road_event u8

# ======== records reached through pointers (batch M1) ========
# The coins: each seat's credits, the coin meters' pulses and the coin chutes' entries (update_coins, pulse_coin_meter,
# add_service_credit, insert_coin)
struct seat_credits 8          # a seat's credits, 8 bytes a seat: seat1_credits (20E0A0) and seat2_credits (20E0A8); add_service_credit takes it in R10, insert_coin from its chute's entry (coin_chute_variant credits)
field seat_credits 00 credits u8          # the credits, up to 9 (seat1_credits, seat2_credits)
field seat_credits 01 coin_units u8       # the coin units toward the next credit: a credit each coins_per_credit (seat1_coin_units, seat2_coin_units)
field seat_credits 02 bonus_units u8      # the coin units toward the next bonus unit: one unit more each bonus_adder (insert_coin)
field seat_credits 04 bookkeeping_byte u8      # seat1_bookkeeping_byte, seat2_bookkeeping_byte (check_game_start takes a credit: "dec.b [R2]" 00089B, "mov.b #0, 2[R2]" 00089D)
struct coin_meter 3            # a coin meter's pulse: coin_meters (20E080), 3 bytes for each chute's meter (seat 1's first and second, then seat 2's); pulse_coin_meter drives it
field coin_meter 00 frames u16            # the vertical blanks its bit stays on (low byte), then off (high byte): 6 and 6 at a pulse; pulse_coin_meter reads and writes the two as one halfword
field coin_meter 00 frames_on u8          # the first of them, counted down first
field coin_meter 01 frames_off u8         # then the second
field coin_meter 02 pulses_owed u8        # the pulses still owed: insert_coin adds one a coin (coin_chute_variant pulses_owed)
struct coin_chute 30 rom       # a coin chute's entry: seat1_first_chute_entry (0114D4) and the three after it, 30 bytes apart; insert_coin reads it in R19
field coin_chute 00 switch_bit u32        # the chute's switch: its bit in switches_released (2-5)
field coin_chute 04 coin_sounds u32 seat_sound_counts       # the address of the seat's pending coin sounds (seat1_coin_sounds, seat2_coin_sounds): one more a coin
struct coin_chute_variant 14 rom   # the rest of a coin chute's entry, twice from its 8: the chute alone (chute_entry_offset 0) and the chutes shared (14); insert_coin goes on with the one chute_entry_offset picks
field coin_chute_variant 00 credits u32 seat_credits   # the seat's credits (seat1_credits, seat2_credits)
field coin_chute_variant 04 pulses_owed u32 coin_meter+2   # its coin meter's pulses owed (20E082, 20E085, 20E088, 20E08B; the second chute's meter counts the first's when shared)
field coin_chute_variant 08 counter u32 bookkeeping_count16   # the address of the bookkeeping's coin count for the chute (701F06-701F0C, bookkeeping_increment16)
field coin_chute_variant 0C coin_units u32 chute_unit_settings    # the address of the chute's coin units: first_chute_multiplier (20E611) or second_chute_multiplier (20E612)
field coin_chute_variant 10 event_code u8   # the byte insert_coin writes to C00074: 11 a first chute's coin, 10 a second's
local insert_coin 011260 R19 chute_entry coin_chute_variant   # "add.w R0, R19": past the entry's first 8 bytes, chute_entry_offset on
# The settings: a copy of them and its checksum, twice (load_settings checks each against its crc16)
struct settings_copy 3C        # the settings from settings_checksum (20E608) and their backup from backup_settings_checksum (20E644): the copy load_settings keeps reads its write count through R10
field settings_copy 00 checksum u16       # the crc16 of the 3A bytes after it (settings_checksum, backup_settings_checksum)
field settings_copy 02 write_count u16    # the EEPROM's saves (eeprom_write_count, backup_eeprom_write_count)
local load_settings 000268,000281 R10 settings settings_copy   # settings_checksum, backup_settings_checksum: the copy whose checksum held
# The seats' inputs
struct seat_inputs 4           # a seat's buttons and analog inputs for the coming frame: seat_1_buttons (20E121) and seat_2_buttons (20E126), which pack_seat_inputs writes, or on ISDN those the link brought (race link_buttons); queue_screen_inputs reads the four at once
field seat_inputs 00 buttons u8           # bit 6 START, 4-5 the shift up and down, 0-3 the others
field seat_inputs 01 wheel u8             # the wheel (analog_inputs inverted), the gas and the brake
field seat_inputs 02 gas u8
field seat_inputs 03 brake u8
local queue_screen_inputs 000C22 R11 seat_record seat_inputs   # on ISDN: the race_copy's link_buttons ("movea.b 2C[20[R10]], R11")
param queue_screen_inputs R12 io_chip
# The cabinet's LCD
local lcd_update 018A5B R11 lcd_scroll text_characters   # screen lcd_scroll: the text it scrolls in, a character every 5 frames
# ======== the board's registers batch M1's files print as numbers (I/O chips at C00000 and C80000, sound RAM, link board) ========
# The two I/O chips (315-5296), one for each screen and seat: C00000 the first's, C80000 the second's, a register each two bytes
C00000  io  IO_1_PORT_A  u8            # port A: the seat's buttons, active low (pack_seat_inputs: the high bits into 0-3, the low two into 4-5, the shift)
C80000  io  IO_2_PORT_A  u8
C00008  io  IO_1_PORT_E  u8         # port E: SERVICE (bit 0), TEST (1), the coin chutes (2-3), START (4), active low (read_switches_and_analog, pack_seat_inputs)
C80008  io  IO_2_PORT_E  u8
C0000A  io  IO_1_PORT_F  u8         # port F: bits 4-5 count as SERVICE and TEST too (read_switches_and_analog); the second chip's bit 7 is the EEPROM's data out (eeprom_read_word)
C8000A  io  IO_2_PORT_F  u8
C0000C  io  IO_1_PORT_G  u8                # port G: the cabinet LCD's data nibble (bits 4-7), its enable strobe (bit 3) and register select, and the DJ/MUSIC and << >> lamps (bits 0-1, port_g_outputs)
C8000C  io  IO_2_PORT_G  u8
C8000E  io  IO_2_PORT_H  u8              # the second chip's port H: the EEPROM's lines (bit 7 data in, 6 clock, 5 chip select; eeprom_set_lines sets bit 4 too)
C0001C  io  IO_1_CNT  u8                  # the chip's CNT outputs: bit 1 the screen on (6 on, 4 off), bit 2 the sound CPU out of reset (start_sound_program)
C8001C  io  IO_2_CNT  u8                  # the second chip's: bit 1 its screen on (86 on, 80 off)
C00060  io  ANALOG_BANK  u8               # the A/D converter's bank of inputs for the next reading (read_switches_and_analog: the other screen's turn)
# The bookkeeping in the sound board's shared RAM (the seats' credits and the block's checksum) and the sound program's start
701F00  io  BOOKKEEPING_CHECKSUM  u16     # the bookkeeping's checksum (701F02-701F7D: bookkeeping_checksum)
701F74  io  BOOKKEEPING_SEAT_1_CREDITS  u16   # seat 1's credits and coin units (write_bookkeeping_credits from seat1_credits; init_coins takes the credits back)
701F76  io  BOOKKEEPING_SEAT_1_BYTE  u16      # the halfword at seat1_bookkeeping_byte (bytes 4-5 of the seat's credit record), kept the same way
701F78  io  BOOKKEEPING_SEAT_2_CREDITS  u16
701F7A  io  BOOKKEEPING_SEAT_2_BYTE  u16
701FF0  io  BOOKKEEPING_BACKUP_SEAT_1_CREDITS  u32   # their copy in the bookkeeping's backup (7C on): seat 1's two halfwords (write_bookkeeping_credits, bookkeeping_clear_credits)
701FF4  io  BOOKKEEPING_BACKUP_SEAT_2_CREDITS  u32
701FFF  io  SOUND_PROGRAM_HOLD  u8        # the sound program's main loop waits while it is not 0 (sound_main_loop); start_sound_program clears it
# The link board (800000 on, a byte in each halfword; 801000 and 801002 its controls)
801000  io  LINK_BOARD_ON  u8            # 1 the link board on, 0 off (network_check, network_check_isdn)
801002  io  LINK_BOARD_FLAGS  u8          # bit 0 this program's flag, bit 7 the board's (network_check_wait_board waits for it)
800000  io  LINK_MACHINE_COUNT  u8        # the machines on the link, which the board writes there (network_check_join, network_check_wait_isdn); first the greeting V70
800002  io  LINK_MACHINE_NUMBER  u8       # this machine's number on the link (network_check_join, network_check_result)
800004  io  LINK_MASTER  u8               # this machine's role: link_master (network_check), read back as MASTER or SLAVE (network_check_result)
800006  io  LINK_RECORDS_SENT  u8         # FF once the program has written its records for the board (network_check, link_send_records)
80000C  io  LINK_BOARD_ERROR  u8          # the board's error byte (link_lost) and self-test result (network_check_four_ics)
80000E  io  LINK_SECOND_BUFFER  u8        # set: the records are at 800720, not 800020 (link_receive_records)
8006E1  io  LINK_ISDN_RECORDS_READY  u8   # ISDN mode: the board has the eight records at 800600 (link_take_master_settings)
80000A  io  LINK_ENTRIES_CLOSED  u8      # the host writes 1 when a race's entries close (race_entries_update) and when its own game starts (game_mode_play, not on ISDN)
# The text layer
31F736  io  MACHINE_ID_TEXT_CELL  u16     # the text layer's cell after THIS MACHINE ID IS (machine_id_text, row 14): network_check_result writes the board's machine number there
# ======== records reached through pointers (batch M2) ========
# A request of the screen's ring of tile pages to unpack (screen + C00 + 8 * place, 64 of them: unpack_queue;
# unpack_read_place and unpack_write_place the places): queue_tile_pages writes it ("mov.w R1, [R11+]" 010FF9,
# "movea.w 300000(R0), [R11+]" 011002), as does the unused queue_unpack_list ("mov.d [R10+], [R11+]" 010FC0),
# and unpack_queued_step takes it ("mov.w [R11+], R10; mov.w [R11+], R11" 010F22); census 208C00-218DFC
struct unpack_request 8
field unpack_request 00 source u32 packed_bytes   # the packed data (unpack's format)
field unpack_request 04 destination u32 unpacked_bytes   # where it unpacks in video RAM (a tile page: 300000 + 400 * page)
# A list of packed blocks for the unused unpack_list and queue_unpack_list: 8-byte entries, the packed data and
# where to unpack it ("mov.w [R10+], R12; mov.w [R10+], R11" 010F66), closed by a negative entry ("test.w [R10]"
# 010F75, 010FCA); nothing passes one (unpack_list carries its pointer in R12 across unpack: untyped)
struct packed_block_entry 8 rom
field packed_block_entry 00 source u32 packed_bytes
field packed_block_entry 04 destination u32
# Packed data for unpack and unpack_queued_step, read a byte at a time ([R10+]: the flag bits, the bytes copied, a
# match's offset and length; a match's halfword "mov.h [R10+], R2" 010E27, 010EEB); census ROM 109E0C-122979
elements packed_bytes u8
# A list of tile pages for unpack_tile_pages and queue_tile_pages: 32-bit entries, the page in the top byte (the
# page at 300000 + 400 * page) and the address of its packed data below, closed by a negative entry ("test.w
# [R10]" 010FA1, 011011; "mov.w [R10+], R0" 010F7A, 010FE9); in the program ROM (boot_tile_pages_5d,
# background_tile_page_lists' lists) or put together in work RAM (race_background page_list); census
# 01AD34-210BC0 (unpack_tile_pages carries its pointer in R12 across unpack: untyped)
elements tile_page_entries u32
# A string for the text layer (print_text_layer; the unused print_text_layer_list and the two erases): the low
# half of the first cell's address ("mov.h [R19+], R18" 01187B, after "movea.b 31F000, R18"), the attribute
# ("mov.b [R19+], R9; shl.h #8, R9" 01187E), then the characters closed by 0 ("mov.b [R19+], R9" 011884,
# 01188E: text_characters); census ROM 00DF84-00E009 (network_check_text and the link's texts). The unused
# print_text_layer_halfwords reads the same two fields, then characters of a halfword each (0118E2, 0118EF)
struct text_layer_record 3 rom
field text_layer_record 00 cell u16             # the first cell's address in video RAM, less 310000
field text_layer_record 02 attribute u8         # the cells' high byte (their palette)
field text_layer_record 03 characters u8[]      # the cells' low bytes, closed by 0
local print_text_layer 01187E R19 text_record text_characters
local print_text_layer_list 011868 R19 text_record text_layer_record   # what print_text_layer hands back: the list's next string
elements text_halfword_characters u16           # print_text_layer_halfwords' characters, ORed with the attribute
local print_text_layer_halfwords 0118DC R19 text_record text_halfword_characters
local network_check_blink_text entry R20 current_object text_layer_record   # the text record its caller points R20 at (not an object): its cell and attribute, then
local network_check_blink_text 00D702,00D708 R20 current_object text_characters   # its characters, closed by 0
# A string draw_text draws in sprites: its place (32 bits, x in the low half and y in the high half: "mov.w
# [R19+], R3; rot.w #10, R3" 011A62), the attribute or-ed into the sprites' ("or.h [R19+], R5" 011A6F), then the
# characters closed by 0 ("movea.b 6[R19], R18" 011A32, draw_text_sprites: text_characters); census
# 00D04C-212C80: the program ROM's (the HUD's, the credits line's, staff_credits_texts) and work RAM's (the
# objects' own: lap_timer_update's at 80, name_entry_update's at 9A and A4)
struct sprite_text_record 6 rom
field sprite_text_record 00 place u32
field sprite_text_record 00 x u16            # place's low half: staff_credits_texts end at a negative one (staff_credits_code_show "test.h [R18]" 0018FA)
field sprite_text_record 04 attribute u16
field sprite_text_record 06 characters u8[]
local draw_text 011A32 R18 text text_characters
# A block of colours for upload_palette (queue_palette's record): its place in the screen's palette RAM (the low
# half of the address, "mov.h [R19+], R18" 011624), its length in bytes ("movz.hw [R19+], R9; shl.h #FB, R9"
# 011627: 32-byte blocks), then the colours ("mov.d [R19+], [R18+]" 01162E-01163A: palette_colours); census ROM
# 03153C-1598C8 (boot_palette_9e00, scene_palettes' records)
struct palette_block 4 rom
field palette_block 00 place u16
field palette_block 02 length u16
field palette_block 04 colours u16[]
local upload_palette 011627 R19 colour_block palette_colours
# A list of colour blocks for upload_palette_list (queue_palette_list's record): its length in bytes ("mov.w
# [R19+], R9; shl.w #FD, R9" 011652: 8 a block), then the blocks (palette_block_entry); census 01AD64-214B28:
# the program ROM's and work RAM's (a palette_fade's own at 28)
struct palette_block_list 4 rom
field palette_block_list 00 length u32
# One of them: the colours' address ("mov.w [R19+], R17" 011659), their place in the palette (the low half of the
# address, "mov.h [R19+], R18" 01165C) and how many blocks of 16 colours ("movz.hw [R19+], R8" 01165F; the copy
# reads the same 32 bytes for each: "mov.d [R17], [R18+]" 011662 to "mov.d 18[R17], [R18+]" 011670)
struct palette_block_entry 8 rom
field palette_block_entry 00 colours u32 palette_colours
field palette_block_entry 04 place u16
field palette_block_entry 06 blocks u16
local upload_palette_list 011652 R19 colour_list palette_block_entry
# The palette fade lists' entries: after the list's length (palette_fade_list), "mov.w [R19+], R18; divu.w #C, R18"
local start_palette_fades 0184D0 R19 fade_list palette_fade_entry
local road_surface_fade_back 0184EC R19 fade_list palette_fade_entry
local race_background_fade_palettes 01850B R19 fade_list palette_fade_entry
# int_vector_40 puts R25 back from the stack ("pop R25" 03F53C) after the two screens' mixers: the screen
# block it interrupted, whose skip_scroll_update and index it reads next
local int_vector_40 03F53C R25 screen screen
# The board's registers these files reach (batch M2). The two I/O chips (315-5296), C00000 for screen 1 and its
# mirror C80000 for screen 2, a register every other byte: ports A-H at 00-0E, CNT at 1C, the ports' directions
# at 1E (runtime/src/io.c)
C00006  io  IO_1_PORT_D  u8       # outputs (update_lamps from port_d_outputs): the coin meters, the CHECK POINT, START and RACE LEADER lamps, the wheel motor
C0000E  io  IO_1_PORT_H  u8       # outputs: the external tile banks, two bits each (int_vector_40 from tile_banks)
C0001E  io  IO_1_DIRECTION  u8    # a bit a port, set for an output: C8, ports D, G and H (init_io_chips)
C80006  io  IO_2_PORT_D  u8       # screen 2's seat: as IO_1_PORT_D (port_d_outputs[1])
C8001E  io  IO_2_DIRECTION  u8    # C8, as chip 1's
# The interrupt controller (D00000): IRQ 0 the VBLANK, 1 its end, 2 the sound CPU, 3 timer 0, 4 timer 1
D00007  io  IRQ_ACKNOWLEDGE  u8        # the pending interrupts: a write keeps only the bits it sets (FE takes IRQ 0, FD 1, FB 2, F7 3, EF 4; 0 all, boot)
D00008  io  IRQ_TIMER_0  u16           # timer 0's count, which starts it (int_vector_40 and int_vector_43 for the LCD; second_task 39)
D0000A  io  IRQ_TIMER_1  u16           # timer 1's count, which starts it (the link's: int_vector_44 1 or 168; second_task 1)
# Video RAM's registers and the sprite chip's
31FF00  io  VIDEO_CONTROL  u16         # the video control register: bit 9 flips the screen (flip_screen), bits 12-15 cleared by reset_tile_layers (init_video 0800, video_control_default); the first of the 72 halfwords int_vector_40 sends from video_registers
31FF05  io  VIDEO_LINE_CONTROL_HIGH  u8   # the high byte of the line control register (31FF04): the line tables' place in video RAM, 400 a step, which vblank_update_scrolls moves every frame (bits 2-3)
500000  io  SPRITE_RENDER_REQUEST  u16  # the sprite chip's first control register: bit 0 draws a sprite frame, bit 1 erases; init_sprite_chip writes 0 (sprite_control_mode) and leaves it to the automatic mode (500006 = 0: every frame)
# The link board (800000): its memory a byte in each halfword, then its registers at 801000
800010  io  LINK_MESSAGE_KIND  u8      # in ISDN mode the kind of the board's message, cleared as it is taken (int_vector_44: 3 a message of race records); the board's greeting Z80 starts here at the network check
# The bookkeeping in the RAM shared with the sound board (the block 701F02-701F7D, its backup 7C on)
701F10  io  BOOKKEEPING_TOTAL_TIME  u32   # TOTAL TIME, in seconds (count_total_time; game_mode_play's game_start_time); its backup at 701F8C
# ======== records reached through pointers (batch M3) ========
struct depth_list 4        # a depth layer's list of sprite groups (screen depth_lists: screen + 5000 + 4 * layer, 416 layers; the object's depth_layer, the segment's projected_segment depth_layer): draw_object and the road rows link each group in at its head, finish_sprite_list chains the layers into the sprite list and empties them
field depth_list 00 head u16             # a jump to the layer's newest group (8000 | its first entry's number), which jumps on to the one drawn before it; 0: the layer is empty (draw_object 00EB1C "movz.hw [R11], R2", 00EB3A "mov.h R12, [R11]"; finish_sprite_list 00F3D6 "xch.h R2, [R11]")
field depth_list 02 tail u16             # the low half of the address of the oldest group's last entry, where finish_sprite_list writes the jump on to the next layer (draw_object 00EB11 "mov.h R10, 2[R11]" into an empty layer; finish_sprite_list 00F3E4 "mov.h 2[R11], R10")
# A list of the DJ's remarks (dj_remark_lists): the routines car_radio_update runs one at a time through
# routine_list_jump (019286 "mov.w [R10+], R0"), closed by 0 (019289 "test.w [R10]")
elements remark_routines u32
# A list of objects to start (stage_background_object_lists for race_background_start_objects, ending_actor_lists and unfinished_ending_actor_count
# for race_car_start_ending): a 32-bit count, then these pairs, read with [R10+] (017BE7, 017BEC; 0146FA, 0146FF)
struct object_list_pair 8 rom
field object_list_pair 00 handler u32           # -> the new object's handler (4)
field object_list_pair 04 data u32              # -> its list_data (80; goal_actor script)
# A list of handlers to start objects with (start_object_list: the object_handlers_* tables, after their 32-bit count),
# read with "mov.w [R10+], 4[R21]" (011E35)
elements object_handlers u32
# Sprite RAM entries 2 and 3 (the sprite list's start: entry 2 jumps to screen 1's list, which ends with a jump to entry
# 3, which jumps to screen 2's, which ends with a jump to entry 4, the end): finish_sprite_list writes them from its
# sprite_list_switches record (00F4C0, 00F4C8): 8700/8D00 (screen 1's halfway copies) or 8A00/8400 (its lists) into
# entry 2, 9A00/9400 or 9700/9D00 into entry 3
400020  io  SPRITE_SCREEN_1_LIST_JUMP  u16
400030  io  SPRITE_SCREEN_2_LIST_JUMP  u16
# ======== records reached through pointers (batch M4) ========
# A control's range in the settings (volume_ranges, 20E638 on: six controls, two bytes each), which the
# VOLUME ADJUSTMENT sets and the inputs are scaled between: test_mode_volume_adjustment_select 01CE82
# "mov.b R0, [R10+]" the position with bit 0 cleared, 01CE88 with it set; test_mode_volume_adjustment_adjust
# 01CEA7-01CEB6 "cmp.b R0, [R11]" each end widened to take the position; test_mode_input_test_draw 01D17A
# "movz.bh [R13+], R2" the low end, 01D185 the high end (read_switches_and_analog scales between them)
struct volume_range 2
field volume_range 00 low u8
field volume_range 01 high u8
# A coin/credit setting's description (coin_setting_descriptions by coin_setting), read a byte at a time:
# pairs of coins and credits ("movz.bw [R11+], R0" 01DE25, 01DE0D), closed by 0, or by a negative byte and a
# text (FREE PLAY, MANUAL SETTING) closed by 0 (01DE4E "mov.b [R11+], R0")
elements coin_setting_description u8
# An item of a ring of the turntable's scenery (its group's item_list, closed by a negative angle): its angle
# round the turntable (005854 "movz.hw [R10+], R0"), the ring's radius (0057E4 "movz.hw [R10+], R2") and its
# height (00580F "movs.hw [R10+], R2"); turntable_scenery_show skips the radius and height of an item on the
# near side (00584A "add.w #2, R10") and keeps the next item's place across turntable_draw (005830, 005835)
struct turntable_scenery_item 6 rom
field turntable_scenery_item 00 angle u16
field turntable_scenery_item 02 radius u16
field turntable_scenery_item 04 height s16
# A screen's lamps among the game's variables: GAME_VARIABLES + 4 * the screen's index ("movea.w [R24](R0),
# R10" with R0 the index: game_mode_play 003014, 003077, 0031F8, 0033E9), the two screens' lamps being 4 bytes
# apart (checkpoint_lamp 20E0C0 and checkpoint_lamp_2 20E0C4, leader_lamp 20E0C8 and leader_lamp_2 20E0CC):
# each lamp four bytes, its state, blinks, period and timer, which game_mode_play sets 32 bits at a time
# ("mov.w #2, C0[R10]", "mov.w #8, C8[R10]"): each byte has its field, and the four together theirs
# (checkpoint_all, leader_all), which those 32-bit stores write
struct screen_lamps CC
field screen_lamps C0 checkpoint_all u32      # the checkpoint lamp's four bytes, which game_mode_play and entry_invitation_step set at once
field screen_lamps C0 checkpoint u8           # its state (checkpoint_lamp, checkpoint_lamp_2)
field screen_lamps C1 checkpoint_blinks u8    # checkpoint_lamp_blinks: FF blinks for good
field screen_lamps C2 checkpoint_period u8    # checkpoint_lamp_period
field screen_lamps C3 checkpoint_timer u8     # checkpoint_lamp_timer
field screen_lamps C8 leader_all u32          # the race leader lamp's four bytes
field screen_lamps C8 leader u8               # its state (leader_lamp, leader_lamp_2)
field screen_lamps C9 leader_blinks u8
field screen_lamps CA leader_period u8
field screen_lamps CB leader_timer u8
# The board's registers the test mode and the scenes reach by their address. The second screen's I/O chip
# (C80000) and mixer (690000) are the first's (C00000, 610000) for screen 2, register for register
610040 io MIXER_1_OFFSET_0_RED u32    # mixer register 40 (with 42 in the 32 bits): colour offset 0's red and green (step_screen_fade fades through 40, 42, 44); test_mode_start clears 40-4B 32 bits at a time
610044 io MIXER_1_OFFSET_0_BLUE u32   # register 44, colour offset 0's blue, with 46 (the second colour offset's red, as MAME's segas32 mixes them)
610048 io MIXER_1_OFFSET_1_GREEN u32  # registers 48 and 4A, the second colour offset's green and blue
690040 io MIXER_2_OFFSET_0_RED u32    # the same registers of screen 2's mixer
690044 io MIXER_2_OFFSET_0_BLUE u32
690048 io MIXER_2_OFFSET_1_GREEN u32
609C00 io BACKGROUND_COLOUR_1 u16     # the background colour of screen 1's palette: the test mode's blue (733, test_mode_set_up_colours, test_mode_crt_test_grid_run), black for the C.R.T. TEST (test_mode_crt_test)
689C00 io BACKGROUND_COLOUR_2 u16     # ... and of screen 2's
701F06 io BOOKKEEPING_CHUTE_A_COINS u16   # COIN CHUTE A: the coins of seat A's first chute (insert_coin, test_mode_bookkeeping_draw_totals)
701F20 io BOOKKEEPING_LONGEST_PLAY u16    # LONGEST play, in seconds (game_mode_play through bookkeeping_set16)
701F22 io BOOKKEEPING_SHORTEST_PLAY u16   # SHORTEST play
D80000 io RANDOM_NUMBER u8         # the board's random numbers (bar_picture_bar_start: a byte each for speed_x and speed_y)
# ======== records reached through pointers (batch M5) ========
# A car's hit box in the screen's list from screen + 514 (after the count, screen hit_box_count at 510), 18 bytes
# each car of the race: player_car_build_hit_boxes writes the player (012275 "mov.h R1, [R11+]") and
# player_car_write_hit_box the rest (0122AD-0122DF); screen own_hit_box points at the screen's own car's
# (012270 "mov.w R11, 500[R25]"), which road_row_hit_check, player_car_check_car_hits and traffic_hit_check read
struct car_hit_box 12
field car_hit_box 00 player u16            # the car's player (012275)
field car_hit_box 02 record u32 race       # its race_copy record, 20FC00 + 80 * player (0122AD "mov.w R12, [R11+]")
field car_hit_box 06 first_segment u16     # the record's segment (0122B0 "mov.h R1, [R11+]")
field car_hit_box 08 last_segment u16      # 4 segments on (0122B3-0122B6)
field car_hit_box 0A along_start u16       # the record's segment_fraction plus the model's box (car_hit_box_sizes, 0122CA-0122D3)
field car_hit_box 0C along_end u16
field car_hit_box 0E across_start u16      # the record's road_x plus the model's box (0122D6-0122DF)
field car_hit_box 10 across_end u16
# An entry of a model's crash table (car crash_table, right after the model's crash_physics record), 16 bytes an
# entry by the impact (player_car_update 007D8B "and.h #1F0, R5", 007D90 "add.w R5, R19"; 007E91, 007E96)
struct crash_entry 10 rom
field crash_entry 00 animation u16[2]      # the crash animation by the side hit (007F04 "movz.hw 0[R19](R8), R9", plus player_car_view_height) -> object animation
field crash_entry 04 speed_factor u16      # a scenery hit's: the speed lost times it / 256 (007D93 "movz.hw 4[R19], R8") -> speed_change
field crash_entry 06 push u16[2]           # a scenery hit's sideways push by the side hit (007DAB "mov.h 6[R19](R8), CE[R20]"); a car's or the traffic's hit takes push[1] as its push factor (007EC3 "movs.hw 8[R19], R9")
field crash_entry 0A bounce_speed u16      # 007DB3, 007EDD "mov.h A[R19], CC[R20]" -> bounce_speed
field crash_entry 0C hit_sound u8          # the sound at the hit (007EFA "movz.hw C[R19], R0", then request_sound)
field crash_entry 0D crash_sound u8        # the sound 10 frames on (007EED "mov.b D[R19], 134[R23]": car crash_sound)
field crash_entry 0E car_speed_factor u16  # a car's or the traffic's hit: the speeds' difference times it / 256 (007E99 "movz.hw E[R19], R8")
local player_car_update 007E0F R10 other_record race   # the other car's race_copy record (007E0F "movea.b 1C00[R24](R10), R10": 20FC00 + 80 * its player)
# The start of a stage's course (stage_courses; car course): the stage command (-4, then the stage's level and
# number, as player_car_build_segment reads it) and the first run (its length, pitch and heading steps, width code
# and road event); player_car_start_course takes the first road width from it (00FCDF "movz.bw 8[R11], R0").
# Census ROM 03CCA0-03EA5C: all 31 start FFFC, the level 0-5, the stage 0-1E, width code 5 (4 for stage 1C)
struct course_start 0A rom
field course_start 00 stage_command s16
field course_start 02 stage_level u8
field course_start 03 stage u8
field course_start 04 run_length s16
field course_start 06 pitch_step s8
field course_start 07 heading_step s8
field course_start 08 width_code u8
field course_start 09 road_event u8
# A place of a car's part for one of the car's views (race_car_rider_places by model: 16 bytes a view, the driver's,
# the passenger's and the two sprays' places; race_car_draw_part 009C77 "add.w R4, R11" by the view, 009C7A "test.w
# [R11]": none when its 32 bits are 0, 009C7E-009C81 "add.h [R11+], R17/R18")
struct car_part_place 4 rom
field car_part_place 00 x u16
field car_part_place 02 y u16
local race_car_draw_part 009C77 R11 places car_part_place   # the place for the car's view
# A car's small image on the checkpoint board, by player in the board's object from 80 (checkpoint_board_update
# 00CBF5 "mov.w CFC8[PC](R1), 80[R20](R0)" from checkpoint_board_car_sprites; checkpoint_board_draw_row 00CDED
# "movea.w 80[R20](R21), R10")
struct board_car_sprite 4
field board_car_sprite 00 animation u16    # 00CDF3 "movz.hw [R10+], R9"
field board_car_sprite 02 palette u16      # 00CDF6 "mov.h [R10+], 3A[R20]"
# A place in the order the cars passed a checkpoint (screen checkpoint_order, screen + 390, 8 a checkpoint):
# checkpoint_board_update 00CC57 "movea.w 390[R25](R0), 14[R20]" (passing_order), 00CC5F "movz.bw [14[R20]], R21"
struct checkpoint_pass 1
field checkpoint_pass 00 player u8
local player_car_drive 00A44A,00A4B9 R11 segment_record road_segment   # the next segment (00A44A "mov.w R12, R11"), or the race_copy's (00A4B9 "add.h R6, R11", ISDN)
local player_car_place_view 00A68D,00A74B R11 segment_record road_segment   # moved by the view's shift (00A68D "and.h #AFFF, R11"), then the next (00A74B)
C00074  io  EVENT_CODE  u8                 # not mapped by the board: the game writes a code of what happened there, bit 1 the seat: a checkpoint passed 8 + 2 * index + the stage level's parity (lap_timer_update), a game's start 1/3 (game_start_c00074_bytes) and end 0/2 then 8/A, a coin (insert_coin), 13 0 0 1F at start-up (initialise_game)
# ======== records reached through pointers (the merge) ========
# The eight sprite list buffers in sprite RAM (init_sprite_lists writes an end, FFFF, at each): each screen's two
# lists, which the frames take in turn (sprite_list_buffers), and their halfway copies 3000 on (the entries
# sprite_list_switches makes entries 2 and 3 jump to: 8400 and 8A00 screen 1's lists, 8700 and 8D00 their
# copies; 9400, 9A00, 9700 and 9D00 screen 2's)
404000  io  SPRITE_LIST_1_A  u16
407000  io  SPRITE_LIST_1_A_HALFWAY  u16
40A000  io  SPRITE_LIST_1_B  u16
40D000  io  SPRITE_LIST_1_B_HALFWAY  u16
414000  io  SPRITE_LIST_2_A  u16
417000  io  SPRITE_LIST_2_A_HALFWAY  u16
41A000  io  SPRITE_LIST_2_B  u16
41D000  io  SPRITE_LIST_2_B_HALFWAY  u16

param race_car_draw_spray R27 spray race_car_spray                     # the spray's 16 bytes in the race car (+C0 or +D0): its frame at C, which sides at D, its kind at E, its effect at 8
param note_road_crest R27 crest_y
