# ======== memory reached through pointers (the pilot of the round) ========
elements object_words u32 ram                   # an object's 0x100 bytes as words: start_object clears them (011D6A-011DC7, "mov.w R0, [R26+]" and the like)
elements object_slot_flags u8 ram              # the object slots' bytes (object_area slots) from the one a search found: start_object and reserve_object_slot set it (011D54, 011DFB "not.b #0, [R28]")

# ======== memory reached through pointers (batch N1) ========
# race/link.c. The text layer's cells (31F000-31FFFF in the video RAM, 64 a row: a halfword each, the character and its
# colour), a cell at a time: network_check_blink_text 00D710 "xor.h R18, [R19+]" (from 31F000 or the text record's
# cell), network_check_clear_status_line 00D724 "mov.h #0, [R0+]" (row 24), network_check_toggle_memory_text 00D741
# "xor.h R26, [R27+]" (row 24 from column 8)
# Each player's car number, a byte each (car_numbers, 20E030-20E037): link_take_master_settings 00D902 "mov.b [R10],
# [R11+]" from each race_copy record's car_number
elements player_car_numbers u8 ram
# The players of a race in race order, a byte each (race_order to race_order_4_7, bytes 20-27 of a race record):
# race_progress_update 00DB99 and 00DBBD "movz.bw [R13+], R2" (the host's record), its sort 00DC83 "movz.bw [R16+],
# R6", 00DCBD "mov.b R6, -2[R16]", 00DCC7 "mov.b R4, [-R16]" (the screen's own record), 00DD04 "movz.bw [R13+], R2";
# players_not_ready 00DEE3 "movz.bw [R0+], R2"
# The record_serial of each player's race_copy record a screen has seen, a byte a player (screen name_entries_seen,
# 328-32F): course_records_update 00DF0B "cmp.b [R10+], 11[R11]", 00DF12 "mov.b 11[R11], -1[R10]"
elements seen_record_serials u8 ram
# A race record's words, two at a time: link_receive_records 00E854-00E8D0 "mov.d [R10+], [R11+]", 32 a pass (two records; alone
# this machine's two, 20FB00, in ISDN mode the eight at 21E000; into race_copy, 20FC00). What goes into race_copy goes
# through the instruction's temporary (block_00-block_F8), which the types do not follow: those writes stay mem_write32
elements race_record_words u32 ram
# A race record's bytes as the link board's memory holds them (a byte in each halfword): link_copy_records_in 00E67C
# on "movt.hb [R10+], [R11+]", the first 70 of each record and then 11 to the next (into 20FC00, or 21E000 in ISDN mode)
elements race_record_bytes u8 ram
# A course's record in the backup, the sound program's RAM (701F32, ten of them, and the copy at 701FAE): as
# course_record, the car's model, the name's three letters and the time; course_records_update 00DF4D "mov.w [R12],
# [R13+]" copies the model and the name as one word (AS32), 00DF51 "mov.h 4[R12], [R13+]" the time
field backup_course_record 00 model u8
field backup_course_record 01 name u8[3]
# The link board's race records link_receive_records hands to link_copy_records_in (800020, or 800720): a device's
# memory, untyped
local link_receive_records 00E660,00E677 R10 source
# players_not_ready: the host's racer_count (1F), then the players in race order after it
local players_not_ready 00DED9 R0 race
local players_not_ready 00DEE0,00DEE3 R0 race race_order_players
# ======== memory reached through pointers (batch N2) ========
# The video side (video.c, palettes.c, unpack.c, text.c, main.c, interrupts.c): what their pointers reach.
# The screen's block: the two rings of sound requests after their places (queue_sound and the unused
# request_second_sound fill them; send_sound_requests "mov.b [R0](R1), 700700[R10]" 03F8EE, 03F90E sends one a frame)
field screen 802 sound_queue u8[20]                 # the requests of the ring of 32 (sound_queue_read, sound_queue_write)
field screen 842 second_sound_queue u8[20]          # the second ring's (second_sound_queue_read, second_sound_queue_write)
elements stage_length_list u16 ram                  # the screen's stage_lengths, then branch_road_length (14A), which init_screen writes in turn ("mov.h R8, [R11+]" 013DB6, from 700[R25])
elements palette_upload_queue u32 ram               # the screen's queue of palette uploads at 900, as queue_palette_upload reads it ("cmp.w #80, [R18]", "mov.w R19, [R18](R9)" 0115E3): [0] the count (palette_upload_count), [1]-[80] the uploads (a colour block; a list with bit 31); upload_queued_palettes walks it ("mov.w [R10+]" 0115F0, 0115F3)
local upload_queued_palettes 0115F0,0115F3 R10 upload_queue palette_upload_queue   # past the count: the uploads (its 0115EB line keeps palette_upload_count)
elements mixer_colour_offsets u16 ram               # mixer_values' colour offset 0 (registers 40, 42, 44: red, green, blue, at 1A0 of the screen), which step_screen_fade sets to the fade's level for the channels fade_channels has ("mov.h R9, [R19+]" 011725)
local step_screen_fade 01170E R19 mixer_register     # screen + 19F: colour_offset_mode ("mov.b #C0, [R19+]" 011713); the line with 011713,011725 types the colour offsets after it
elements palette_fade_colours u16 ram               # a palette fade's 16 colours as they step (the object's 34-53): palette_fade_prepare "mov.h R0, [R12+]" 0185EF, palette_fade_step "add.h R0, [R11+]" 0186BA; queued through the object's record at 28
elements palette_fade_differences s8 ram            # for each of the fade's 16 colours, how far its red, green and blue still are from the second palette's (the object's 54-83): palette_fade_prepare "mov.b R2, [R13+]" 018601, palette_fade_step "test.b [R10]" 01867B, a step a frame toward 0
elements background_fades u32 ram object            # the palette fades a race_background_fade_watch keeps, a word each from its 80: race_background_fade_palettes "mov.w R21, [R17+]" 01853B; race_background_fade_watch_update "mov.w [R10+], R1" 018556, 0 once one has ended ("mov.w #0, -4[R10]" 018568)
elements unpacked_bytes u8 board                    # what unpack and unpack_queued_step write a byte at a time, a tile page in video RAM (300000 + 400 * page; census 300400-317BFF): a packed byte ("mov.b [R10+], [R11+]" 010DCC, 010E6C) or one a match copies from further back in it ("mov.b [R2+], [R11+]" 010E1D); unpack_list, which takes any address, is unused
elements text_layer_cells u16 board                 # cells of the text layer (31F000-31FEFF, 64 a row): the character in the low byte, the attribute (palette) in the high byte; print_text_layer "mov.h R9, [R18+]" 01188B, print_hex_digits "mov.h R0, [-R11]" 011950, the unused erases and print_text_layer_halfwords
elements text_layer_font_rows u32 board             # the text layer's characters in video RAM (318000 on, 32 bytes each): a row of eight 4-bit pixels a word (load_shadowed_text_font "rvbyt R6, [R18+]" 011854)
elements boot_register_values u8 rom                # the bytes the reset copies a byte at a time ("mov.b [R10+], [R11+]" 000026): 0000A4-0000B3 to E00000 (which the board does not map), 0000B8-0000C3 to the interrupt controller (D00000)
elements cleared_ram_words u32 ram                  # work RAM the reset clears 8 bytes at a time ("mov.d R0, [R10+]" 000554 on: 21E000-21E3FF, 21F000-21FFFF, the game's variables 20E000-20FFFF), and second_task the variables again after the test mode (0007C2)
elements port_output_bits u8 ram                    # a seat's image of an I/O chip's output port (port_d_outputs, port_g_outputs: a byte each screen): update_button_lamps sets the DJ/MUSIC and << >> lamps ("mov.b [R11], R0" 03FA54)
elements vblank_count u8 ram                        # the vertical blanks a screen's task has had (screen_1_vblanks, screen_2_vblanks by screen_vblank_counters): int_vector_40 "inc.b [R0]" 03F618
# A 32-bit value of the bookkeeping (in the RAM the sound program shares, 701F02-701F7D) and its backup 7C on:
# count_total_time adds a second to TOTAL TIME ("cmp.w #FFFFFFFF, [R7]", "inc.w [R7]", "inc.w 7C[R7]" 011301-011310)
# The tile layers' scroll registers in video RAM (MAME's segas32_v.cpp: 31FF10 + 8 * layer the x scroll, its
# fraction in the first halfword and its integer part in the second, then the y scroll), which
# vblank_update_scrolls writes from video_registers for NBG0 or NBG1 and NBG2 or NBG3 (+ 8 * the screen's index)
31FF10 io NBG0_SCROLL_X u32
31FF14 io NBG0_SCROLL_Y u32
31FF20 io NBG2_SCROLL_X u32
31FF24 io NBG2_SCROLL_Y u32
# ======== memory reached through pointers (batch N3) ========
# The sprite lists in sprite RAM (400000-41FFFF), a halfword at a time: entries of 8 words, as MAME's segas32_v.cpp
# reads them: word 0 the command and its attributes (bits 15-14: 00 a sprite, 01 a clip, 10 a jump to entry n,
# 8000 | n, 11 the end; bit 7 flips it up and down, bit 6 across: the flags' bits 6-7), 1 the source's height and
# width, 2 the height on the screen (and bits 16-19 of the graphics' address), 3 the width on the screen (bit 11
# screen 2's sprite layer, sprite_screen_bit; bits 13 and 15 the bank), 4 y, 5 x, 6 the graphics' offset in the
# bank, 7 the palette and priority. Every word goes into the list and its halfway copy 3000 bytes on (element
# 6144: the list the board shows between two frames, init_sprite_lists): put_object_sprites 00F003-00F040 "mov.h R0,
# 3000[R10]", "mov.h R0, [R10+]"; draw_object 00EB1F "mov.w R2, [R10]" and 00EB27 the jump to the layer's last
# group, 32 bits (the jump and a word 0, its offsets unused); road_row_draw writes words 0-1 and 6-7 32 bits at a
# time (00F7D1 "mov.w R0, 3000[R10]", 00F880 "mov.w R7, 3000[R10]"), fork_branch_draw too (013F90-014016)
# A place in the screen's ring of sprite places (screen next_sprite_place: 20C000 and 21C000, 16 bytes a place,
# wrapping at & EFFF): what an object or a road row's item was drawn with last frame, for the halfway copy of its
# sprites (the average of the two frames'). draw_object_smoothed (the object's previous_place) keeps its zoom there
# (00F1C3-00F1CC "mov.h R3, [R27+]", read back from 00F189 "movz.hw [R28+], R7"); road_row_draw and road_row_pair_draw
# keep each item's sprite size, place and graphics (00F7D9 "mov.h R7, 8[R27]", 00F7E3 "mov.h R3, [R27]"), and blank
# the four places after the last (blank_sprite_places, 00F8ED "mov.w R26, [R27+]"); 00F726 "mov.w #F800F800, 4[R27]"
# marks an item off the screen
struct sprite_place 10
field sprite_place 00 size u32            # height and width at once (the blank after a row's last item)
field sprite_place 00 height u16          # draw_object_smoothed: height_scale; the road rows: the sprite's height (word 2 less its bits)
field sprite_place 02 width u16           # width_scale; the sprite's width (word 3 less its bits)
field sprite_place 04 place u32           # y and x at once: F800F800 off the screen
field sprite_place 04 y u16
field sprite_place 06 x u16
field sprite_place 08 rom_offset u16      # the road rows: the graphics' offset (word 6): the halfway copy is drawn only when it is the same sprite as last frame's
field sprite_place 0A mirror_x u16        # road_row_pair_draw: the mirrored item's x (its second sprite)
# The same places a word at a time, as the road rows blank the four after their last item (00F8ED-00F8FF,
# 00FC9E-00FCB0 "mov.w R26, [R27+]; and.h R21, R27": moved within the ring's page, which the record's type does not
# follow)
elements sprite_place_words u32 ram
# A frame of an animation (sprite_animation frames): its piece count, then its pieces (sprite_piece, 8 bytes each),
# a halfword at a time: fork_branch_sprite reads its first piece's attributes and sprite straight from the frame
# (014072 "mov.h 2[R26], R27", 014076 "movz.hw 4[R26], R26")
field sprite_frame 02 piece_words u16[]
local fork_branch_sprite 01406E R26 frames sprite_frame   # "mov.w 4[R26], R26": the first frame of animation 74
param fork_branch_sprite R12 size_and_attributes
param fork_branch_sprite R14 width_bits
# An object list (object_area list, next_list: 200200 and 200300, the two lists of screen 1), its entries after the
# count a halfword each, the low half of an object's address, 0 at the end: run_objects 011CE4 "mov.h [R22+], R20"
elements object_links u16 ram
# A list of objects to start, at its start: its 32-bit count, then the objects (start_object_list: their handlers,
# object_handlers, 011E2C "mov.w [R10+], R8"; race_car_start_ending and race_background_start_objects: the
# (handler, data) pairs, object_list_pair, 0146F1 and 017BD8 "mov.w [R10+], R0", for stage_background_object_lists
# the pairs' length in bytes)
struct object_start_list 4 rom
field object_start_list 00 count u32
local race_car_start_ending 0146E9 R10 actor_list object_start_list   # "mov.w 14A20[PC](R0), R10": ending_actor_lists's list
local race_background_start_objects 017BD2 R10 object_list object_start_list   # "mov.w 181B8[PC](R0), R10": stage_background_object_lists's list
# A screen's two tilemaps' page selects in the copy of the video registers (video_registers, 20E240 on, sent to
# 31FF40): screen 1's NBG0 at 20E240 and NBG2 8 on, screen 2's NBG1 at 20E244 and NBG3 8 on (the other screen's in
# between); a layer's upper two pages in its first word, its lower two in the second, a byte a page (MAME's
# segas32_v.cpp). set_up_background 0179B9 "mov.w [R19+], [R11]", 0179BD "mov.w [R19+], 8[R11]"; race_background_update
# 01756B "mov.w 17AE0[PC](R0), [R11]", 01783F "mov.w [R10+], [20[R20]]"; race_background_lower_animation_cycle 0178F3
# "mov.h ..., 2[20[R20]]", race_background_upper_animation_cycle 01797B "mov.h ..., [20[R20]]"
struct tilemap_pages C
field tilemap_pages 00 pages u32
field tilemap_pages 00 upper_pages u16
field tilemap_pages 02 lower_pages u16
field tilemap_pages 08 second_pages u32
local race_background_update 01755D R11 page_word_address   # "mov.w [R10+], R11": the tilemap's page selects (race_background_layer_setup page_word_address): named here, or the C names it after its type
local set_up_background 0179B6 R11 pages_address   # "mov.w [R10+], R11": background_registers pages_address: named here, or the C names it after its type
# A screen's two tilemaps' tile banks (tile_banks, 20E2B0 for screen 1: NBG0, NBG2 2 on; 20E2B1 for screen 2: NBG1,
# NBG3): set_up_background 0179C5 "mov.b [R19+], [R11]", 0179C9 "mov.b [R19+], 2[R11]"; race_background_update
# 0175EC, 01776B "mov.b ..., [24[R20]]"
struct tilemap_banks 3
field tilemap_banks 00 tile_bank u8
field tilemap_banks 02 second_tile_bank u8
local set_up_background 0179C2 R11 tile_banks_address   # "mov.w [R10+], R11": background_registers tile_banks_address: named here, or the C names it after its type
# A halfword of the video registers' copies (video_registers, sent to 31FF00 on: a tilemap's zoom steps at F50 on, 200
# 1:1, and its zoom centre at F30 on, MAME's segas32_v.cpp) or of the tilemaps' scroll targets (scroll_targets,
# 20E290 on, which the vertical blank moves into the scroll registers F10 on: 20E292 NBG0's x, 20E296 its y), one
# register each: set_up_background 017A06 "mov.h #0, [R11]" and the like; race_background_update 01785E "mov.h R0,
# [18[R20]]", 017897 "mov.h R0, [1C[R20]]"
# Two of the mixer's register copies (screen mixer_values, screen + 160 on: registers 22 and 26 for screen 1's two
# tilemaps, 24 and 28 for screen 2's), read and written 32 bits at a time for the low four bits of the first (the
# layer's priority): set_up_background 017A1A "mov.w [R11], R0" ... 017A25 "mov.w R0, [R11+]"; race_background_update
# 017575 "mov.w [R10], R0", 017580 "mov.w R0, [R10]"
# The road's palettes at a change of background (background_road_palettes): the address of the road's fade list
# (into screen road_palette_fades), then the fades to start (start_palette_fades, a palette_fade_list):
# race_background_update 0177A7 "mov.w [R19+], 208[R25]"
struct road_palette_record 4 rom
field road_palette_record 00 road_fades u32
# The palette fades a sunset_fade started, an object each from its + 18 (sunset_fade fades, count of them):
# sunset_fade_start 017D11 "mov.w R21, [R17+]", sunset_fade_wait 017D2A "mov.w [R10+], R1", 017D3C "mov.w #0, -4[R10]"
elements fade_objects u32 ram object
# A race's racers (the race record's racer_count, then race_order: a player each), a byte at a time: screen
# traffic_racer_list (host_record + 1F) for traffic_spawner_update (013867 "movz.bw [R10+], R9", 01386A "movz.bw
# [R10+], R0"), car_radio_start's walk from the host record (018F88, 018F90)
local car_radio_start 018F88,018F90 R10 race racer_numbers   # past the count: the racers
# The stretches of road the race's players see (screen + F84: visible_stretch_count, then each stretch's top and
# bottom), a halfword at a time: traffic_spawner_update 013886 "mov.h R8, [R11+]", traffic_out_of_view 013D31
# "cmp.h [R10+], R0"
elements stretch_ends u16 ram
# ======== memory reached through pointers (batch N4) ========
# The tile layers' registers as the scenes keep the addresses of them in their objects (route_map, ranking_background,
# title_screen, car_select: copied from route_map_registers, ranking_background_registers, title_screen_layer_setups
# and car_select_tilemap_setups by screen) and write through them: census 20E212-20E2B1
# The tile layer's line tables in video RAM the car select writes (car_select rowscroll_address and rowselect_address:
# 31C000 and 31C400 for screen 1, four pages 800 apart)
elements line_selects u16 board   # the tilemap row a line shows (car_select_run fills 98 on, a line each; census 31C400-31DFBE)
# The text layer (31F000-31FEFF: 64 cells a row, 80 bytes; each cell the attribute (palette) in the high byte and the
# character in the low), which the test mode draws on cell by cell
# test_mode_draw_count's cell (R10) as a record of one cell, so that its write R3 cells on stays an address: as text_layer_cells
# (which its callers' cells would give it) the C indexed TEXT_CELLS(*p_cell)[plural_offset] with plural_offset FFFFFFFE or
# FFFFFFFD (the bookkeeping's TOTAL lines: the S two or three cells back), 4G cells on in C
# test_mode_blink_address holds the cursor's cell (test_mode_set_cursor) on the pages with a cursor, and the prompt's text
# record (test_mode_show_exit_prompt, test_mode_show_continue_prompt) on those with a prompt, which test_mode_blink_prompt reads
# The rest of the test mode's memories: its texts, the memory test, the volume adjustment, the output test, the bookkeeping
# A text record of the test mode (test_mode_draw_texts; the prompts test_mode_blink_prompt blinks: PUSH TEST BUTTON TO
# EXIT at 01F2BC, TO CONTINUE at 01F2DE): lines of a cell (the low half of its address in the text layer) and characters
# closed by 0, a 0 cell after the last. test_mode_blink_prompt reads the first line's cell (01F05E "movz.hw [R10], R11")
# to see whether the prompt is shown; test_mode_draw_texts walks the lines a halfword and bytes at a time (untyped: its
# reads print ROM16 and ROM8, as all its callers pass records of the program ROM)
elements chip_test_results u8 ram     # the memory test's result for a chip (memory_test_results, 20E716 on, 25 chips): 0 not yet, 1 testing, 2 good, 3 bad (test_mode_memory_test_draw 01C958 "movz.bw [R10+], R0")
elements program_halfwords u16        # the program ROM as test_mode_memory_test_checksum sums it, a halfword at a time (000000-03FFF7 and 100000-1FEFFF)
elements analog_positions u8 ram      # a control's position as the test mode reads it from the A/D converter (test_mode_analog_inputs, 20E710 on: the two wheels, gas pedals and brakes): test_mode_read_controls writes them, the volume adjustment reads them
elements output_bits u32 ram          # the game's output bits as the output test toggles them ("not1 R0, [R11]" 01D4DC: port_d_outputs or port_g_outputs, 32 bits from the byte, the bit below 8)
# The bookkeeping in the RAM the sound program shares (701F00 on): the TIME HISTOGRAM's counts and the courses' records
elements stage_game_counts u16 board  # how many games ended at a stage (701F24 on, seven: PRE-STAGE, STAGE 1-5, COMPLETE), which test_mode_bookkeeping_draw_histogram walks twice
struct backup_course_record 6 board   # a course's record as the backup keeps it (701F32 on, ten: AUSTRALIA to KENYA), laid out as course_record: load_course_records copies it into course_records, test_mode_bookkeeping_draw_histogram shows it; a negative model: none
field backup_course_record 04 time u16        # in hundredths
scoped global test_mode_blink_prompt 20E708 test_mode_prompt_text u32 test_mode_text_bytes   # test_mode_blink_address as the prompt pages use it: a text record of the ROM (test_mode_show_exit_prompt 01F2BC, test_mode_show_continue_prompt 01F2DE), not a cell
elements tile_pixel_rows u32 board    # a row of a tile's eight pixels in video RAM, 4 bits each (test_mode_set_up_colours: the four solid tiles from 318020)
# The scenes' own (route map, car select, ranking, name entry, attract)
elements player_place u8 ram          # a player's place in the race (screen places, 0 first): stage_result_line place_address (screen + 320 + player), the final place it draws big
elements racer_numbers u8 ram      # the race's order in its race record from racer_count (race 1F: the count, then the players at 20 on): car_select_entries race_order, which car_select_entries_run walks a byte at a time (005FD9 "movz.bw [R10+], R8", 005FE0 "movz.bw [R10+], R0")
elements object_tile_pages u32 ram    # a list of tile pages an object puts together in itself for queue_tile_pages (ranking_background page_list, 4 entries and FFFFFFFF from B0): the page in the top byte, the packed data's address below (as tile_page_entries)
elements course_record_bytes u8 ram   # a byte of a course's record in course_records (20E300 + 6 * course, the layout of course_record): ranking_record_start reads the name's letters from 20E301 (00271E, 00272E "mov.b [R11+], R4")
elements floor_line_offsets u16 ram   # the car select floor's 120 line offsets, 1/64 pixels (screen + 3800 on): car_select_skew_floor adds a growing step to each and car_select_update_rowscroll copies them, shifted, into the line scroll table
elements name_letters u8 ram          # the name entry's name, a letter a byte (name_entry name, A0 on, three letters and a 0), as name_entry_lcd_update reads it
elements item_place_words u16 ram     # a turntable scenery item's place as it was last drawn (turntable_scenery item_places, from 80: two halfwords an item of its item_list, the low half of its previous_place and its flags bit 5), for draw mode 0A's halfway copy: turntable_scenery_show puts them into the object before turntable_draw and keeps them after (005837-00584D); an item on the near side gets 0 flags (005845 "mov.h #0, 2[R11]")
# The board's registers these files print as numbers
C00050 io ANALOG_INPUTS u8               # the A/D converter's first input, the rest a register every 2 bytes: a write starts the conversion and eight reads shift the reading out, bit 7 first (test_mode_read_controls, read_switches_and_analog: C00050 + 4 * the bank)
701F14 io BOOKKEEPING_GAMES_A u16        # NUMBER OF GAME(S) A, B at 701F16: the games each seat started (game_mode_play by the screen's index, bookkeeping_increment16; test_mode_bookkeeping_draw_totals)
701F24 io BOOKKEEPING_HISTOGRAM u16      # the TIME HISTOGRAM: how many games ended at PRE-STAGE, then STAGE 1-5 and COMPLETE, seven halfwords (stage_game_counts)
701F32 io BOOKKEEPING_COURSE_RECORDS u8  # the courses' records, ten of 6 bytes (backup_course_record): the first one's model
# ======== memory reached through pointers (batch N5) ========
# The road's surface (road_surface_lines, road_surface_halfway_lines, road_surface_fill_lines,
# road_surface_fill_skipped_lines, road_tiles_change_update, road_surface_start, road_surface_update)
elements road_points u16 ram                  # the points buffer (screen + 3800, its two halves by points_buffer): the first segment in view's number, then each point's depth, x and line (road_surface_lines "mov.h R3, [R26+]" 016D88...), closed by FFFF (016E7C)
elements line_scroll_values u16 board         # a tilemap's line scroll table in the video RAM (the road's: line_scroll_table, 31C000 on), a halfword a line, written from the bottom line up ("mov.h R2, [-R11]" 016E09; road_surface_fill_skipped_lines downwards, "mov.h R0, [R11+]" 0170EB)
param road_surface_fill_lines R11 line_scroll_place line_scroll_values   # the line scroll table's place it fills up from ("mov.h R0, [-R11]" 017041, 017088), handed back
elements tile_page_places u32 ram             # road_tiles_change's lists of the four tile pages it copies from (object 18) and to (object 28): their addresses in the video RAM (300000 + 400 * page; "mov.w R0, [R10+]" 0171F8-017220)
elements road_page_table_bytes u8 ram         # the road's page table (road_surface 18), every other byte from table_half: road_tiles_change_update writes its half a byte at a time ("mov.b [R12+], [R11]" then 2 on, 017280-017292)
elements tiles_change_flags u8 ram            # the road's flag a road_tiles_change goes by (road_tiles_change flag: road_surface first_tiles_flag 0A or second_tiles_flag 0B), read until it turns off (017319 "test.b [3C[R20]]")
elements page_select u32 ram             # a tilemap's page word in video_registers (20E248 for screen 1's road, 20E24C screen 2's: road_surface_layer_setup page_register), its four pages a byte each; road_surface_update writes it each frame (016B8A "mov.w -24[R20](R0), [54[R20]]")
elements video_register_words u16 ram         # the copy of a tilemap's scroll register in the game's variables (scroll_targets: 20E2A2, 20E2A6 for screen 1's road), which the VBLANK sends (road_surface_start "mov.h #160, [R0]" 016C40, "mov.h #0, [R0]" 016C49)
elements tile_bank_values u8 ram              # a tilemap's tile bank in tile_banks (20E2B2, 20E2B3: road_surface_start "mov.b #0, [R11]" 016C55)
elements mixer_value_pairs u32 ram            # two of a screen's mixer register copies (screen mixer_values: 186 for screen 1's road, 188 screen 2's), read and written as one word (road_surface_start 016C64-016C6E: the road's priority 8 into the low four bits)
elements road_palette_lists u32               # a stage's road palettes (stage_road_palettes): the address of its fade list ("mov.w [R19+], 208[R25]" 016CF4: screen road_palette_fades), then its palette list (queue_palette_list)
# The race cars' riders and sprays (race_car_draw, race_car_draw_spray): 16 bytes each in the race car, from 90
struct race_car_rider 10      # a rider of the car, 16 bytes in the race car: the driver at 90, the passenger at A0 (race_cars_start 0095C8-009643, race_car_draw first_rider and second_rider)
field race_car_rider 00 places u32 car_part_place   # its places by the car's view (race_car_rider_places by model; race_car_draw "mov.w [R27], R11" -> race_car_draw_part)
field race_car_rider 04 animation u16     # its animation (race_car_driver_animations, race_car_passenger_animations), plus the car's view
field race_car_rider 06 palette u16       # its palette
field race_car_rider 08 gesture_mask u8   # the gesture bits it shows (race_car gesture_shown): drawn when none of them is set
field race_car_rider 0A frame u8          # the frame it is at (1 on to frames, 0 while the car stands)
field race_car_rider 0B frames u8         # how many frames it has
struct race_car_spray 10      # a spray at the car's wheels, 16 bytes in the race car: spray 2 at C0, spray 1 at D0 (race_cars_start 00964A-009680; race_car_draw_spray R27)
field race_car_spray 00 animations u32 spray_animation_numbers   # its animation by its kind (race_car_spray_2_animations, race_car_spray_1_animations)
field race_car_spray 04 places u32 car_part_place   # its places by the car's view (race_car_rider_places + 8 or + C)
field race_car_spray 08 effect u32 wheel_effect_values   # the race record's wheel effect it shows (race wheel_effect_2 for spray 2, wheel_effect_1 for spray 1)
field race_car_spray 0C frame u8          # the frame it is at (0: none shown)
field race_car_spray 0D side u8           # its side's bit (2, 1): shown while race wheel_effect_sides has it
field race_car_spray 0E kind u8           # the effect it shows (dust, smoke, water...)
elements spray_animation_numbers u16          # a spray's animation by its kind (race_car_spray_2_animations 009F2C, race_car_spray_1_animations 009F4C: race_car_draw_spray 009C64 "movz.hw [0[R27]](R0), R12")
elements wheel_effect_values u8 ram           # a race record's wheel effect (race wheel_effect_1, wheel_effect_2), read by the spray that shows it (race_car_draw_spray 009C33 "movz.bw [8[R27]], R2")
elements sprite_words u16 board               # a sprite entry's words in the screen's sprite list (sprite RAM 404000 on), a halfword each (MAME segas32_v.cpp: 0 the command and its flags, 1 the source's size, 2 the height and 3 the width with their bits, 4 y, 5 x, 6 the sprite ROM offset, 7 the palette); the list's copy is 3000 bytes on (1800 halfwords): race_car_put_sprite writes both ("mov.h R0, 3000[R10]; mov.h R0, [R10+]"); race_car_draw links a group in with the entry's first two halfwords at once
# The players in race order (race race_order, 20 on): race_cars_start, checkpoint_board_update and player_car_check_place
# (from host_record + 1F: the count, then the players), player_car_build_hit_boxes, lcd_race_info_update walk them
elements race_order_players u8 ram            # a race record's players in race order, a byte each (race race_order)
local race_cars_start 009597,0095A0 R10 race race_order_players   # "movz.bw [R10+], R0" (009597: past racer_count), "movz.bw [R10+], R1" (0095A0): each racer
local checkpoint_board_update 00CBDF,00CBE2 R10 race race_order_players   # the same walk (00CBDF past racer_count, 00CBE2 each racer)
local player_car_check_place 007462 R10 race race_order_players   # "movz.bw [R10+], R9" (007462): past racer_count, then the players by place ([R10](R9))
local player_car_build_hit_boxes 012238,01225D R10 racer_list race_order_players   # "movea.b 20[308[R25]], R10" then "movz.bw [R10+], R1" (01225D)
# The cars' hit boxes (screen + 514 on, 12 bytes each, and the screen's car's own from 5AE): player_car_write_hit_box
# writes a box from its record on (R11 two bytes in), player_car_build_hit_boxes the player before it
local player_car_build_hit_boxes 01225A,012291 R11 hit_box car_hit_box   # the first box ("mov.w R0, [R11+]" 01225A: past hit_box_count), then the next one player_car_write_hit_box hands back
local player_car_write_hit_box 0122BC R19 box_size item_hit_box   # car_hit_box_sizes by the model ("movea.w 126BC[PC](R2), R19"), read a bound at a time ("add.h [R19+], R15" 0122CA-0122DC)
param player_car_write_hit_box R12 racer_copy
# The race's display: the texts objects keep for draw_text_at, and the checkpoint counts
elements object_text_characters u8 ram        # a text an object keeps for draw_text_at (lap_timer, goal, checkpoint_board's rows), a character each: time_to_text writes it from its end back ("mov.b R1, [-R10]"), closed by 0
elements checkpoint_counts u8 ram             # a player's count of the checkpoints passed (screen checkpoints_passed + player: checkpoint_board checkpoint_count)
# The torque curves (screen + 1300, 200 bytes a gear): player_car_build_torque_curves writes them, player_car_drive reads the gear's
elements torque_curve_forces u16 ram          # a gear's drive force by the speed (>> 8), a halfword each (player_car_build_torque_curves "mov.h R4, [R10+]" 00A081; player_car_drive "movea.b 1300[R25](R1), R2" 00A1DE by the gear)
# The bookkeeping in the RAM the sound program shares (701F02-701F7D) and its backup 7C on (701F7E-701FF9): a counter's
# address (R7) reaches both
struct bookkeeping_count16 7E board   # a 16-bit count of the bookkeeping (the coins by chute 701F06-701F0C, the games by seat, the longest and shortest play 701F20, 701F22) and its backup's
field bookkeeping_count16 00 value u16
field bookkeeping_count16 7C backup u16
struct bookkeeping_count32 80 board   # a 32-bit count of the bookkeeping (the play time by seat, 701F18 + 4 * index) and its backup's
field bookkeeping_count32 00 value u32
field bookkeeping_count32 7C backup u32
struct bookkeeping_count8 7D board    # an 8-bit one (bookkeeping_increment8, bookkeeping_add8, bookkeeping_set8: none in use) and its backup's
field bookkeeping_count8 00 value u8
field bookkeeping_count8 7C backup u8
elements bookkeeping_halfwords u16 board      # the bookkeeping's 3E halfwords (701F02) or its backup's (701F7E), which bookkeeping_checksum sums ("xor.h [R2+], R0")
elements seat_sound_counts u8 ram             # a seat's pending coin or service sounds (seat1_coin_sounds-seat2_service_sounds: coin_chute coin_sounds, add_service_credit R11)
elements chute_unit_settings u8 ram           # a chute's coin units in the settings (first_chute_multiplier, second_chute_multiplier: coin_chute_variant coin_units)
# The analog inputs (read_switches_and_analog, link_send_seat_inputs)
elements analog_values u8 ram                 # the analog inputs, a byte each: raw (analog_raw) and scaled (analog_inputs), from 4 * the screen's turn (4 on screen 1's, 2 on screen 2's)
elements analog_calibration_bytes u8 ram      # the analog inputs' calibration in the settings, a low and a high byte each (20E638 on: analog_calibration_addresses)
field screen 130 input_ring u32[8]            # the screen's ring of eight frames of inputs (queue_screen_inputs at input_write_place, take_screen_inputs at input_read_place)
# The sound requests and the RAM the sound program shares
elements music_fade_delays u8 board           # byte 18 of a screen's first music track in the RAM the sound program shares (music_fade_addresses: 700918, 70F118), the delay of its fade out
# The cabinet LCD's text (screen F10-F1F) and the settings' words
elements lcd_characters u8 ram                # the LCD's text (screen lcd_text, F10 on), a character each: lcd_race_info_update writes the order page's car numbers from F18
elements settings_words u16 ram               # the settings (20E600 on) as the EEPROM holds them, a word each (eeprom_read_settings "mov.h R0, [R11+]" 00034C, eeprom_write_settings "mov.h [R10+], R0" 0003AD)
# The time digits (time_digits_draw): three records of 8 bytes in the object, the units at 80, the tens at 88, the hundreds at 90
struct time_digit 8           # a digit of the time (time_digits units_flip-units_remaining, tens_..., hundreds_...)
field time_digit 00 flip s8               # 0 still; 1 squeezing shut (the width 40 less a frame), FF opening again as the new digit
field time_digit 01 digit u8              # the digit shown
field time_digit 02 width u16             # its width (object width_scale), 40 a frame from 100
field time_digit 04 remaining u32         # the digits from it up when it last changed (0: a leading zero, left out)
# The road's segments (player_car_project_road copies the width code and the road event as one halfword: "mov.h 14[R18], C[R19]" 0101F1)
field road_segment 14 width_code_and_event u16   # the width code (low byte) and the road event (high byte) at once

# ======== memory reached through pointers (batch P1) ========
# The system's files (test_mode.c, sound.c, main.c, interrupts.c, coins.c, lcd.c) and the video's (video.c,
# palettes.c, unpack.c): what their pointers reach, R12 and R20 typed where the code sets them.
# The test mode: the text layer's cells (text_layer_cells) the pages draw through R12, a cell at a time
local test_mode_memory_test_draw 01C94D,01C96B R12 text_place text_layer_cells   # 31F000, then each chip's cell ("mov.h [R11+], R12": memory_test_result_cells), four cells of its result ("mov.h R0, [R12+]" 01C971-01C983)
param test_mode_memory_test_draw R12 text_place
local test_mode_coin_assignments_draw 01DDD5,01DE1E R12 line_cell text_layer_cells   # 31F522, the first line's cell, then the next line's (80 bytes on, 01DE1E); the text after the lines a cell at a time ("mov.h R0, [R12+]" 01DE4B)
param test_mode_coin_assignments_draw R12 line_cell
local test_mode_bookkeeping_draw_histogram 01E956 R12 cell text_layer_cells   # the record's time, a digit at a time: "add.w [R13+], R12" moves to the next digit's cell by time_digit_step cell_step (2, 4 or 6: whole cells)
param test_mode_bookkeeping_draw_histogram R12 cell
param test_mode_bookkeeping_draw_histogram R0 last_character
# The memory test's walk of the sound RAM (test_mode_memory_test_nibbles takes the video RAM and the sprite RAM, two
# memories; test_mode_memory_test_words the work RAM, but its read of a wrong word back, -4[R12] after the last one,
# would be element -1 of 220000, which ram() takes as 200000: both stay untyped)
elements tested_sound_ram_bytes u8 board    # the sound RAM as test_mode_memory_test_bytes writes and reads it, a byte at a time (700000-701FFE: test_mode_memory_test passes 700000 and 1FFF bytes; "mov.b R3, [R12+]" 01CB0C, "cmp.b R3, [R12+]" 01CB48, "xor.b -1[R12], R0" 01CB50)
local test_mode_memory_test_bytes 01CB03,01CB3F R12 tested_address tested_sound_ram_bytes   # "mov.w R10, R12": the RAM's start, for the writes and then for the reads
# The volume adjustment and the input test: the calibration (volume_ranges, 20E638: a low and a high byte each) and
# the controls' positions (test_mode_analog_inputs, 20E710), a byte at a time through R12
local test_mode_volume_adjustment_draw 01CEEF R12 calibration analog_calibration_bytes   # "movea.b 638[R24], R12": each control's range ("movz.bw [R12+], R0", "movz.bw [R12+], R1" 01CEF7-01CF2B)
local test_mode_volume_adjustment_draw 01CF53 R12 analog_input analog_positions   # "movea.b 710[R24], R12": each control's position ("movz.bw [R12+], R0" 01CF5B)
param test_mode_volume_adjustment_draw R12 last_analog_input
local test_mode_input_test_draw 01D165 R12 analog_input analog_positions   # "movea.b 710[R24], R12": each control's position ("movz.bw [R12+], R0" 01D177)
local test_mode_input_test_draw 01D1C4,01D1D2,01D209,01D215 R12 port_a io   # monitor A's I/O chip (C00000), then the port of the switch in its low byte (input_test_items), 8 (port E) and A (port F)
param test_mode_input_test_draw R12 last_port
param test_mode_input_test_draw R14 last_bar_cells
local test_mode_output_test_draw 01D4AC R12 output_bits output_bits   # "add.w R24, R12": the output's byte in the game's variables (port_d_outputs, port_g_outputs), its bit tested ("test1 R0, [R12]" 01D4B2)
# The game assignments: the time difficulty a byte a stage, and the settings' backup copy 3C bytes on
elements time_difficulty_seconds u8 ram     # the seconds each stage's time gets, a byte each (time_difficulty, 20E632 on: PRE-STAGE, STAGE 1-4, FINAL STAGE), which test_mode_game_assignments_draw walks ("movz.bw [R12+], R0" 01DAD2)
local test_mode_game_assignments_draw 01DAC8 R12 difficulty_seconds time_difficulty_seconds   # "movea.b 32[R20], R12": time_difficulty
param test_mode_game_assignments_draw R12 difficulty_seconds
param test_mode_game_assignments_draw R0 character
struct backed_up_setting 3D          # a setting (a byte of the settings in settings_magic's page, 20E600) and its copy in the backup 3C bytes on (from backup_settings_checksum, the copy load_settings keeps): the pages' EXIT compares each setting of test_mode_setting_places with its copy ("cmp.b [R20], 3C[R20]")
field backed_up_setting 00 value u8
field backed_up_setting 3C backup u8
local test_mode_game_assignments_exit 01DBB3 R20 current_object backed_up_setting   # "mov.b [R10+], R20": the setting's low byte (test_mode_setting_places) into R20 (settings_magic's page)
local test_mode_coin_assignments_exit 01E0B5 R20 current_object backed_up_setting
local test_mode_network_assignments_exit 01E5FC R20 current_object backed_up_setting
# The bookkeeping in the RAM the sound program shares, as the first bookkeeping page reads it through R20 (701F00;
# the counts are halfwords and the times words, kept with their backup 7C on: bookkeeping_count16, bookkeeping_count32)
struct bookkeeping_block 7E board    # 701F00: the checksum, then the bookkeeping (701F02-701F7D) that bookkeeping_checksum sums
field bookkeeping_block 00 checksum u16          # BOOKKEEPING_CHECKSUM
field bookkeeping_block 02 coin_credits u16      # COIN CREDIT: the credits the coins gave (update_coins: bookkeeping_add16 at 701F02)
field bookkeeping_block 04 service_credits u16   # SERVICE CREDIT
field bookkeeping_block 06 chute_a_coins u16     # COIN CHUTE A (BOOKKEEPING_CHUTE_A_COINS: the coins of seat A's first chute)
field bookkeeping_block 0A chute_b_coins u16     # COIN CHUTE B (seat B's first chute: coin_chute_variant counter 701F0A)
field bookkeeping_block 10 total_time u32        # TOTAL TIME, seconds (BOOKKEEPING_TOTAL_TIME)
field bookkeeping_block 14 games_a u16           # NUMBER OF GAME(S) A (BOOKKEEPING_GAMES_A)
field bookkeeping_block 16 games_b u16           # NUMBER OF GAME(S) B
field bookkeeping_block 18 play_time_a u32       # GAME PLAY TIME A, seconds (bookkeeping_count32 by the screen's index)
field bookkeeping_block 1C play_time_b u32       # GAME PLAY TIME B
field bookkeeping_block 20 longest_play u16      # LONGEST (BOOKKEEPING_LONGEST_PLAY)
field bookkeeping_block 22 shortest_play u16     # SHORTEST (BOOKKEEPING_SHORTEST_PLAY)
local test_mode_bookkeeping_draw_totals 01E740 R20 current_object bookkeeping_block   # "movea.b 701F00, R20"
# The coins: the port D outputs a coin meter's bit is in (update_coins passes them in R12) and the seats' credits
# in the bookkeeping (write_bookkeeping_credits writes four halfwords from R12)
local update_coins 011190,0111A0,0111B6,0111C6 R12 r12 port_output_bits   # "movea.b 92[R24], R12", "movea.b 93[R24], R12": port_d_outputs for seat 1's meters, port_d_outputs[1] for seat 2's (pulse_coin_meter "or.b R0, [R12]", "and.b R0, [R12]")
local pulse_coin_meter 0111FF,01120C R12 meter_bits bookkeeping_halfwords   # 701F74 (BOOKKEEPING_SEAT_1_CREDITS) and 701FF0 in the backup: write_bookkeeping_credits "mov.h A0[R24], [R12+]" 011213-011225
param pulse_coin_meter R12 meter_bits
# The course: init_screen adds up each road's lengths walking its stream of 6-byte entries (stage_courses), R12 the entry
struct course_entry 6 rom            # an entry of a course's stream (car course): a section's length, or a command when negative (FFFF goes on at the address after it; FFFE a fork and FFFA end the road; FFFC, a stage's command, is 4 bytes long)
field course_entry 00 length u16                 # init_screen "movz.hw [R12], R0" 013D7B, then "add.w #6, R12"
field course_entry 02 jump u32 course_entry      # FFFF's: where the course goes on ("mov.w [-R12], R12" 013DAC)
local init_screen 013D6F,013D7E,013DAC,013DB1 R12 course_entry course_entry   # the road's first entry (stage_courses, "mov.w [R10+], R12"), the next one ("add.w #6, R12"), where an FFFF entry goes on ("mov.w [-R12], R12" 013DAC), and the one after a stage's 4-byte command ("sub.w #2, R12" 013DB1)
# The unused unpack_list carries its list's next entry in R12 across unpack
local unpack_list 010F6C R12 r12 packed_block_entry   # "xch.w R10, R12": the next entry (8 bytes on)
# ======== memory reached through pointers (batch P2) ========
# The network check's link board in R12 (800000: network_check 00D142 "movea.b 800000, R12", network_check_again
# 00D0D6). wait_for_frame calls unpack_queued_step, which sets R12, only while the screen unpacks tiles (screen
# unpack_state), none of which is under way at the check: what the calls that wait for a frame (wait_for_frame,
# network_check_wait_board, network_check_wait_isdn, network_check_four_ics through network_check_halt) hand back in
# R12 is the board still, as the code after them reads it
local network_check 00D1F7 R12 r12 io    # network_check_wait_board (then network_check_greeting reads the greetings at 800000 and 800010)
local network_check 00D20B R12 r12 io    # network_check_wait_board (then the self-test byte 80000C cleared)
local network_check 00D213 R12 r12 io    # network_check_four_ics (then link_master into 800004)
local network_check 00D232 R12 r12 io    # network_check_wait_board (then network_check_five_ics)
local network_check 00D240 R12 r12 io    # wait_for_frame, the wait for the board's flag (then 800002 to 800E20, FF at 800006, the waits on 800008 and 80000C)
local network_check_isdn 00D2E6 R12 r12 io       # network_check_wait_isdn (then network_check_join)
local network_check_five_ics 00D825 R12 r12 io   # network_check_four_ics (then the self-test byte again)
# A course's record in course_records (20E300 + 6 * course: course_records_update 00DF23 "movea.b 300[R24](R8),
# R12", the course record_course * 6), laid out as course_record (the ROM's defaults) and backup_course_record
# (the backup's, 701F32), the name's three letters at 01-03: course_records_update writes the name as a byte and a
# halfword (as the race record's record_name and record_name_rest) and copies the model and the name to the backup
# as one word
struct course_record_entry 6   # a course's record in course_records (20E300 + 6 * course) as course_records_update writes it, the name as a byte and a halfword (course_record_copy: the same record, as load_course_records copies it)
field course_record_entry 00 model u8      # the car's model (00DF33 "mov.b 12[R11], 0[R12]": race record_model)
field course_record_entry 01 name u8       # the name's first letter (00DF39 "mov.b 13[R11], 1[R12]": race record_name)
field course_record_entry 02 name_rest u16 # its other two (00DF3F "mov.h 14[R11], 2[R12]": race record_name_rest)
field course_record_entry 04 time u16      # in hundredths (00DF29 "cmp.h R1, 4[R12]": the race's finish_time at or under it replaces it)
local course_records_update 00DF23 R12 record_entry course_record_entry
param course_records_update R12 record_entry
# The view the car hands on (player_car_publish, clear_published_car: R12 = screen + 600 + 20 * view_ring_slot)
local player_car_publish 00755F R12 view view    # ISDN: the first view ("movea.b 600[R25], R12")
local player_car_publish 007584 R12 view view    # the next of the ring ("movea.b 600[R25](R0), R12")
local clear_published_car 007696 R12 view view
field view 14 segment_remaining u16      # the rest of the car's segment (car segment_remaining): player_car_publish 0075DF "mov.h 3A[R23], 14[R12]", clear_published_car clears it; nothing reads it back
# The race cars' hit boxes: each racer's race_copy record (player_car_build_hit_boxes 01227B "movea.b 1C00[R24](R1),
# R12": 20FC00 + 80 * its player), and the screen's list of boxes from 514 (player_car_check_car_hits 01259C "mov.w
# [R14+], R5" past hit_box_count, then a box of 18 bytes at a time: [R14+] through its fields, the rest skipped)
local player_car_build_hit_boxes 01227B R12 racer_copy race
local player_car_check_car_hits 01259C R14 r14 car_hit_box
local player_car_check_car_hits 012577,01259C,0125A4,0125AC,0125B6,0125B9,0125D6,0125D9,0125DC,0125DF,01260E,01261A,012622 R14 other_hit_box   # the screen's hit boxes (510), the other cars'
# The next segment of the ring ((segment + 20) & AFFF in the page, R12): player_car_drive works out the length of
# the car's segment from the two headings, player_car_world_position (which gets it from player_car_drive or
# player_car_place_on_road) the height and the heading between them, player_car_place_view the view's ("or.h #2000,
# R12; and.h #AFFF, R12" after the view's shift) and, twelve segments on (+180), where the view looks
local player_car_drive 00A41A R12 next_segment road_segment
local player_car_drive 00A4C0 R12 next_segment road_segment   # ISDN: the race_copy's segment's next
param player_car_drive R12 next_segment
local player_car_place_on_road 0077CE R12 next_segment road_segment
local player_car_place_view 00A696 R12 view_segment road_segment   # after the view's shift (the and.h #AFFF at 00A69B goes into the read of its y)
local player_car_place_view 00A724 R12 far_segment road_segment   # twelve segments on
local player_car_place_view 00A754 R12 far_segment road_segment   # twelve segments on from the next
param player_car_place_view R12 view_segment

# The road's line select table in the video RAM (road_surface line_select_table: 31C400 on for screen 1's road, a
# halfword a line, the depth's tilemap row plus the stripe in r14), written from the bottom line up ("mov.h R0,
# [-R12]"); the lines above the road's top are cleared two at a time
elements line_select_pairs u32 board   # two lines' selects at once, as road_surface_lines and road_surface_halfway_lines clear the lines above the road ("test.w [-R12]" 016E51, 016FE2; "mov.w #0, [R12]" 016E4D, 016FDE)
local road_surface_lines 016D1A,016DC2,016E1A,016E43 R12 select_place line_selects   # "movea.w [5C[R20]](R0), R12" (016D1A): the bottom line's place; "sub.w R9, R12" (016DC2) past skipped lines; "mov.h R0, [-R12]" (016E1A) a line's select; "mov.h #0, [-R12]" (016E43) the line above the road

# The courses' records in work RAM (course_records, 20E300 on, ten of 6 bytes), laid out as course_record:
# load_course_records fills them through R12 from the backup (backup_course_record) or the defaults

# [R12+]" 001D9C)
struct course_record_copy 6   # a course's record in course_records (20E300 + 6 * course) as load_course_records copies it, the name as three bytes (course_record_entry: the same record, as course_records_update writes it)

local load_course_records 001DB0 R12 record_copy course_record_copy+1   # "and.b #7, [R12+]": past the model
local load_course_records 001DB4 R12 record_copy course_record_copy+2
local load_course_records 001DB8 R12 record_copy course_record_copy+3
local load_course_records 001DBC R12 record_copy course_record_copy+4
